39pub(super) const PIN_GATE_COMMAND: &str = r#"export PATH="$PWD/ci/rust-script-bin:$PATH"; export HERMIT_RUST_SCRIPT_ARTIFACT_ROOT="$PWD/target/ci/rust-scripts"; export HERMIT_PREBUILT_RUST_SCRIPTS_REQUIRED=1; hermit_run_pin_check() { if command -v with-proxy >/dev/null 2>&1; then with-proxy "$@"; else "$@"; fi; }; hermit_run_pin_check ./ci/run-reverie-pin-check.sh --repo "$PWD""#;
40const LINT_CHECKS_COMMAND: &str = r#"export PATH="$PWD/ci/rust-script-bin:$PATH"; export HERMIT_RUST_SCRIPT_ARTIFACT_ROOT="$PWD/target/ci/rust-scripts"; export HERMIT_PREBUILT_RUST_SCRIPTS_REQUIRED=1; ./ci/lint-checks-node.sh"#;

42/// Literal argv rendering only, not admission authority. The driver retains its
43/// private authenticated slot proof and checks the complete derived graph.
44pub fn admitted_pin_command(tag: &str, floor: Option<&str>) -> Result<Option<String>, String> {
45    let (base, flag) = match tag {
46        "pre.reverie_pin" | "pre.reverie_pin_on_host" => (PIN_GATE_COMMAND, "--base-ref"),
47        "check.lint_checks" => (LINT_CHECKS_COMMAND, "--reverie-pin-base-ref"),
48        other if other.starts_with("pre.reverie_pin") => {
49            return Err(format!("unknown pin node {other}"));
50        }
51        _ => return Ok(None),
52    };
53    match floor {
54        Some(sha) if crate::ledger::admission_hex(sha, 40) => {
55            Ok(Some(format!("{base} {flag} '{sha}'")))
56        }
57        Some(_) => Err("pin command requires one literal full lowercase SHA".into()),
58        None => Ok(Some(base.into())),
59    }
60}

72/// The pinned-root workspace producer is two nodes. The compile node runs the
73/// Cargo half of `build.workspace` and needs no rust-script tool, so it starts
74/// beside `build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root` instead of after it. The prepare
75/// node keeps the producer's tag, so every consumer still names it, and runs
76/// only `./ci/nextest-binaries.rs prepare`, the one part that needs the
77/// prebuilt rust-script tools.

81const PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG: &str = "build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root";
82/// The exact text at which the workspace payload is cut: everything before it
83/// is Cargo, everything after the ` && ` is preparation.
84const WORKSPACE_PREPARATION_BOUNDARY: &str = " && ./ci/nextest-binaries.rs prepare ";
85const PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_DESC: &str = "Build every workspace target, the backend plugins and the one Hermit binary in the validate profile, beside the rust-script build";
86const PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_DESCRIPTION: &str = "The Cargo half of the pinned-root workspace build and the one Cargo compilation of Hermit in the full and portable validations, in the [profile.validate] profile: release optimisation with debug assertions and overflow checks on, so cells run an optimised Hermit with detcore's debug_assert invariants and the determinism-log hash lines --verify compares. It first cleans and builds detcore-dbt alone, so reverie-dbt's DynamoRIO cache exists before hermit-install stages the DBT client, DynamoRIO, SaBRe, e9patch and the LiteInst runtime into target/install_pkg, then builds the whole workspace, all targets, with the union of the features every prepared Nextest selection names. Nothing in it runs a rust-script (run-with-reverie-dbt-budget.sh compiles its pin checks with rustc, and no build script calls one), so it does not wait for build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root and the two compile side by side in the same pinned root, writing different target directories; it keeps the rust-script environment, with HERMIT_PREBUILT_RUST_SCRIPTS_REQUIRED=1, so a rust-script added here later fails at once instead of compiling or running unprepared. Its only consumer is build.workspace_in_pinned_root, which lists the executables this build produced. Until 2026-10-06 the two halves were one node that waited for the rust-script tools, about 183 seconds, before this 175-to-182-second Cargo phase began. preferred_inner_jobs=32 is kept from the cold measurement at hermit@846baeca.";
87const PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_DESC: &str = "List every prepared Nextest executable from the one validate-profile build and record each selection's subset";
88const PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_DESCRIPTION: &str = "The preparation half of the pinned-root workspace build. Once build.workspace_compile_in_pinned_root has built every workspace target and build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root has published the prebuilt rust-script tools, `nextest-binaries.rs prepare full` runs in the same pinned root, target directory and Cargo home: one `cargo nextest list` over the unified validate-profile build, the hermit_modes guests and record workloads from that build, and a dev-profile build of the nextest-cpu-wrapper. It hashes every listed executable and publishes all selections in one record, so no selection recompiles anything or relinks target/validate/hermit. Every consumer of the workspace build names this node, so none starts before that record exists. The budget is measured: in eight full validations on 2026-10-06 the unsplit node took 235 to 241 seconds, of which Cargo reported 45 to 46 for detcore-dbt and 130 to 136 for the workspace, leaving 58 to 60 seconds for container start and preparation. The CPU-wrapper build is the largest part, 27 to 28 seconds at 32 workers in those runs; a cold 16-worker build of it outside the root took 28 seconds, 135 CPU-seconds and a 2.4 GB memory peak. The 32-worker preference is the width preparation ran at inside the unsplit node.";

96/// The group of every tool self-test node: `selftest.<name>`.

100/// One repository tool's self-test, run by `test-harness selftest <name>`.
101pub struct ToolSelfTest {
102    /// The `selftest` argument and the job of its `selftest.<name>` node.
103    pub name: &'static str,
104    /// The program, relative to the repository root.
105    pub program: &'static str,
106    pub args: &'static [&'static str],
107    /// `None` runs the self-test in every validation. `Some(triggers)` runs
108    /// it on main and whenever a path changed since the merge base with
109    /// `origin/main` is under one of the triggers (a trigger ending in `/` is
110    /// a directory; any other trigger is one tracked path, a file or a
111    /// submodule); otherwise the node prints a NOT RUN line
112    /// (`self_test_selection`) and passes.
113    pub run_when_changed: Option<&'static [&'static str]>,
114    /// `test-harness selftest` passes the `hermit-manifest-plan` binary that
115    /// its own build wrote beside it through `HERMIT_MANIFEST_PLAN_BIN`, so the
116    /// program runs that binary instead of building one with Cargo inside the
117    /// node's CPU cap. Only the scorecard reads it.
118    pub manifest_plan_helper: bool,
119}
120

123/// path (`self_test_selection`'s tests check it).

141/// The tool self-tests the validation DAG runs as `selftest.<name>` leaf nodes.
142/// They test the repository's own tooling, not a precondition of any product
143/// node, so no node depends on them; each still turns the validation red when
144/// it fails. `assert_tool_self_test_nodes` requires exactly one node per entry
145/// in every profile that runs `gate.manifest`'s local audits.
146pub const TOOL_SELF_TESTS: &[ToolSelfTest] = &[
147    ToolSelfTest {
148        name: "scorecard",
149        program: "ci/compat-envelope/scorecard.rs",
150        args: &["self-test-and-check"],
151        run_when_changed: None,
152        manifest_plan_helper: true,
153    },
154    // The scorecard's commands tier runs its commands against a scratch
155    // clone, ledger and reverie repository, and costs minutes; the regression
156    // tier above runs in seconds. It runs when one of its known inputs
157    // changes, and always on main, which also catches a change elsewhere that
158    // it reads: the scorecard's own directory; the manifest-plan crate it
159    // builds and runs, with that crate's path dependencies (detcore-model/,
160    // and agent-utils, a gitlink that `git diff` lists without a trailing
161    // slash, and .gitmodules, which says where it comes from) and Cargo.lock;
162    // the rust-script prelude it includes and the
163    // prepared rust-script launchers; the script that pins its ledger corpus;
164    // and the E2E manifests its cells and its `system-utils/record-getpid`
165    // command fixture come from.
166    ToolSelfTest {
167        name: "scorecard_commands",
168        program: "ci/compat-envelope/scorecard.rs",
169        args: &["self-test-commands"],
170        run_when_changed: Some(SCORECARD_INPUTS),
171        manifest_plan_helper: false,
172    },
173    ToolSelfTest {
174        name: "pressure_test",
175        program: "ci/compat-envelope/pressure-test.rs",
176        args: &["self-test"],
177        run_when_changed: None,
178        manifest_plan_helper: false,
179    },
180    // The removed shell front door accumulated plan/scheduler/receipt guards
181    // that now belong to the Rust validate driver. Exercise those brackets
182    // without executing the validation DAG.
183    ToolSelfTest {
184        name: "validate_rs",
185        program: "scripts/validate.rs",
186        args: &["--self-test"],
187        run_when_changed: None,
188        manifest_plan_helper: false,
189    },
190    ToolSelfTest {
191        name: "manifest_cli",
192        program: "tests/manifest-cli.rs",
193        args: &["self-test"],
194        run_when_changed: None,
195        manifest_plan_helper: false,
196    },
197    // The DBT budget wrapper gates roughly twenty portable nodes and fails
198    // CLOSED on a pin it is not calibrated for. Nothing else notices: a
199    // truncated node reads like a fast one. This asserts end to end that the
200    // wrapper still REACHES its wrapped command at the recorded pin.
201    ToolSelfTest {
202        name: "dbt_budget",
203        program: "ci/run-with-reverie-dbt-budget-test.sh",
204        args: &[],
205        run_when_changed: None,

207    },

209pub const HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL: &str = "hosted-portable";
210const HOSTED_PRIVILEGED_LABEL: &str = "hosted-privileged";
211const HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX: &str = "_on_host";
212/// Hermit backends the GitHub-hosted portable profile omits.
213///
214/// GitHub-hosted runners expose `/dev/kvm` through nested virtualization but
215/// provide no PMU, so every KVM guest fails when its clock opens the retired
216/// branch counter (`perf_event_open` returns ENOENT). They also lack CPUID
217/// faulting, which the in-guest LiteInst runtime of the `liteinst` and
218/// `in-guest-trap` backends needs before the guest's first instruction
219/// (`detcore-liteinst: initialization failed: CPUID faulting is unavailable`,
220/// the same refusal that keeps the in-guest `test.cli` cases off these runners;
221/// see [`HOSTED_PORTABLE_CPUID_FAULTING_CLI_TESTS`]). The local `full` and
222/// `portable` profiles still select every cell of these backends. The hosted E2E commands,
223/// their result ownership, the hosted expected population, and the hosted
224/// scorecard verification all omit the same backends, so an omitted cell is
225/// never reported as a pass. `.github/workflows/ci-portable.yml`,
226/// `ci/check-shard-coverage.sh`, and `ci/hermetic/run-split-validate.sh`
227/// apply the same filter to `ci/expected-e2e-plan.json`; a test below keeps
228/// them in agreement with this list.
229pub const HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS: &[&str] = &["kvm", "liteinst", "in-guest-trap"];
230
231/// `test.cli` cases the GitHub-hosted portable profile excludes by exact name.
232///
233/// Each starts the in-guest LiteInst runtime, which needs CPUID faulting, a
234/// host capability like the PMU. GitHub-hosted runners have none: in portable
235/// run 37383177918 the first 25 refused with `detcore-liteinst:
236/// initialization failed: CPUID faulting is unavailable: No such device`, and
237/// the runtime-staging case failed earlier only because the prebuilt tree did
238/// not yet ship the runtime it launches. The five `liteinst_in_guest_verify_`
239/// cases came later and run guests under the same runtime. In portable run
240/// <https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/actions/runs/37543769782> four later
241/// cases (exit reaping, unscheduled deaths, userfaultfd self-service and the
242/// `/dev/fuse` refusal) failed with the same error. The FUSE plain-file case
243/// starts the same runtime; that run reported it passed only because the
244/// runner has no FUSE mount, so it returned before launching anything, and on
245/// a runner with one it would fail the same way. The four `in_guest_trap_`
246/// cases run guests under the same runtime with site patching off
247/// (`--backend=in-guest-trap`), and the three self-signal-death cases run
248/// guests under both in-guest backends. The two scalar SIGALRM tests also
249/// require this runtime capability. Only `test.cli_on_host`
250/// excludes them, with one exact-name filterset (`-E 'not (test(=NAME) |
251/// ...)'`) rather than substring `--skip`s; the local `test.cli` keeps running
252/// every one in the pinned root, and a test below holds both sides to this
253/// list.
254pub const HOSTED_PORTABLE_CPUID_FAULTING_CLI_TESTS: &[&str] = &[
255    "liteinst_backend_stats_report_the_guests_own_dispatch_paths",
256    "liteinst_in_guest_programs::in_guest_trap_dispatch_record_reports_no_patched_sites",
257    "liteinst_in_guest_programs::in_guest_trap_refuses_a_guest_library_that_turns_site_patching_back_on",
258    "liteinst_in_guest_programs::in_guest_trap_sets_site_patching_off_and_refuses_a_caller_who_turns_it_on",
259    "liteinst_in_guest_programs::in_guest_trap_sigalrm_scalar_identity_matches_ptrace",
260    "liteinst_in_guest_programs::in_guest_trap_verifies_a_program_with_no_site_patched",
261    "liteinst_in_guest_programs::liteinst_in_guest_a_root_that_aborts_ends_with_its_own_status",

321fn hosted_portable_excludes(cell: &DagManifest) -> bool {
322    cell.backend
323        .as_deref()
324        .is_some_and(|backend| HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS.contains(&backend))
325}
326
327/// Every backend set the hosted-portable exclusion has named, oldest first.
328/// Plans constructed while only KVM was excluded carry `--exclude-backend kvm`
329/// alone and omit only KVM's cells; retained receipts of that shape must keep
330/// reading. The generator writes only the last entry,
331/// [`HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS`].
332const HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUSION_GENERATIONS: &[&[&str]] =
333    &[&["kvm"], HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS];
334
335/// Whether constructed `step` omits `backend`'s cells under the hosted-portable
336/// exclusion. Only a hosted-portable step whose harness selector carries the
337/// exact exclusion flags of one generation omits that generation's backends; a
338/// plan retained before any exclusion has neither the flags nor the omission,
339/// and keeps reading as before. The flags follow the bucket's whole selector,
340/// as the generator writes them: `--prebuilt` for most buckets, and
341/// `--diagnostic-results` for the compat bucket, whose selector ends there.
342pub(crate) fn hosted_step_omits_backend(step: &Step, backend: &str) -> bool {
343    let anchor = step.manifest.as_ref().map_or("--prebuilt", |manifest| {
344        manifest_selector_flags(&manifest.category)
345    });
346    step.labels == [HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL]
347        && HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUSION_GENERATIONS
348            .iter()
349            .any(|excluded| {
350                excluded.contains(&backend)
351                    && carries_exclusion_once(&step.cmd, excluded, anchor, " ")
352            })
353}
354
355/// Whether constructed `step` omits `backend`'s cells: under the
356/// hosted-portable exclusion ([`hosted_step_omits_backend`]), or as the
357/// portable-strict-compat-only compat step that carries exactly the
358/// [`PORTABLE_FOCUSED_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS`] flags after its selector. A plan
359/// retained before that step carried the flags owns every compat cell of its
360/// own expected plan and keeps reading as before.
361pub(crate) fn step_omits_backend(step: &Step, backend: &str) -> bool {
362    hosted_step_omits_backend(step, backend)
363        || (step.tag() == PORTABLE_FOCUSED_COMPAT_TAG
364            && PORTABLE_FOCUSED_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS.contains(&backend)
365            && carries_exclusion_once(
366                &step.cmd,
367                PORTABLE_FOCUSED_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS,
368                manifest_selector_flags("compat"),
369                " ",
370            ))
371}
372
373/// Whether `cmd` carries the current hosted-portable exclusion flags exactly
374/// once, directly after `anchor` and followed by `after`.
375fn carries_hosted_exclusion_once(cmd: &str, anchor: &str, after: &str) -> bool {
376    carries_exclusion_once(cmd, HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS, anchor, after)
377}
378
379/// Whether `cmd` carries the exclusion flags for `excluded` exactly once,
380/// directly after `anchor` and followed by `after`, and no other
381/// `--exclude-backend` word. `test-harness` refuses a repeated
382/// `--exclude-backend`, so a command that repeats the flags would fail when
383/// run; a substring check alone cannot see the repeat. The word count also
384/// keeps one generation's flags from matching a prefix of a later one's.
385fn carries_exclusion_once(cmd: &str, excluded: &[&str], anchor: &str, after: &str) -> bool {
386    let exclusion = exclusion_flags(excluded);
387    let anchored = format!("{anchor}{exclusion}{after}");
388    let anchored_count = if after.is_empty() {
389        usize::from(cmd.ends_with(&anchored))
390    } else {
391        cmd.matches(&anchored).count()
392    };
393    anchored_count == 1
394        && cmd
395            .split_whitespace()
396            .filter(|word| *word == "--exclude-backend")
397            .count()
398            == excluded.len()
399}
400
401fn hosted_portable_exclusion_flags() -> String {
402    exclusion_flags(HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS)
403}
404
405fn exclusion_flags(excluded: &[&str]) -> String {
406    excluded
407        .iter()
408        .map(|backend| format!(" --exclude-backend {backend}"))
409        .collect()
410}

653#[derive(Deserialize)]
654#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
655struct ExpectedPlan {
656    schema: u64,
657    cells: Vec<ExpectedCell>,
658}
659
660#[derive(Deserialize)]
661#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
662struct ExpectedCell {
663    lane: String,
664    category: String,
665    test: String,
666    mode: String,
667    backend: String,
668    #[serde(default)]
669    requires_host_capabilities: Vec<String>,
670    /// Routing and budget fields for consumers outside the DAG (Buck cell
671    /// generation). Optional so plans retained before they existed still parse.
672    #[serde(default)]
673    #[allow(dead_code)]
674    requires: Vec<String>,
675    #[serde(default)]
676    #[allow(dead_code)]
677    timeout_seconds: Option<u64>,
678    #[serde(default)]
679    #[allow(dead_code)]
680    cpu_timeout_seconds: Option<u64>,
681    #[serde(default)]
682    #[allow(dead_code)]
683    classification: Option<String>,
684}
685
686impl From<ExpectedCell> for DagManifest {
687    fn from(cell: ExpectedCell) -> Self {
688        Self {
689            lane: cell.lane,
690            category: cell.category,
691            test: Some(cell.test),
692            mode: Some(cell.mode),
693            backend: Some(cell.backend),
694        }
695    }
696}
697
698struct Scratch(PathBuf);
699
700impl Scratch {
701    fn create() -> Result<Self, String> {
702        let nonce = SystemTime::now()
703            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
704            .map_err(|error| format!("clock is before Unix epoch: {error}"))?
705            .as_nanos();
706        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
707            "hermit-generate-validation-dag-{}-{nonce}",
708            std::process::id()
709        ));
710        fs::create_dir(&path).map_err(|error| {
711            format!(
712                "cannot create scratch directory {}: {error}",
713                path.display()
714            )
715        })?;
716        Ok(Self(path))
717    }
718}
719
720impl Drop for Scratch {
721    fn drop(&mut self) {
722        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
723    }
724}
725
726pub fn repo_root() -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
727    let output = Command::new("git")
728        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
729        .output()
730        .map_err(|error| format!("cannot run git rev-parse: {error}"))?;
731    if !output.status.success() {
732        return Err(format!(
733            "git rev-parse failed: {}",
734            String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).trim()
735        ));
736    }
737    Ok(PathBuf::from(
738        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).trim(),
739    ))
740}
741
742fn generated_plan(root: &Path, scratch: &Path) -> Result<DagConfig, String> {
743    // In-process, so neither generate-validation-dag nor the freshness test
744    // compiles scripts/validate.rs (see scripts/lib/validate_generator.rs).
745    let repetitions = SUPER_REPETITIONS
746        .parse::<i64>()
747        .map_err(|error| format!("invalid SUPER_REPETITIONS {SUPER_REPETITIONS:?}: {error}"))?;
748    crate::validate_generator::committed_generated_partition(
749        root,
750        &scratch.join("run-state"),
751        repetitions,
752    )
753}
754
755/// The required cells each run type's manifest bucket nodes own: the default
756/// run type's from ci/expected-e2e-plan.json (dereferencing to them), and
757/// each run type a node selects by label from the manifests themselves.
758struct Populations {
759    full: Vec<DagManifest>,
760    labelled: BTreeMap<&'static str, Vec<DagManifest>>,
761}
762
763impl std::ops::Deref for Populations {
764    type Target = Vec<DagManifest>;
765
766    fn deref(&self) -> &Self::Target {
767        &self.full
768    }
769}
770
771impl Populations {
772    /// The required cells of the run type `label` selects: its labelled
773    /// cells when a node selects it by label, else the default run type's
774    /// subset for that profile.
775    fn for_label(&self, label: &str) -> Vec<&DagManifest> {
776        match self.labelled.get(label) {
777            Some(cells) => cells.iter().collect(),
778            None => expected_for_label(label, &self.full),
779        }
780    }
781
782    /// The cells a manifest bucket node owns.
783    fn owned_by(&self, step: &Step) -> Vec<&DagManifest> {
784        let Some(selector) = &step.manifest else {
785            return Vec::new();
786        };
787        let cells = match crate::validation_dag_static::manifest_run_type(&step.tag()) {
788            Some(label) => self.labelled.get(label).map_or(&[][..], Vec::as_slice),
789            None => &self.full[..],
790        };
791        cells
792            .iter()
793            .filter(|cell| cell.lane == selector.lane && cell.category == selector.category)
794            .collect()
795    }
796
797    fn all(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &DagManifest> {
798        self.full.iter().chain(self.labelled.values().flatten())
799    }
800}
801
802fn expected_cells(root: &Path) -> Result<Populations, String> {
803    let path = root.join(EXPECTED_PLAN);
804    let text = fs::read_to_string(&path)
805        .map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
806    let full = expected_cells_from_json(&text)
807        .map_err(|error| format!("invalid {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
808    let manifests = crate::runner::ManifestSet::load(root)?;
809    let mut labelled = BTreeMap::new();
810    for label in crate::validation_dag_static::manifest_run_types() {
811        let cells = manifests
812            .select(&crate::runner::Selection {
813                population: Some(crate::runner::Population::Required),
814                labels: vec![label.to_string()],
815                ..Default::default()
816            })?
817            .into_iter()
818            .map(|cell| DagManifest {
819                lane: cell.test.lane.clone(),
820                category: cell.category.clone(),
821                test: Some(cell.id.test.clone()),
822                mode: Some(cell.id.mode.clone()),
823                backend: cell.id.backend.clone(),
824            })
825            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
826        if cells.is_empty() {
827            return Err(format!("run type {label} requires no manifest cell"));
828        }
829        labelled.insert(label, cells);
830    }
831    Ok(Populations { full, labelled })
832}
833
834/// Decode the same source-owned expected population for generation and retained
835/// plan verification. Duplicates are refused before constructing any set.
836pub fn expected_cells_from_json(text: &str) -> Result<Vec<DagManifest>, String> {
837    let plan: ExpectedPlan = serde_json::from_str(text)
838        .map_err(|error| format!("invalid expected E2E plan: {error}"))?;
839    if plan.schema != 1 {
840        return Err("expected E2E plan schema must be 1".into());
841    }
842    let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
843    let mut cells = Vec::new();
844    for cell in plan.cells {
845        if [
846            &cell.lane,
847            &cell.category,
848            &cell.test,
849            &cell.mode,
850            &cell.backend,
851        ]
852        .iter()
853        .any(|field| field.trim().is_empty())
854            || cell
855                .requires_host_capabilities
856                .iter()
857                .any(|field| field.trim().is_empty())
858        {
859            return Err("expected E2E plan contains an empty identity or capability".into());
860        }
861        let cell: DagManifest = cell.into();
862        if !seen.insert(result_identity(&cell)) {
863            return Err("expected E2E plan contains a duplicate cell identity".into());
864        }
865        cells.push(cell);
866    }
867    Ok(cells)
868}
869
870fn normalize_step(step: &mut Step, root: &Path, run_state: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
871    let root = root
872        .to_str()
873        .ok_or_else(|| "repository root is not valid UTF-8".to_string())?;
874    let run_state = run_state
875        .to_str()
876        .ok_or_else(|| "generator scratch path is not valid UTF-8".to_string())?;
877    step.result_manifests = Some(
878        step.result_manifests
879            .take()
880            .unwrap_or_default()
881            .into_iter()
882            .filter(|manifest| matches!(manifest, ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(_)))
883            .collect(),
884    );
885    step.cmd = step
886        .cmd
887        .replace(run_state, "$VALIDATE_RUN_STATE")
888        .replace(root, "$PWD");
889    step.desc = step
890        .desc
891        .replace(run_state, "$VALIDATE_RUN_STATE")
892        .replace(root, "$PWD");
893    step.description = step
894        .description
895        .replace(run_state, "$VALIDATE_RUN_STATE")
896        .replace(root, "$PWD");
897    for value in step.env.values_mut() {
898        *value = value
899            .replace(run_state, "$VALIDATE_RUN_STATE")
900            .replace(root, "$PWD");
901    }
902    if step.timeout <= 0 || step.cpu_timeout <= 0 {
903        return Err(format!(
904            "{} does not carry explicit wall/CPU budgets: wall={} cpu={}",
905            step.tag(),
906            step.timeout,
907            step.cpu_timeout
908        ));
909    }
910    if step.hint.rss_baseline_bytes.is_none() && step.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes.is_none() {
911        return Err(format!(
912            "{} does not carry an explicit memory budget",
913            step.tag()
914        ));
915    }
916    Ok(())
917}
918
919fn shell_quote(value: &str) -> String {
920    if !value.is_empty()
921        && value
922            .bytes()
923            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"@%+=:,./-_".contains(&byte))
924    {
925        return value.to_string();
926    }
927    format!("'{}'", value.replace('\'', r"'\''"))
928}
929
930fn pinned_root_twin_tag(tag: &str) -> String {
931    format!("{tag}{PINNED_ROOT_TWIN_SUFFIX}")
932}
933
934fn is_manifest_run(step: &Step) -> bool {
935    step.manifest.is_some() || (step.group == "quick" && step.job == "e2e_verify")
936}
937
938fn is_hosted_variant(step: &Step) -> bool {
939    step.job.ends_with(HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX)
940}
941
942fn hosted_resource_tuples(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<Vec<(String, String, i64, i64)>, String> {
943    let selected = select_steps_by_labels(cfg, &[HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL.to_string()])?;
944    let mut tuples = Vec::new();
945    for step in &selected.steps {
946        let tag = step.tag();
947        let tag = tag
948            .strip_suffix(HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX)
949            .unwrap_or(&tag)
950            .to_string();
951        for (resource, demand) in &step.hint.resources {
952            let capacity = selected
953                .resource_caps
954                .get(resource)
955                .copied()
956                .ok_or_else(|| {
957                    format!(
958                        "{HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL} step {} demands undeclared resource {resource}",
959                        step.tag()
960                    )
961                })?;
962            tuples.push((tag.clone(), resource.clone(), *demand, capacity));
963        }
964    }
965    tuples.sort();
966    Ok(tuples)
967}
968
969fn is_pinned_root_producer(step: &Step) -> bool {
970    PINNED_ROOT_PRODUCER_STEPS.contains(&step.tag().as_str())
971        || step.job == "manifest_guests"
972        || (step.job == "privileged_tests"
973            && step.cmd.contains("cargo ")
974            && step.cmd.contains("publish-hermit-e2e-artifact.sh"))
975}
976
977/// Manifest bucket nodes that run on the validation host rather than in the
978/// pinned root: the compatibility corpus exercises programs installed on the
979/// host, 31 of which the pinned image does not carry.
980pub const HOST_MANIFEST_RUNS: &[&str] = &[
981    "e2e.manifest_compat",
982    "portablecompat.manifest_compat",
983    "sabrecompat.manifest_compat",
984    "strictcompat.manifest_compat",
985    "rrcompat.manifest_compat",
986];
987
988/// The run type a manifest bucket node selects with `test-harness run
989/// --label`, from the node's static source; `None` for the default run type.
990pub fn manifest_run_type(tag: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
991    crate::validation_dag_static::manifest_run_type(tag)
992}
993
994/// The release Hermit compatprep.hermit_release_in_pinned_root builds, as the
995/// host sees it: the pinned root's /src/target is ignored/hermetic/split/target.
996pub const PORTABLE_FOCUSED_HERMIT_BIN: &str = "ignored/hermetic/split/target/release/hermit";
997
998/// The step that runs the compat bucket for the portable-strict-compat-only
999/// run type against [`PORTABLE_FOCUSED_HERMIT_BIN`].
1000const PORTABLE_FOCUSED_COMPAT_TAG: &str = "portablecompat.manifest_compat";
1001
1002/// Backends whose cells the `PORTABLE_FOCUSED_COMPAT_TAG` step omits with
1003/// `--exclude-backend`. Its release build, `cargo build --release -p hermit
1004/// --features third-party-backends`, does not build the in-guest Detcore
1005/// runtime (libdetcore_liteinst.so, the separate detcore-liteinst package)
1006/// that the `liteinst` and `in-guest-trap` backends load, so each of their
1007/// cells would end backend-unavailable. The run type stays what it was, the
1008/// corpus's ptrace cells under one release build; the full and portable
1009/// profiles run the in-guest cells (e2e.manifest_compat, whose e2e artifact
1010/// ships the runtime). The step's owned results and the run type's expected
1011/// population omit the same backends.
1012pub const PORTABLE_FOCUSED_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS: &[&str] = &["liteinst", "in-guest-trap"];
1013
1014fn portable_focused_excludes(cell: &DagManifest) -> bool {
1015    cell.backend
1016        .as_deref()
1017        .is_some_and(|backend| PORTABLE_FOCUSED_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS.contains(&backend))
1018}
1019
1020fn runs_in_pinned_root(step: &Step) -> bool {
1021    !is_hosted_variant(step)
1022        && !HOST_MANIFEST_RUNS.contains(&step.tag().as_str())
1023        && !is_buck_import_twin(step)
1024        && (is_manifest_run(step) || PINNED_ROOT_EXECUTION_STEPS.contains(&step.tag().as_str()))
1025}
1026
1027/// The host-side name of the one Hermit binary that build.e2e_artifact publishes
1028/// inside the pinned root at target/ci/hermit.
1029pub(crate) const HOST_HERMIT_LINK_TAG: &str = "build.host_hermit_link";
1030
1031/// Route the local host consumers of the Hermit producers to the pinned-root
1032/// build.
1033///
1034/// The strict compatibility rows exercise host-installed programs that the
1035/// pinned image does not carry, so they, and the fixtures they read, still run
1036/// on the host. They reach the one Hermit through build.host_hermit_link, which
1037/// links the host's target/ci/hermit to the pinned root's published binary; that
1038/// binary runs on the host because its loader and libraries are the host nix
1039/// store paths the image was built from. No local host step may depend on the
1040/// workspace build itself.
1041fn route_host_consumers_to_pinned_build(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
1042    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
1043        if is_hosted_variant(step)
1044            || is_pinned_root_producer(step)
1045            || step.job.ends_with(PINNED_ROOT_TWIN_SUFFIX)
1046            || step.cmd.starts_with("./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh ")
1047            || !step
1048                .labels
1049                .iter()
1050                .any(|label| label != HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL)
1051        {
1052            continue;
1053        }
1054        let tag = step.tag();
1055        for dependency in &mut step.deps {
1056            if dependency == "build.workspace" {
1057                return Err(format!(
1058                    "local host step {tag} depends on build.workspace; only the pinned root builds Hermit"
1059                ));
1060            }
1061            // The corpus-only lane builds its release Hermit in the pinned
1062            // root; the host producer drops that lane's label.
1063            if dependency == "compatprep.hermit_release"
1064                && step.labels == ["portable-strict-compat-only"]
1065            {
1066                *dependency = pinned_root_twin_tag("compatprep.hermit_release");
1067            }
1068            if dependency == "build.e2e_artifact" {
1069                *dependency = if tag == HOST_HERMIT_LINK_TAG {
1070                    pinned_root_twin_tag("build.e2e_artifact")
1071                } else {
1072                    HOST_HERMIT_LINK_TAG.into()
1073                };
1074            }
1075        }
1076        step.deps.sort();
1077        step.deps.dedup();
1078    }
1079    Ok(())
1080}
1081
1082/// A pinned-root producer that compiles or publishes Hermit itself, as opposed
1083/// to the host tooling producers (rust scripts, the manifest plan, the manifest
1084/// guests) whose host and pinned-root copies test-harness validate requires.
1085fn builds_hermit(step: &Step) -> bool {
1086    matches!(
1087        step.tag().as_str(),
1088        "build.workspace" | "build.e2e_artifact" | "build.liteinst_allocator_fixtures"
1089    ) || step.job == "privileged_tests"
1090}
1091
1092/// Retire host copies of the Hermit producers that no local host step consumes.
1093///
1094/// Every producer in [`PINNED_ROOT_PRODUCER_STEPS`] gains an `_in_pinned_root`
1095/// twin, and the local profiles consume the twins. A host copy of a Hermit
1096/// producer survives in a local profile only while some local host step still
1097/// depends on it. Until 2026-09-30 the host originals of build.workspace,
1098/// build.runtime_release, build.e2e_artifact and build.liteinst_runtime_release
1099/// kept their local labels and a full validation compiled Hermit on the host as
1100/// well as in the pinned root. An unconsumed copy keeps only its hosted-portable
1101/// label, or is removed when it has none. Host tooling producers are left as
1102/// they are.
1103fn retire_unconsumed_host_producers(cfg: &mut DagConfig) {
1104    loop {
1105        let before = (
1106            cfg.steps.len(),
1107            cfg.steps.iter().map(|s| s.labels.len()).sum::<usize>(),
1108        );
1109        let tags = cfg
1110            .steps
1111            .iter()
1112            .filter(|step| {
1113                is_pinned_root_producer(step)
1114                    && builds_hermit(step)
1115                    && !is_hosted_variant(step)
1116                    && !step.job.ends_with(PINNED_ROOT_TWIN_SUFFIX)
1117            })
1118            .map(Step::tag)
1119            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
1120        for tag in tags {
1121            let local_consumer = cfg.steps.iter().any(|step| {
1122                step.tag() != tag
1123                    && step
1124                        .labels
1125                        .iter()
1126                        .any(|label| label != HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL)
1127                    && step.deps.iter().any(|dependency| dependency == &tag)
1128            });
1129            let any_consumer = cfg
1130                .steps
1131                .iter()
1132                .any(|step| step.deps.iter().any(|dependency| dependency == &tag));
1133            if local_consumer {
1134                continue;
1135            }
1136            let step = cfg
1137                .steps
1138                .iter_mut()
1139                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
1140                .expect("tag was collected from these steps");
1141            step.labels.retain(|label| label == HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL);
1142            if step.labels.is_empty() && !any_consumer {
1143                cfg.steps.retain(|step| step.tag() != tag);
1144            }
1145        }
1146        let after = (
1147            cfg.steps.len(),
1148            cfg.steps.iter().map(|s| s.labels.len()).sum::<usize>(),
1149        );
1150        if after == before {
1151            break;
1152        }
1153    }
1154}
1155
1156// Dagrun appends admitted argv after the complete wrapper command. Re-quote
1157// each resulting argument before appending it to the original shell payload;
1158// preserve literal bytes and the original command's argument placement.
1159pub const PINNED_ROOT_COMMAND_GUARD: &str = r#"/src/ci/hermetic/assert-no-network.sh && /src/ci/hermetic/assert-build-dependencies.sh && hermit_payload=$1 && shift && if [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; then printf -v hermit_extra ' %q' "$@"; hermit_payload+=$hermit_extra; fi && exec bash -c "$hermit_payload""#;
1160pub(super) const LEGACY_PINNED_ROOT_COMMAND_GUARD: &str = r#"/src/ci/hermetic/assert-no-network.sh && /src/ci/hermetic/assert-build-dependencies.sh && exec bash -c "$1""#;
1161
1162fn needs_proc_locks_runtime(tag: &str) -> bool {
1163    matches!(
1164        tag,
1165        "test.hermit_integration" | "e2e.manifest_c_programs" | "quick.e2e_verify"
1166    )
1167}
1168
1169fn pinned_root_command(step: &Step) -> String {
1170    let mut env_names = PINNED_ROOT_FORWARDED_ENV
1171        .iter()
1172        .copied()
1173        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
1174    if is_manifest_run(step)
1175        || step
1176            .structured_test_results_manifest()
1177            .ok()
1178            .flatten()
1179            .is_some()
1180    {
1181        env_names.insert("DAGRUN_TEST_COUNTS_PATH");
1182    }
1183    env_names.extend(step.env.keys().map(String::as_str));
1184    let mut argv = vec![
1185        "./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh".to_string(),
1186        "--src".into(),
1187        ".".into(),
1188        "--out".into(),
1189        "ignored/hermetic/split".into(),
1190        "--src-rw".into(),
1191        "--cargo-home".into(),
1192        "ignored/hermetic/split/cargo".into(),
1193    ];
1194    // Proc-locks snapshots include OFD locks from other PID namespaces. Share
1195    // the native host lease inode, not one file per container or validation.
1196    if needs_proc_locks_runtime(&step.tag()) {
1197        argv.push("--proc-locks-runtime".into());
1198    }
1199    if step.tag() == "test.regular_crates" {
1200        argv.push("--nextest-calibration".into());
1201    }
1202    for name in env_names {
1203        argv.extend(["--env".into(), name.into()]);
1204    }
1205    argv.extend([
1206        "--".into(),
1207        "bash".into(),
1208        "-c".into(),
1209        PINNED_ROOT_COMMAND_GUARD.into(),
1210        "bash".into(),
1211        if step.tag() == "test.regular_crates" {
1212            step.cmd.replace("./ci/run-nextest-counted.sh", "./ci/run-nextest-counted.sh --calibration-launch-proof /run/hermit-nextest-launch.json")
1213        } else { step.cmd.clone() },
1214    ]);
1215    argv.iter()
1216        .map(|argument| shell_quote(argument))
1217        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
1218        .join(" ")
1219}
1220
1221// The authored source already contains wrapped manifest commands. Keep their
1222// command payload unchanged while carrying the current environment policy into
1223// the outer wrapper; otherwise only newly cloned producers see added settings.
1224pub(crate) fn refresh_pinned_root_environment(tag: &str, command: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
1225    let (header, payload) = command
1226        .split_once(" -- bash -c ")
1227        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{tag} has an unrecognized pinned-root command boundary"))?;
1228    let words = header.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>();
1229    let mut refreshed = header.to_owned();
1230    let lease_options = words
1231        .iter()
1232        .filter(|word| **word == "--proc-locks-runtime")
1233        .count();
1234    match (needs_proc_locks_runtime(tag), lease_options) {
1235        (true, 0) => {
1236            let position = header.find(" --env ").unwrap_or(header.len());
1237            refreshed.insert_str(position, " --proc-locks-runtime");
1238        }
1239        (true, 1) | (false, 0) => {}
1240        _ => return Err(format!("{tag} has an unexpected proc-locks runtime option")),
1241    }
1242    for name in PINNED_ROOT_FORWARDED_ENV {
1243        let count = words
1244            .windows(2)
1245            .filter(|pair| pair[0] == "--env" && pair[1] == *name)
1246            .count();
1247        match count {
1248            0 => refreshed.push_str(&format!(" --env {name}")),
1249            1 => {}
1250            _ => {
1251                return Err(format!(
1252                    "{tag} forwards pinned-root environment name {name} more than once"
1253                ));
1254            }
1255        }
1256    }
1257    let legacy = format!("{} bash ", shell_quote(LEGACY_PINNED_ROOT_COMMAND_GUARD));
1258    let current = format!("{} bash ", shell_quote(PINNED_ROOT_COMMAND_GUARD));
1259    let payload = if let Some(command) = payload.strip_prefix(&legacy) {
1260        format!("{current}{command}")
1261    } else if payload.starts_with(&current) {
1262        payload.to_owned()
1263    } else {
1264        return Err(format!("{tag} has an unrecognized pinned-root argv guard"));
1265    };
1266    Ok(format!("{refreshed} -- bash -c {payload}"))
1267}
1268
1269/// The fetch node. Its memory cap, 1.75 GiB, is sized for a COLD Cargo home,
1270/// not the warm shared one ordinary runs reuse (where the fetch takes 16-18 s).
1271/// Measured 2026-10-08 at hermit 8d464d3b, `run-split-validate.sh --fetch-only`
1272/// with no seed and no cap: peaks of 1,133,400,064 and 1,142,509,568 bytes
1273/// (most of it page cache from writing a ~958 MB Cargo home), 20-22 s. The
1274/// earlier 1 GiB cap OOM-killed every cold fetch, which is every
1275/// `--frozen-validate` run (https://github.com/rrnewton/dev-hermit/issues/549).
1276/// 1.5x the higher peak is 1,713,764,352 bytes, rounded up to 7 x 256 MiB.
1277fn pinned_root_fetch() -> Result<Step, String> {
1278    let text = format!(
1279        r#"{{"description":"Pinned-root fetch node","steps":[{{"group":"setup","job":"pinned_root_fetch","desc":"Fetch locked Cargo inputs","description":"Fetch locked Cargo inputs before network-disabled pinned-root commands.","cmd":{},"deps":[],"env":{{"VALIDATE_VERBOSITY":"1"}},"labels":[],"result_manifests":[],"timeout":600,"cpu_timeout":600,"hint":{{"rss_baseline_bytes":1879048192,"hard_mem_max_bytes":1879048192}},"fail_fast_family":"setup.pinned_root_fetch"}}]}}"#,
1280        serde_json::to_string(PINNED_ROOT_FETCH_COMMAND).expect("constant is serializable")
1281    );
1282    let mut step = dag_from_json(&text)
1283        .map_err(|error| format!("internal pinned-root fetch node is invalid: {error}"))?
1284        .steps
1285        .into_iter()
1286        .next()
1287        .ok_or_else(|| "internal pinned-root fetch node disappeared".to_string())?;
1288    step.deps = vec!["pre.reverie_pin".into()];
1289    Ok(step)
1290}
1291
1292/// Add the hosted label to the corpus-derived portable compatibility rows.
1293///
1294/// Authored hosted steps and host-only variants come from the independent typed
1295/// source. The corpus rows are regenerated, so their label is derived here from
1296/// their typed generated partition rather than copied from the output artifact.
1297fn materialize_hosted_portable_selection(cfg: &mut DagConfig) {
1298    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
1299        if generated_partition(step) == Some(GeneratedPartition::PortableCompat)
1300            && !step
1301                .labels
1302                .iter()
1303                .any(|label| label == HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL)
1304        {
1305            step.labels.push(HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL.into());
1306            step.labels.sort();
1307            step.labels.dedup();
1308        }
1309    }
1310}
1311
1312/// Separate the hosted dependency closure without dropping fixture success checks.
1313/// Generated compatibility rows keep their commands and run-state paths; only
1314/// their hosted identity and dependencies change. Runtime consumes these nodes.
1315fn materialize_hosted_test_variants(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
1316    let mut split = cfg
1317        .steps
1318        .iter()
1319        .filter(|step| {
1320            runs_in_pinned_root(step)
1321                && step
1322                    .labels
1323                    .iter()
1324                    .any(|label| label == HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL)
1325        })
1326        .map(Step::tag)
1327        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
1328    // 16 until test.dbt_parity was retired (slice S13 of
1329    // https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3301); 15 until the one-build
1330    // change of 2026-09-30 moved the host consumers of the retired host Hermit
1331    // build into the pinned root: check.dbt_runtime_abi,
1332    // check.backend_parity_suites, lint.clippy, doc.doctests and doc.rustdoc,
1333    // which were already in this split's dependency closure and became its
1334    // roots; 20 until check.backend_parity_suites was retired with
1335    // tests/backend-parity (also slice S13). 20 since test.detcore_time and its hosted twin were enrolled.
1336    // 19 since test.liteinst_strict, whose hosted twin was
1337    // test.liteinst_strict_on_host, was retired with the LiteInst host hybrid
1338    // (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3520).
1339    if split.len() != 19 {
1340        return Err(format!(
1341            "hosted test split has {} roots, expected 19",
1342            split.len()
1343        ));
1344    }
1345    // The hosted test consumers need a producer whose prepared population and
1346    // metadata belong to their committed profile. Keep the shared test cases
1347    // and features, including kvm-native-test-support, while separating the
1348    // hosted artifact identity from the pinned local producer.
1349    // Split the producer before closing over its shared downstream consumers,
1350    // so no hosted path retains a dependency on the local full producer.
1351    split.insert("build.workspace".into());
1352    loop {
1353        let previous = split.len();
1354        for step in &cfg.steps {
1355            if step
1356                .labels
1357                .iter()
1358                .any(|label| label == HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL)
1359                && step
1360                    .labels
1361                    .iter()
1362                    .any(|label| label != HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL)
1363                && step
1364                    .deps
1365                    .iter()
1366                    .any(|dependency| split.contains(dependency))
1367            {
1368                split.insert(step.tag());
1369            }
1370        }
1371        if split.len() == previous {
1372            break;
1373        }
1374    }
1375    // 213 until test.dbt_parity was retired (slice S13 of
1376    // https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3301); 212 until the 189
1377    // compat.<program> nodes that depended on compatprep.fixtures became the
1378    // e2e.manifest_compat bucket (2026-10-01), whose hosted twin is authored.
1379    // 23 until check.backend_parity_suites was retired with
1380    // tests/backend-parity (also slice S13). 23 since test.detcore_time and its hosted twin were enrolled.
1381    // 22 since test.liteinst_strict, one of the split's roots, was retired
1382    // with the LiteInst host hybrid (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3520).
1383    if split.len() != 22 {
1384        return Err(format!(
1385            "hosted test dependency closure has {} nodes, expected 22",
1386            split.len()
1387        ));
1388    }
1389    let mut variants = Vec::new();
1390    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
1391        if split.contains(&step.tag()) {
1392            let mut hosted = step.clone();
1393            hosted.job.push_str(HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX);
1394            hosted.labels = vec![HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL.into()];
1395            hosted.fail_fast_family = Some(hosted.tag());
1396            let owner = hosted.tag();
1397            for result in hosted.result_manifests.iter_mut().flatten() {
1398                if let ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(result) = result {
1399                    result.owner = owner.clone();
1400                }
1401            }
1402            step.labels.retain(|label| label != HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL);
1403            variants.push(hosted);
1404        }
1405    }
1406    cfg.steps.extend(variants);
1407    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
1408        if step.labels == [HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL] {
1409            for dependency in &mut step.deps {
1410                if split.contains(dependency) {
1411                    dependency.push_str(HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX);
1412                }
1413            }
1414        }
1415    }
1416    let hosted_workspace = cfg
1417        .steps
1418        .iter_mut()
1419        .find(|step| step.tag() == "build.workspace_on_host")
1420        .ok_or("hosted workspace producer is absent")?;
1421    let local_prepare = "./ci/nextest-binaries.rs prepare full";
1422    if hosted_workspace.cmd.matches(local_prepare).count() != 1 {
1423        return Err("hosted workspace producer lost the exact local preparation command".into());
1424    }
1425    hosted_workspace.cmd = hosted_workspace.cmd.replace(
1426        local_prepare,
1427        "./ci/nextest-binaries.rs prepare hosted-portable",
1428    );
1429    // The hosted profile has its own selections, so its workspace build
1430    // compiles their union rather than the local full profile's.
1431    let local_prebuild = crate::nextest_binaries::unified_prebuild_command(cfg, "full")?;
1432    let hosted_prebuild =
1433        crate::nextest_binaries::unified_prebuild_command(cfg, HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL)?;
1434    let hosted_workspace = cfg
1435        .steps
1436        .iter_mut()
1437        .find(|step| step.tag() == "build.workspace_on_host")
1438        .ok_or("hosted workspace producer is absent")?;
1439    let local_prebuild = local_prebuild.replace(
1440        "./ci/nextest-binaries.rs prepare full",
1441        "./ci/nextest-binaries.rs prepare hosted-portable",
1442    );
1443    if hosted_workspace.cmd.matches(&local_prebuild).count() != 1 {
1444        return Err("hosted workspace producer lost the local unified workspace build".into());
1445    }
1446    hosted_workspace.cmd = hosted_workspace
1447        .cmd
1448        .replace(&local_prebuild, &hosted_prebuild);
1449    // The hosted runner builds at three Cargo workers on varying hardware, so
1450    // the hosted twin alone carries a longer wall; its CPU bound and memory
1451    // hints stay the local node's. See HOSTED_WORKSPACE_WALL_DESCRIPTION.
1452    if hosted_workspace.timeout != crate::validation_dag_static::WORKSPACE_WALL_SECONDS {
1453        return Err(format!(
1454            "hosted workspace producer inherited a {}s wall, not build.workspace's {}s",
1455            hosted_workspace.timeout,
1456            crate::validation_dag_static::WORKSPACE_WALL_SECONDS
1457        ));
1458    }
1459    hosted_workspace.timeout = crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_WORKSPACE_WALL_SECONDS;
1460    hosted_workspace
1461        .description
1462        .push_str(crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_WORKSPACE_WALL_DESCRIPTION);
1463    // The publisher verifies the binary it publishes against the preparation
1464    // record of the profile its workspace producer prepared.
1465    let hosted_publisher = cfg
1466        .steps
1467        .iter_mut()
1468        .find(|step| step.tag() == "build.e2e_artifact_on_host")
1469        .ok_or("hosted E2E publisher is absent")?;
1470    let local_assert = "./ci/nextest-binaries.rs assert full";
1471    if hosted_publisher.cmd.matches(local_assert).count() != 1 {
1472        return Err("hosted E2E publisher lost the exact prepared-record assertion".into());
1473    }
1474    hosted_publisher.cmd = hosted_publisher.cmd.replace(
1475        local_assert,
1476        "./ci/nextest-binaries.rs assert hosted-portable",
1477    );
1478    Ok(())
1479}
1480
1481/// The authored script unit-test node, which the local lanes run.
1482const SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG: &str = "check.script_unit_tests";
1483/// Its hosted twin, which carries the longer hosted wall.
1484const HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG: &str = "check.script_unit_tests_on_host";
1485
1486/// Give the hosted portable lane its own copy of check.script_unit_tests.
1487///
1488/// The hosted runner runs the node at three CPUs on slower hardware, so the
1489/// twin alone carries a longer wall; its command, dependencies, CPU bound and
1490/// memory hints stay the local node's. See
1491/// HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_DESCRIPTION for the measurement.
1492fn materialize_hosted_script_unit_tests(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
1493    let local = cfg
1494        .steps
1495        .iter_mut()
1496        .find(|step| step.tag() == SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG)
1497        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} is absent"))?;
1498    if !local
1499        .labels
1500        .iter()
1501        .any(|label| label == HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL)
1502    {
1503        return Err(format!(
1504            "{SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} lost its {HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL} label"
1505        ));
1506    }
1507    if local.timeout != crate::validation_dag_static::SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_SECONDS {
1508        return Err(format!(
1509            "{SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} has a {}s wall, not its authored {}s",
1510            local.timeout,
1511            crate::validation_dag_static::SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_SECONDS
1512        ));
1513    }
1514    let mut hosted = local.clone();
1515    local.labels.retain(|label| label != HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL);
1516    hosted.job.push_str(HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX);
1517    hosted.labels = vec![HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL.into()];
1518    hosted.fail_fast_family = Some(hosted.tag());
1519    hosted.timeout = crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_SECONDS;
1520    hosted
1521        .description
1522        .push_str(crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_DESCRIPTION);
1523    cfg.steps.push(hosted);
1524    Ok(())
1525}
1526
1527/// The label of the Buck E2E nodes a `--e2e-runner buck-local|buck-hybrid` full run
1528/// adds to the `full` selection; see [`buck_e2e_selection`].
1529pub const FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL: &str = "full-buck-e2e";
1530/// The host node that runs every E2E cell under Buck/Tpx.
1531pub const BUCK_CELLS_TAG: &str = "e2e.buck_cells";
1532/// The wall bound of e2e.buck_cells, in seconds, which ci/buck-e2e/validate-node
1533/// cannot read from dagrun.
1534const BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV: &str = "HERMIT_VALIDATE_BUCK_STEP_WALL_SECONDS";
1535/// The host node that stages the inputs Buck does not build for
1536/// [`BUCK_CELLS_TAG`]. It needs only the pinned Reverie, so it runs beside the
1537/// rust-script build and the manifest-plan build that the cells wait for.
1538pub const BUCK_STAGE_TAG: &str = "e2e.buck_stage";
1539const BUCK_TWIN_SUFFIX: &str = "_buck";
1540/// The assignment that puts a twin's `target/debug/test-harness run` in import mode,
1541/// naming where e2e.buck_cells leaves the Buck rows.
1542pub const BUCK_IMPORT_ASSIGNMENT: &str =
1543    "E2E_IMPORT_RESULTS=\"$VALIDATE_RUN_STATE/buck-e2e/results\" ";
1544const BUCK_CELLS_COMMAND: &str = r#"export PATH="$PWD/ci/rust-script-bin:$PATH"; export HERMIT_RUST_SCRIPT_ARTIFACT_ROOT="$PWD/target/ci/rust-scripts"; export HERMIT_PREBUILT_RUST_SCRIPTS_REQUIRED=1; ./ci/buck-e2e/validate-node --cells-only"#;
1545/// The stage keeps the rust-script environment although it runs no rust-script
1546/// (its cargo builds, ci/publish-hermit-e2e-artifact.sh, `test-harness build` and
1547/// tests/compat/prepare_real_compat_fixtures.sh call none), so one added later
1548/// fails loudly instead of compiling unprepared. It no longer waits for
1549/// build.rust_scripts, so whether such a call finds the prepared binaries would
1550/// depend on timing: it would fail or run the prepared binary, never run
1551/// something unprepared.
1552const BUCK_STAGE_COMMAND: &str = r#"export PATH="$PWD/ci/rust-script-bin:$PATH"; export HERMIT_RUST_SCRIPT_ARTIFACT_ROOT="$PWD/target/ci/rust-scripts"; export HERMIT_PREBUILT_RUST_SCRIPTS_REQUIRED=1; ./ci/buck-e2e/validate-node --stage-only"#;
1553/// The scorecard that judges the cargo buckets' result files, and its twin.
1554const FULL_SCORECARD_TAG: &str = "full-scorecard.compatibility";
1555/// The `full` nodes that exist only to build inputs for, or to run, the cargo
1556/// E2E buckets. A Buck full run drops them with the buckets they serve.
1557pub const BUCK_REPLACED_PRODUCERS: &[&str] = &[
1558    "build.host_hermit_link",
1559    "build.manifest_guests_in_pinned_root",
1560    "compatprep.fixtures",
1561    "privileged-build.manifest_guests_in_pinned_root",
1562    "setup.manifest_plan_in_pinned_root",
1563];
1564
1565/// The two `full-buck-e2e` nodes that run the Buck runner itself rather than
1566/// replace a cargo node: the stage and the cells.
1567fn is_buck_runner_node(step: &Step) -> bool {
1568    matches!(step.tag().as_str(), BUCK_CELLS_TAG | BUCK_STAGE_TAG)
1569}
1570
1571fn is_buck_import_twin(step: &Step) -> bool {
1572    step.job.ends_with(BUCK_TWIN_SUFFIX)
1573        && step.manifest.is_some()
1574        && step.labels == [FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL]
1575}
1576
1577/// The `full` selection of a Buck E2E run: every `full` node except the cargo
1578/// E2E buckets, their scorecard and [`BUCK_REPLACED_PRODUCERS`], plus the
1579/// `full-buck-e2e` nodes: e2e.buck_stage, e2e.buck_cells, one import twin per
1580/// bucket and the twin scorecard. Each twin owns its bucket's cells and writes its bucket's
1581/// result files, so the selection reports the same cells at the same paths.
1582///
1583/// Refuses a `full` node that still depends on a replaced node, since the
1584/// label closure would silently bring the cargo path back.
1585pub fn buck_e2e_selection(committed: &DagConfig) -> Result<DagConfig, String> {
1586    let replaced = buck_replaced_tags(committed)?;
1587    let mut pruned = committed.clone();
1588    for step in &mut pruned.steps {
1589        if replaced.contains(&step.tag()) {
1590            step.labels.retain(|label| label != "full");
1591        }
1592    }
1593    let selected =
1594        select_steps_by_labels(&pruned, &["full".to_string(), FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL.into()])?;
1595    let kept = selected
1596        .steps
1597        .iter()
1598        .map(Step::tag)
1599        .filter(|tag| replaced.contains(tag))
1600        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
1601    if !kept.is_empty() {
1602        let dependents = selected
1603            .steps
1604            .iter()
1605            .filter(|step| step.deps.iter().any(|dep| kept.contains(dep)))
1606            .map(Step::tag)
1607            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
1608        return Err(format!(
1609            "the Buck E2E selection still needs replaced node(s) {kept:?}, through {dependents:?}"
1610        ));
1611    }
1612    Ok(selected)
1613}
1614
1615/// The cargo nodes a Buck full run replaces: every bucket and scorecard that
1616/// has a `_buck` twin, and the producers only they consume.
1617fn buck_replaced_tags(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<BTreeSet<String>, String> {
1618    let tags = cfg.steps.iter().map(Step::tag).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
1619    let mut replaced = BTreeSet::new();
1620    for step in &cfg.steps {
1621        if step.labels != [FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL] || is_buck_runner_node(step) {
1622            continue;
1623        }
1624        let cargo = step
1625            .tag()
1626            .strip_suffix(BUCK_TWIN_SUFFIX)
1627            .map(str::to_string)
1628            .ok_or_else(|| format!("{} is a Buck node with no cargo counterpart", step.tag()))?;
1629        if !tags.contains(&cargo) {
1630            return Err(format!("{} replaces absent node {cargo}", step.tag()));
1631        }
1632        replaced.insert(cargo);
1633    }
1634    for producer in BUCK_REPLACED_PRODUCERS {
1635        if !tags.contains(*producer) {
1636            return Err(format!("Buck-replaced producer {producer} is absent"));
1637        }
1638        replaced.insert((*producer).to_string());
1639    }
1640    Ok(replaced)
1641}
1642
1643/// Add the `full-buck-e2e` nodes: e2e.buck_stage, which stages the inputs Buck
1644/// does not build; e2e.buck_cells, which runs every cell under Buck/Tpx against
1645/// them; one host import twin per `full` E2E bucket; and a twin of the full
1646/// scorecard that waits for the twins.
1647///
1648/// A twin keeps its bucket's manifest selector and test-harness arguments, so
1649/// it owns the same cells and writes the same result files. It only adds
1650/// `E2E_IMPORT_RESULTS`, so the harness executes nothing and publishes the
1651/// Buck rows instead; a cell with no row becomes an ERROR row.
1652fn materialize_buck_e2e(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
1653    let buckets = cfg
1654        .steps
1655        .iter()
1656        .filter(|step| {
1657            step.manifest.is_some()
1658                && matches!(step.group.as_str(), "e2e" | "privileged-e2e")
1659                && step.labels.iter().any(|label| label == "full")
1660        })
1661        .cloned()
1662        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
1663    if buckets.len() != 16 {
1664        return Err(format!(
1665            "the full profile has {} E2E buckets, expected 16",
1666            buckets.len()
1667        ));
1668    }
1669    let deps = [
1670        BUCK_CELLS_TAG,
1671        "build.rust_scripts",
1672        "gate.manifest",
1673        "pre.reverie_pin",
1674        "setup.manifest_plan",
1675    ];
1676    let mut added = Vec::new();
1677    let mut bucket_tags = BTreeSet::new();
1678    for bucket in &buckets {
1679        let cargo = bucket.tag();
1680        // A twin executes no cell, so it runs on the host (runs_in_pinned_root
1681        // excludes it): keep only the payload of an authored pinned-root wrapper.
1682        let payload = if bucket
1683            .cmd
1684            .starts_with("./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh ")
1685        {
1686            let argv = shell_words::split(&bucket.cmd)
1687                .map_err(|error| format!("{cargo}: invalid pinned-root quoting: {error}"))?;
1688            let boundary = argv
1689                .iter()
1690                .position(|arg| arg == "--")
1691                .ok_or_else(|| format!("{cargo}: missing pinned-root payload boundary"))?;
1692            match &argv[boundary..] {
1693                [_, bash, dash_c, guard, name, payload]
1694                    if bash == "bash"
1695                        && dash_c == "-c"
1696                        && name == "bash"
1697                        && [PINNED_ROOT_COMMAND_GUARD, LEGACY_PINNED_ROOT_COMMAND_GUARD]
1698                            .contains(&guard.as_str()) =>
1699                {
1700                    payload.clone()
1701                }
1702                _ => return Err(format!("{cargo}: unrecognized pinned-root invocation")),
1703            }
1704        } else {
1705            bucket.cmd.clone()
1706        };
1707        let launcher = [
1708            "./ci/run-with-hermit-e2e-artifact.sh --require-install target/debug/test-harness run ",
1709            "./ci/run-with-hermit-e2e-artifact.sh target/debug/test-harness run ",
1710        ]
1711        .into_iter()
1712        .find(|launcher| payload.matches(launcher).count() == 1)
1713        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{cargo} lost its one test-harness run launcher"))?;
1714        let mut twin = bucket.clone();
1715        twin.job.push_str(BUCK_TWIN_SUFFIX);
1716        let tag = twin.tag();
1717        twin.desc = format!("{} (Buck rows)", bucket.desc);
1718        // Lead with the counterpart's own description: the twin owns exactly
1719        // its cells, so what that says about the selection holds here too.
1720        twin.description = format!(
1721            "{} Buck import twin: publishes the cells of {cargo} from the rows e2e.buck_cells wrote: test-harness in import mode (E2E_IMPORT_RESULTS) with {cargo}'s arguments, so it owns the same cells and writes the same result files. It executes no cell. A cell with no row, a row from another commit or an unclean tree, or a PASS without complete evidence is an ERROR, so executed equals plan. Selected only by scripts/validate.rs --e2e-runner buck-local|buck-hybrid, which drops {cargo}.",
1722            bucket.description
1723        );
1724        twin.labels = vec![FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL.into()];
1725        twin.cmd = payload.replace(
1726            launcher,
1727            &format!("{BUCK_IMPORT_ASSIGNMENT}target/debug/test-harness run "),
1728        );
1729        twin.deps = deps.iter().map(|dep| (*dep).to_string()).collect();
1730        twin.env.clear();
1731        // The import's parity post-pass counts its bound from the step start,
1732        // so dagrun must not kill the step first.
1733        twin.timeout = crate::parity::PARITY_STEP_WALL_FLOOR.as_secs() as i64;
1734        twin.cpu_timeout = 600;
1735        twin.hint.resources.clear();
1736        twin.hint.est_duration_s = 1.0;
1737        twin.hint.rss_baseline_bytes = Some(1 << 30);
1738        twin.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes = Some(3 << 30);
1739        twin.hint.classification = dagrun::model::StepClass::Light;
1740        twin.hint.preferred_inner_jobs = None;
1741        twin.fail_fast_family = Some(tag.clone());
1742        for result in twin.result_manifests.iter_mut().flatten() {
1743            if let ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(result) = result {
1744                result.owner = tag.clone();
1745            }
1746        }
1747        bucket_tags.insert(cargo);
1748        added.push(twin);
1749    }
1750
1751    let scorecard = cfg
1752        .steps
1753        .iter()
1754        .find(|step| step.tag() == FULL_SCORECARD_TAG)
1755        .ok_or("the full scorecard is absent")?;
1756    let scorecard_deps = scorecard
1757        .deps
1758        .iter()
1759        .filter(|dep| bucket_tags.contains(*dep))
1760        .count();
1761    if scorecard_deps != bucket_tags.len() {
1762        return Err(format!(
1763            "{FULL_SCORECARD_TAG} waits for {scorecard_deps} of the {} full E2E buckets",
1764            bucket_tags.len()
1765        ));
1766    }
1767    let mut scorecard_twin = scorecard.clone();
1768    scorecard_twin.job.push_str(BUCK_TWIN_SUFFIX);
1769    scorecard_twin.labels = vec![FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL.into()];
1770    scorecard_twin.description = format!(
1771        "{FULL_SCORECARD_TAG}, run after the Buck import twins instead of the cargo buckets. {}",
1772        scorecard.description
1773    );
1774    for dep in &mut scorecard_twin.deps {
1775        if bucket_tags.contains(dep) {
1776            dep.push_str(BUCK_TWIN_SUFFIX);
1777        }
1778    }
1779    scorecard_twin.fail_fast_family = Some(scorecard_twin.tag());
1780    added.push(scorecard_twin);
1781
1782    let release_artifact = cfg
1783        .steps
1784        .iter()
1785        .find(|step| step.tag() == "build.buck_release_artifact")
1786        .ok_or("build.buck_release_artifact is absent")?
1787        .clone();
1788
1789    // The stage is three Cargo builds and the fixtures, in target/release,
1790    // target/stage-hermit and target/validate, so it keeps the release
1791    // artifact's 64-GiB hard cap, CPU-bound class and 32-wide CARGO_BUILD_JOBS.
1792    let mut stage = release_artifact.clone();
1793    stage.group = "e2e".into();
1794    stage.job = "buck_stage".into();
1795    stage.desc = "Stage the inputs Buck does not build for the E2E cells".into();
1796    stage.description = "Selected only by scripts/validate.rs --e2e-runner buck-local|buck-hybrid, with e2e.buck_cells. On the host it runs ci/buck-e2e/stage --from-cargo (ci/buck-e2e/validate-node --stage-only): a Cargo build in the checkout's target/ of the release test-harness and hermit-manifest-plan (target/release), the validate-profile hermit (target/stage-hermit, copied to target/validate/hermit) and detcore-dbt then the install bundle (target/validate), published with ci/publish-hermit-e2e-artifact.sh; then git archive HEAD and the CI-selected fixtures built from it. It writes ci/buck-e2e/staged/ for HEAD, SOURCE_SHA last, and refuses a tree with uncommitted changes to tracked files. It needs no buck2 and no runner, only the pinned Reverie (pre.reverie_pin): it builds nothing in target/ci or target/debug, so it runs beside build.rust_scripts (target/ci/rust-script-build) and setup.manifest_plan (target/debug), and Cargo's per-profile-directory locks keep the three from waiting on each other. Until 2026-10-05 the stage ran inside e2e.buck_cells, after those two and gate.manifest: in the full validation of hermit f53e746779 on a 284-core host they took 202, 34 and 3 seconds before the 422.74-second e2e.buck_cells began.".into();
1797    stage.labels = vec![FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL.into()];
1798    stage.cmd = BUCK_STAGE_COMMAND.into();
1799    stage.deps = vec!["pre.reverie_pin".into()];
1800    // ci/buck-e2e/stage with its builds side by side (02eba338ab), cold:
1801    // 143.3 to 144.2 s wall at 32 jobs and 128.6 s at 96, 2276 to 2312 CPU-s.
1802    // Since reproducible staging (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/pull/3948)
1803    // it builds in a fixed directory: six full validations on 2026-10-08/09
1804    // measured 172.6 to 181.2 s wall and 2438 to 2561 CPU-s.
1805    // The CPU bound is the next 300-s bucket above 1.5 x 2561 (3842). The wall
1806    // bound keeps build.buck_release_artifact's 1800 s for the same builds,
1807    // about 10 times the measurement; it also encloses the stage's lock wait,
1808    // whose default (HERMIT_BUCK_STAGE_LOCK_TIMEOUT, 1200 s) leaves room for one
1809    // stage after it.
1810    stage.timeout = 1800;
1811    stage.cpu_timeout = 3900;
1812    stage.hint.est_duration_s = 177.0;
1813    // The baseline cloned from build.buck_release_artifact (8.39 GiB) is below
1814    // what ci/buck-e2e/stage peaks at with its builds running side by side:
1815    // cgroup memory.peak 8.69 and 9.27 GiB cold at 32 jobs, 9.47 GiB at 96.
1816    stage.hint.rss_baseline_bytes = Some(10 << 30);
1817    stage.fail_fast_family = Some(BUCK_STAGE_TAG.into());
1818    added.push(stage);
1819
1820    let mut cells = release_artifact;
1821    cells.group = "e2e".into();
1822    cells.job = "buck_cells".into();
1823    cells.desc = "Run every E2E cell under Buck/Tpx".into();
1824    cells.description = "Selected only by scripts/validate.rs --e2e-runner buck-local|buck-hybrid, which passes the runner in HERMIT_VALIDATE_E2E_RUNNER and the internal buck2 in HERMIT_VALIDATE_BUCK2. On the host (ci/buck-e2e/validate-node --cells-only) it refuses with exit 2 unless e2e.buck_stage staged the inputs Buck does not build for HEAD (ci/buck-e2e/staged/SOURCE_SHA), regenerates the Buck third-party rules, stages the RE link inputs for buck-hybrid, and runs every cell of ci/expected-e2e-plan.json under Buck (ci/buck-e2e/run --no-verdict), locally or with RE-routed cells on Meta RE. It judges nothing: the rows go to $VALIDATE_RUN_STATE/buck-e2e/results for the import twins, which own the verdicts. The verify logs (run1_log_*, run2_log_*) of every cell execution that did not pass, which for an RE cell exist only as its Tpx artifacts, go to $E2E_RESULT_ROOT/buck-failed-verify-logs with an index.jsonl, each log bounded at 1 GiB + 1 MiB (hermit's own 1 GiB log bound plus its truncation marker); so do the last 64 KiB of each such execution's Hermit stderr (hermit-stderr.tail) and, in its index entry, the class of its HERMIT_INTERNAL_FAILURE line, whether or not it left a log. Any other runner value exits 2; nothing falls back to cargo.".into();
1825    cells.labels = vec![FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL.into()];
1826    cells.cmd = BUCK_CELLS_COMMAND.into();
1827    // The rust-script tools (bootstrap/regenerate-rust-deps runs two) and the
1828    // debug test-harness the import twins share come from the producers that
1829    // the stage no longer waits for.
1830    let mut cells_deps = deps[1..]
1831        .iter()
1832        .map(|dep| (*dep).to_string())
1833        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
1834    cells_deps.push(BUCK_STAGE_TAG.into());
1835    cells_deps.sort();
1836    cells.deps = cells_deps;
1837    // Measured: across the 131 e2e.buck_cells runs in the retained step
1838    // profiles of one development host (2026-09 to 2026-10-08), median 164 s,
1839    // p95 520 s, max 831 s, 6 over 600 s, none over 1000 s, none timed out;
1840    // the slowest are Buck RE downloads, which buck2 does not time out.
1841    // 2400 s is 2.9 times that maximum and equals check.lint_checks, the
1842    // largest other node budget in the full selection, so it costs the
1843    // preparation reserve nothing. It was 3600 s, carried from the node that
1844    // also staged. The committed selection is never clamped to the time left
1845    // after preparation, so a 3600 s node made every buck-runner full
1846    // validation refuse once its preparation passed the 600 s that a 4200 s
1847    // run budget left (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3896; see
1848    // committed_selections_survive_a_slow_preparation).
1849    cells.timeout = 2400;
1850    // dagrun gives a step the time it started (DAGRUN_STEP_STARTED_MONOTONIC_NS)
1851    // but not its wall bound; validate-node keeps the Buck invocation records
1852    // only in what is left of this one. The committed Buck selection is never
1853    // clamped, so this is the bound dagrun enforces.
1854    cells
1855        .env
1856        .insert(BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV.into(), cells.timeout.to_string());
1857    cells.cpu_timeout = 14400;
1858    // 422.74 s for the node that also staged (hermit f53e746779, before
1859    // 02eba338ab overlapped the stage's builds), minus the 258.9 s its step
1860    // log shows those then-serial Cargo builds took (46.10, 46.60, 120 and
1861    // 46.20 s): at most 163.8 s for the rest, of which the archive and the
1862    // fixtures also moved to e2e.buck_stage.
1863    cells.hint.est_duration_s = 165.0;
1864    // Not lowered with the stage gone: the 10-GiB baseline covered the stage's
1865    // measured peak, and the peak of the Buck run alone (its daemon and up to
1866    // 32 cells at once) has not been measured.
1867    cells.hint.rss_baseline_bytes = Some(10 << 30);
1868    cells.fail_fast_family = Some(BUCK_CELLS_TAG.into());
1869    added.push(cells);
1870
1871    cfg.steps.extend(added);
1872    Ok(())
1873}
1874
1875/// Cut the workspace producer's payload into its Cargo half and its
1876/// preparation half. Both halves keep the leading rust-script environment, so
1877/// `join_workspace_payloads` restores the original bytes exactly. Refuses
1878/// unless the payload starts with that environment and contains the
1879/// preparation boundary exactly once.
1880pub(crate) fn split_workspace_payload(payload: &str) -> Result<(String, String), String> {
1881    let environment = crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_ENVIRONMENT;
1882    if payload.matches(WORKSPACE_PREPARATION_BOUNDARY).count() != 1 {
1883        return Err(format!(
1884            "workspace producer lost its exact Nextest preparation boundary `{WORKSPACE_PREPARATION_BOUNDARY}`"
1885        ));
1886    }
1887    if !payload.starts_with(environment) {
1888        return Err("workspace producer lost its leading rust-script environment".into());
1889    }
1890    let (compile, profile) = payload
1891        .split_once(WORKSPACE_PREPARATION_BOUNDARY)
1892        .expect("boundary counted above");
1893    let preparation = &WORKSPACE_PREPARATION_BOUNDARY[" && ".len()..];
1894    let prepare = format!("{environment}{preparation}{profile}");
1895    if join_workspace_payloads(compile, &prepare)? != payload {
1896        return Err("workspace producer payload does not split losslessly".into());
1897    }
1898    Ok((compile.to_string(), prepare))
1899}
1900
1901/// Rejoin the two halves into the payload the unsplit producer ran: the Cargo
1902/// half, ` && `, then the preparation half without its repeated environment.
1903pub fn join_workspace_payloads(compile: &str, prepare: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
1904    let environment = crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_ENVIRONMENT;
1905    let preparation = &WORKSPACE_PREPARATION_BOUNDARY[" && ".len()..];
1906    let Some(prepare_tail) = prepare.strip_prefix(environment) else {
1907        return Err(format!(
1908            "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG} lost its leading rust-script environment"
1909        ));
1910    };
1911    if !prepare_tail.starts_with(preparation) {
1912        return Err(format!(
1913            "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG} runs something before `{preparation}`"
1914        ));
1915    }
1916    if !compile.starts_with(environment) {
1917        return Err(format!(
1918            "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} lost its leading rust-script environment"
1919        ));
1920    }
1921    if compile.contains(preparation.trim_end()) {
1922        return Err(format!(
1923            "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} runs Nextest preparation itself"
1924        ));
1925    }
1926    Ok(format!("{compile} && {prepare_tail}"))
1927}
1928
1929/// The complete payload of the pinned-root workspace producer, rejoined from
1930/// its two nodes. Refuses unless both nodes exist, run under byte-identical
1931/// pinned-root wrappers (the same --src, --out, --cargo-home, forwarded
1932/// environment and guard), and the prepare node depends directly on the
1933/// compile node. Checks written for the unsplit producer apply unchanged to
1934/// the returned bytes.
