1935pub fn pinned_workspace_producer_payload(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<String, String> {
1936    let find = |tag: &str| {
1937        cfg.steps
1938            .iter()
1939            .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
1940            .ok_or_else(|| format!("validation DAG lost {tag}"))
1941    };
1942    let compile = find(PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG)?;
1943    let prepare = find(PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG)?;
1944    if !prepare
1945        .deps
1946        .iter()
1947        .any(|dependency| dependency == PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG)
1948    {
1949        return Err(format!(
1950            "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG} can prepare before {PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} has built"
1951        ));
1952    }
1953    let wrapper = |step: &Step| -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
1954        if !step.cmd.starts_with("./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh ") {
1955            return Err(format!("{} does not run in the pinned root", step.tag()));
1956        }
1957        let mut argv =
1958            shell_words::split(&step.cmd).map_err(|error| format!("{}: {error}", step.tag()))?;
1959        argv.pop();
1960        Ok(argv)
1961    };
1962    if wrapper(compile)? != wrapper(prepare)? {
1963        return Err(format!(
1964            "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} and {PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG} run under different pinned-root wrappers"
1965        ));
1966    }
1967    join_workspace_payloads(
1968        &crate::nextest_build_selections::execution_command(compile)?,
1969        &crate::nextest_build_selections::execution_command(prepare)?,
1970    )
1971}
1972
1973/// Turn the pinned-root workspace twin into its compile and prepare nodes.
1974/// `twin` arrives with its pinned-root dependencies and environment set and
1975/// its payload not yet wrapped.
1976fn split_pinned_workspace_twin(twin: Step) -> Result<[Step; 2], String> {
1977    let (compile_payload, prepare_payload) = split_workspace_payload(&twin.cmd)?;
1978    let mut compile = twin.clone();
1979    compile.job = PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_JOB.into();
1980    compile.desc = PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_DESC.into();
1981    compile.description = PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_DESCRIPTION.into();
1982    compile.cmd = compile_payload;
1983    compile
1984        .deps
1985        .retain(|dependency| dependency != PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG);
1986    compile.fail_fast_family = None;
1987    compile.cmd = pinned_root_command(&compile);
1988
1989    let mut prepare = twin;
1990    prepare.desc = PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_DESC.into();
1991    prepare.description = PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_DESCRIPTION.into();
1992    prepare.cmd = prepare_payload;
1993    // The twin's own dependencies, build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root among
1994    // them, stay on the prepare node.
1995    prepare.deps.push(PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG.into());
1996    prepare.deps.sort();
1997    prepare.deps.dedup();
1998    prepare.hint.est_duration_s = PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_EST_SECONDS;
1999    prepare.hint.rss_baseline_bytes = Some(PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_RSS_BASELINE_BYTES);
2000    prepare.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes = Some(PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_HARD_MEM_MAX_BYTES);
2001    prepare.timeout = PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_WALL_SECONDS;
2002    prepare.cpu_timeout = PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_CPU_SECONDS;
2003    prepare.cmd = pinned_root_command(&prepare);
2004    Ok([compile, prepare])
2005}
2006
2007fn materialize_pinned_root(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
2008    cfg.steps.retain(|step| {
2009        step.tag() != PINNED_ROOT_FETCH_TAG && !step.job.ends_with(PINNED_ROOT_TWIN_SUFFIX)
2010    });
2011
2012    let producers = cfg
2013        .steps
2014        .iter()
2015        .filter(|step| is_pinned_root_producer(step))
2016        .cloned()
2017        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
2018    let producer_tags = producers.iter().map(Step::tag).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2019    let has_rust_scripts = producer_tags.contains("build.rust_scripts");
2020
2021    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
2022        if is_hosted_variant(step) {
2023            continue;
2024        }
2025        if !runs_in_pinned_root(step) {
2026            continue;
2027        }
2028        if step.tag() == "test.envelope_levels" {
2029            let previous =
2030                "ARGS='run --base-env=minimal --no-virtualize-cpuid --max-timeslice=disabled'";
2031            if step.cmd.matches(previous).count() != 1 {
2032                return Err(
2033                    "working-envelope command lost its exact guest argument boundary".into(),
2034                );
2035            }
2036            step.cmd = step.cmd.replace(previous, "ARGS='run --base-env=minimal --no-virtualize-cpuid --max-timeslice=disabled --mount=type=tmpfs,target=/test --workdir=/test'");
2037        }
2038        step.env
2039            .insert("HERMIT_E2E_EMPTY_WORKDIR".into(), "/test".into());
2040        step.deps = step
2041            .deps
2042            .iter()
2043            .map(|dependency| {
2044                if producer_tags.contains(dependency) {
2045                    pinned_root_twin_tag(dependency)
2046                } else {
2047                    dependency.clone()
2048                }
2049            })
2050            .collect();
2051        // The image supplies Nextest. Test execution consumes the metadata
2052        // prepared in that same root, not a host installation or Cargo cache.
2053        step.deps.retain(|dependency| dependency != "setup.nextest");
2054        if step.group == "privileged-e2e"
2055            && producer_tags.contains("build.e2e_artifact")
2056            && !step
2057                .deps
2058                .iter()
2059                .any(|dependency| dependency == "build.e2e_artifact_in_pinned_root")
2060        {
2061            step.deps.push("build.e2e_artifact_in_pinned_root".into());
2062        }
2063        if has_rust_scripts
2064            && !step
2065                .deps
2066                .iter()
2067                .any(|dependency| dependency == "build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root")
2068        {
2069            step.deps.push("build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root".into());
2070        }
2071        if !step
2072            .deps
2073            .iter()
2074            .any(|dependency| dependency == PINNED_ROOT_FETCH_TAG)
2075        {
2076            step.deps.push(PINNED_ROOT_FETCH_TAG.into());
2077        }
2078        step.deps.sort();
2079        step.deps.dedup();
2080        if !step.cmd.starts_with("./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh ") {
2081            step.cmd = pinned_root_command(step);
2082        } else {
2083            step.cmd = refresh_pinned_root_environment(&step.tag(), &step.cmd)?;
2084        }
2085    }
2086
2087    let mut twins = Vec::with_capacity(producers.len());
2088    for producer in &producers {
2089        let mut twin = producer.clone();
2090        twin.job.push_str(PINNED_ROOT_TWIN_SUFFIX);
2091        twin.labels.retain(|label| label != HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL);
2092        if producer.tag() == "compatprep.hermit_release" {
2093            twin.labels
2094                .retain(|label| label == "portable-strict-compat-only");
2095        }
2096        twin.deps = producer
2097            .deps
2098            .iter()
2099            .filter(|dependency| producer_tags.contains(*dependency))
2100            .map(|dependency| pinned_root_twin_tag(dependency))
2101            .collect();
2102        if producer.tag() == "build.e2e_artifact" {
2103            // This host-only node is a no-op in the default Cargo mode. In the
2104            // explicit Buck mode it prepares one content-addressed binary
2105            // before the network-disabled pinned root installs those bytes.
2106            twin.deps.push("build.buck_release_artifact".into());
2107        }
2108        if producer.tag() != "build.rust_scripts" && has_rust_scripts {
2109            twin.deps.push("build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root".into());
2110        }
2111        if producer.job == "manifest_guests" && producer_tags.contains("setup.manifest_plan") {
2112            twin.deps.push("setup.manifest_plan_in_pinned_root".into());
2113        }
2114        // The host rust-script producer prepares the tracked Rust dagrun before
2115        // opening its Cargo build directory. The pinned-root twin has private
2116        // agent-utils state and publishes only rust-script artifacts, so it
2117        // must not repeat that host-cache preparation.
2118        if producer.tag() == "build.rust_scripts" {
2119            if twin.cmd.matches(DAGRUN_PREPARE_COMMAND).count() != 1 {
2120                return Err(
2121                    "rust-script producer lost its exact dagrun preparation boundary".into(),
2122                );
2123            }
2124            twin.cmd = twin.cmd.replacen(DAGRUN_PREPARE_COMMAND, "", 1);
2125        }
2126        if producer.tag() == "compatprep.hermit_release" {
2127            twin.deps.push("gate.manifest".into());
2128        }
2129        twin.deps.push(PINNED_ROOT_FETCH_TAG.into());
2130        twin.deps.sort();
2131        twin.deps.dedup();
2132        twin.env
2133            .insert("HERMIT_E2E_EMPTY_WORKDIR".into(), "/test".into());
2134        // Only the trailing `nextest-binaries.rs prepare` needs the prebuilt
2135        // rust-script tools, so the Cargo half starts without them.
2136        if producer.tag() == "build.workspace" {
2137            twins.extend(split_pinned_workspace_twin(twin)?);
2138            continue;
2139        }
2140        twin.cmd = pinned_root_command(&twin);
2141        twins.push(twin);
2142    }
2143
2144    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
2145        if step.tag() == "compatprep.hermit_release" {
2146            step.labels
2147                .retain(|label| label != "portable-strict-compat-only");
2148        }
2149    }
2150    cfg.steps.push(pinned_root_fetch()?);
2151    cfg.steps.extend(twins);
2152    route_host_consumers_to_pinned_build(cfg)?;
2153    retire_unconsumed_host_producers(cfg);
2154    Ok(())
2155}
2156
2157// These six shared ancestors need separate immutable IDs because quick/super
2158// use the measured 1200-second Rust-script CPU budget, while the other profiles
2159// keep the established 7200-second cold-build budget. This is generation, not
2160// a runtime rewrite of the selected graph. Each `selftest.<name>` node also
2161// gets a variant (`is_quick_super_variant`): it depends on gate.manifest, so
2162// without one a quick/super selection would pull the ordinary producers too.
2163const QUICK_SUPER_VARIANTS: &[&str] = &[
2164    "build.rust_scripts",
2165    "build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root",
2166    "gate.manifest",
2167    "setup.manifest_plan",
2168    "setup.manifest_plan_in_pinned_root",
2169    "setup.nextest",
2170];
2171
2172fn quick_super_variant(tag: &str) -> String {
2173    format!("quick-super-{tag}")
2174}
2175
2176fn is_quick_super_variant(tag: &str) -> bool {
2177    QUICK_SUPER_VARIANTS.contains(&tag)
2178        || tag
2179            .strip_prefix(TOOL_SELF_TEST_GROUP_PREFIX)
2180            .is_some_and(|name| TOOL_SELF_TESTS.iter().any(|tool| tool.name == name))
2181}
2182
2183fn materialize_quick_super_budgets(cfg: &mut DagConfig) {
2184    let is_quick_super = |label: &str| matches!(label, "quick" | "super");
2185    let mut variants = Vec::new();
2186    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
2187        if is_quick_super_variant(&step.tag()) {
2188            let mut variant = step.clone();
2189            variant.group = format!("quick-super-{}", variant.group);
2190            variant.labels.retain(|label| is_quick_super(label));
2191            variant.fail_fast_family = Some(variant.tag());
2192            if step.group == "build" {
2193                variant.timeout = crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_WALL_SECONDS;
2194                variant.cpu_timeout =
2195                    crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_QUICK_SUPER_CPU_SECONDS;
2196                variant.hint.rss_baseline_bytes =
2197                    Some(crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_RSS_BASELINE_BYTES);
2198                variant.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes =
2199                    Some(crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_HARD_MEM_MAX_BYTES);
2200                variant.hint.est_duration_s = 0.0;
2201            }
2202            step.labels.retain(|label| !is_quick_super(label));
2203            variants.push(variant);
2204        }
2205    }
2206    cfg.steps.extend(variants);
2207    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
2208        if !step.labels.is_empty() && step.labels.iter().all(|label| is_quick_super(label)) {
2209            for dependency in &mut step.deps {
2210                if is_quick_super_variant(dependency) {
2211                    *dependency = quick_super_variant(dependency);
2212                }
2213            }
2214            step.deps.sort();
2215            step.deps.dedup();
2216        }
2217    }
2218}
2219
2220/// Keep direct preflight dependencies on host commands and tests so focused
2221/// selection cannot drop their source/gate ordering. Pinned preparation retains
2222/// its original pin/fetch prerequisites and can overlap the manifest audit.
2223fn materialize_focused_preflight(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
2224    for (labels, gate, pin, manifest_producer) in [
2225        (
2226            vec![
2227                "full",
2228                "portable",
2229                "quick",
2230                "super",
2231                "privileged",
2232                HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL,
2233            ],
2234            "gate.manifest",
2235            "pre.reverie_pin",
2236            "setup.manifest_plan",
2237        ),
2238        (
2239            vec![HOSTED_PRIVILEGED_LABEL],
2240            "gate.manifest_on_host",
2241            "pre.reverie_pin_on_host",
2242            "setup.manifest_plan_on_host",
2243        ),
2244    ] {
2245        let selected = select_steps_by_labels(
2246            cfg,
2247            &labels.into_iter().map(str::to_string).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
2248        )?;
2249        let executable_tags = selected
2250            .steps
2251            .iter()
2252            .map(Step::tag)
2253            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2254        let preflight = dagrun::select_steps_by_tags(cfg, &[gate.into()], false)?;
2255        let gate_ancestors = preflight
2256            .steps
2257            .iter()
2258            .map(Step::tag)
2259            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2260        let pin_preflight = dagrun::select_steps_by_tags(cfg, &[pin.into()], false)?;
2261        let pin_ancestors = pin_preflight
2262            .steps
2263            .iter()
2264            .map(Step::tag)
2265            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2266        for step in &mut cfg.steps {
2267            let tag = step.tag();
2268            if !executable_tags.contains(&tag) {
2269                continue;
2270            }
2271            let pinned_preparation =
2272                step.job.ends_with(PINNED_ROOT_TWIN_SUFFIX) || tag == PINNED_ROOT_FETCH_TAG;
2273            if !pinned_preparation && !gate_ancestors.contains(&tag) {
2274                step.deps.push(gate.into());
2275            }
2276            if !pin_ancestors.contains(&tag) && !gate_ancestors.contains(&tag) {
2277                step.deps.push(pin.into());
2278            }
2279            // Manifest commands need their canonical test-harness producer
2280            // even when focused selection uses other already-built artifacts.
2281            if is_manifest_run(step)
2282                || step
2283                    .job
2284                    .strip_suffix(HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX)
2285                    .unwrap_or(&step.job)
2286                    == "manifest_guests"
2287            {
2288                step.deps.push(manifest_producer.into());
2289                if step.cmd.starts_with("./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh ") {
2290                    step.deps.push("setup.manifest_plan_in_pinned_root".into());
2291                }
2292            }
2293            step.deps.sort();
2294            step.deps.dedup();
2295        }
2296    }
2297    Ok(())
2298}
2299
2300fn materialize_runtime_policy(cfg: &mut DagConfig) {
2301    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
2302        if step.fail_fast_family.is_none() {
2303            step.fail_fast_family = Some(step.tag());
2304        }
2305        // The CLI verbosity is scheduler invocation policy. Leaving a fixed
2306        // value on every node both duplicates that policy and prevents a
2307        // caller-selected level from reaching child helpers.
2308        step.env.remove("VALIDATE_VERBOSITY");
2309    }
2310}
2311
2312fn materialize_regular_calibration_launch(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
2313    let producer = cfg
2314        .steps
2315        .iter()
2316        .any(|step| step.tag() == "build.rust_scripts");
2317    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
2318        match step.tag().as_str() {
2319            "test.regular_crates" => {
2320                if !producer
2321                    || !step.cmd.contains("--nextest-calibration")
2322                    || !step
2323                        .cmd
2324                        .contains("--calibration-launch-proof /run/hermit-nextest-launch.json")
2325                {
2326                    return Err(
2327                        "regular pinned calibration lost its explicit launch observation boundary"
2328                            .into(),
2329                    );
2330                }
2331                step.deps.push("build.rust_scripts".into());
2332                step.deps.sort();
2333                step.deps.dedup();
2334            }
2335            "test.regular_crates_on_host" => {
2336                const RUNNER: &str = "./ci/run-nextest-counted.sh";
2337                if step.cmd.matches(RUNNER).count() != 1 || step.cmd.contains("--calibration-") {
2338                    return Err(
2339                        "regular host calibration requires its explicit native command boundary"
2340                            .into(),
2341                    );
2342                }
2343                step.cmd = step
2344                    .cmd
2345                    .replace(RUNNER, &format!("{RUNNER} --calibration-host"));
2346            }
2347            _ => {}
2348        }
2349    }
2350    Ok(())
2351}
2352
2353#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, Ord, PartialEq, PartialOrd)]
2354enum GeneratedPartition {
2355    PortableCompat,
2356    PortableFocusedCompat,
2357    StrictCompat,
2358    SabreCompat,
2359    E9patchCompat,
2360    RrCompat,
2361    SuperCompat,
2362    SuperStress,
2363}
2364
2365fn generated_partition(step: &Step) -> Option<GeneratedPartition> {
2366    match step.group.as_str() {
2367        // The focused lane's corpus is the static bucket node
2368        // portablecompat.manifest_compat; only its fixtures are generated.
2369        "portablecompatprep" => {
2370            return Some(GeneratedPartition::PortableFocusedCompat);
2371        }
2372        // The strict lane's rows are the compat.yaml cells labelled
2373        // strict-compat-only, run by the static node
2374        // strictcompat.manifest_compat; only its fixtures are generated.
2375        "strictcompatprep" => return Some(GeneratedPartition::StrictCompat),
2376        // The SaBRe lane's rows are the compat.yaml cells labelled
2377        // sabre-compat-only, run by the static node
2378        // sabrecompat.manifest_compat; only its fixtures are generated.
2379        "sabrecompatprep" => return Some(GeneratedPartition::SabreCompat),
2380        "e9patchcompat" | "e9patchcompatprep" => {
2381            return Some(GeneratedPartition::E9patchCompat);
2382        }
2383        // The rr lane's rows are the compat.yaml replay cells labelled
2384        // rr-compat-only, run by the static node rrcompat.manifest_compat;
2385        // only its fixtures are generated.
2386        "rrcompatprep" => return Some(GeneratedPartition::RrCompat),
2387        _ => {}
2388    }
2389    if step.group == "superstress" {
2390        return Some(GeneratedPartition::SuperStress);
2391    }
2392    if step.tag() == "super-compatprep.fixtures"
2393        || (step.group == "compat" && step.labels.iter().any(|label| label == "super"))
2394    {
2395        return Some(GeneratedPartition::SuperCompat);
2396    }
2397    if step.tag() == "compatprep.fixtures"
2398        || (step.group == "compat"
2399            && step
2400                .labels
2401                .iter()
2402                .any(|label| matches!(label.as_str(), "portable" | "full")))
2403    {
2404        return Some(GeneratedPartition::PortableCompat);
2405    }
2406    None
2407}
2408
2409fn refresh_generated_partitions(
2410    mut committed: DagConfig,
2411    generated: DagConfig,
2412) -> Result<DagConfig, String> {
2413    let mut replacements = BTreeMap::<GeneratedPartition, Vec<Step>>::new();
2414    for step in generated.steps {
2415        let Some(partition) = generated_partition(&step) else {
2416            if step.labels == ["generator-dependency-anchor"] || step.tag() == "build.rust_scripts"
2417            {
2418                continue;
2419            }
2420            return Err(format!(
2421                "generated-plan exporter emitted static-looking node {}",
2422                step.tag()
2423            ));
2424        };
2425        replacements.entry(partition).or_default().push(step);
2426    }
2427    for (partition, expected) in [
2428        // 1 since the portable strict corpus became the manifest bucket
2429        // e2e.manifest_compat on 2026-10-01: only compatprep.fixtures remains.
2430        (GeneratedPartition::PortableCompat, 1usize),
2431        // 1 since the focused portable lane runs the same bucket through the
2432        // static node portablecompat.manifest_compat: only its fixtures remain.
2433        (GeneratedPartition::PortableFocusedCompat, 1usize),
2434        // 1 since the strict lane's 193 probes became compat.yaml cells run by
2435        // the static node strictcompat.manifest_compat: only its fixtures remain.
2436        (GeneratedPartition::StrictCompat, 1usize),
2437        // 1 since the SaBRe lane's 212 probes became compat.yaml cells run by
2438        // the static node sabrecompat.manifest_compat: only its fixtures remain.
2439        (GeneratedPartition::SabreCompat, 1usize),
2440        // 175 since the one-build change of 2026-09-30 added
2441        // e9patchcompatprep.release_resources, which stages the release
2442        // resources the retired host build.runtime_release used to provide.
2443        (GeneratedPartition::E9patchCompat, 175usize),
2444        // 1 since the rr lane's 139 probes became compat.yaml replay cells run
2445        // by the static node rrcompat.manifest_compat: only its fixtures remain.
2446        (GeneratedPartition::RrCompat, 1usize),
2447        (GeneratedPartition::SuperCompat, 5usize),
2448        // 7 since each stress probe's 20 repetition nodes became one node that
2449        // runs the repetitions and reports each as a structured test result:
2450        // the 2 availability nodes plus 5 probe nodes.
2451        (GeneratedPartition::SuperStress, 7usize),
2452    ] {
2453        let actual = replacements.get(&partition).map_or(0, Vec::len);
2454        if actual != expected {
2455            return Err(format!(
2456                "generated {partition:?} partition has {actual} nodes, expected {expected}"
2457            ));
2458        }
2459    }
2460
2461    let mut inserted = BTreeSet::new();
2462    let mut steps = Vec::with_capacity(committed.steps.len());
2463    for step in committed.steps {
2464        let Some(partition) = generated_partition(&step) else {
2465            steps.push(step);
2466            continue;
2467        };
2468        if inserted.insert(partition) {
2469            steps.extend(
2470                replacements
2471                    .remove(&partition)
2472                    .expect("validated generated partition exists"),
2473            );
2474        }
2475    }
2476    // The independent typed source intentionally contains no corpus-derived
2477    // partition anchors. Append every generated partition exactly once in a
2478    // stable order after replacing any legacy anchors supplied by a focused
2479    // test fixture.
2480    for partition in [
2481        GeneratedPartition::PortableCompat,
2482        GeneratedPartition::PortableFocusedCompat,
2483        GeneratedPartition::StrictCompat,
2484        GeneratedPartition::SabreCompat,
2485        GeneratedPartition::E9patchCompat,
2486        GeneratedPartition::RrCompat,
2487        GeneratedPartition::SuperCompat,
2488        GeneratedPartition::SuperStress,
2489    ] {
2490        if inserted.insert(partition) {
2491            steps.extend(
2492                replacements
2493                    .remove(&partition)
2494                    .expect("validated generated partition exists"),
2495            );
2496        }
2497    }
2498    if inserted.len() != 8 {
2499        return Err(format!(
2500            "committed DAG has anchors for {} of 8 generated partitions",
2501            inserted.len()
2502        ));
2503    }
2504    committed.steps = steps;
2505    Ok(committed)
2506}
2507
2508fn attach_result_ownership(cfg: &mut DagConfig, cells: &Populations) {
2509    for step in &mut cfg.steps {
2510        let structured = step
2511            .result_manifests
2512            .take()
2513            .unwrap_or_default()
2514            .into_iter()
2515            .filter(|manifest| matches!(manifest, ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(_)))
2516            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
2517        let hosted_portable = step.labels == [HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL];
2518        let portable_focused = step.tag() == PORTABLE_FOCUSED_COMPAT_TAG;
2519        let mut owned = cells
2520            .owned_by(step)
2521            .into_iter()
2522            .filter(|cell| !(hosted_portable && hosted_portable_excludes(cell)))
2523            .filter(|cell| !(portable_focused && portable_focused_excludes(cell)))
2524            .cloned()
2525            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
2526        if step.tag() == "quick.e2e_verify" {
2527            owned.extend(cells.iter().filter(|cell| quick_verify_cell(cell)).cloned());
2528        }
2529        owned.sort_by_key(result_identity);
2530        let mut manifests = owned
2531            .into_iter()
2532            .map(ResultManifest::ManifestCell)
2533            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
2534        manifests.extend(structured);
2535        step.result_manifests = Some(manifests);
2536    }
2537}
2538
2539fn assert_structured_result_producers(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
2540    let mut expected = BTreeMap::<&str, StructuredResultProducerKind>::new();
2541    for kind in StructuredResultProducerKind::ALL {
2542        for tag in kind.tags() {
2543            if expected.insert(tag, kind).is_some() {
2544                return Err(format!(
2545                    "structured result producer registry declares {tag} more than once"
2546                ));
2547            }
2548        }
2549    }
2550    // 118 since the five super stress probe nodes, each writing one row per
2551    // repetition, joined them; 113 since rrcompat.manifest_compat, the rr
2552    // lane's bucket, joined them;
2553    // 112 since strictcompat.manifest_compat, the strict lane's bucket, joined
2554    // them; 111 since sabrecompat.manifest_compat, the SaBRe lane's bucket, joined
2555    // them; 110 since portablecompat.manifest_compat, the focused lane's corpus
2556    // bucket, joined them; 109 since e2e.manifest_compat and its hosted twin
2557    // joined the test-harness producers (2026-10-01); 120 since
2558    // test.detcore_time and its hosted twin were enrolled; 121 since
2559    // privileged-test.pmu_detcore_time_cases took the 29 tests_time cases that
2560    // need a PMU (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3663). 137 with
2561    // the 16 Buck import twins. 133 since test.liteinst_strict, its hosted
2562    // twin, liteinst.strict and super.liteinst_python3_verify_diagnostics
2563    // were retired with the LiteInst host hybrid
2564    // (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3520). 134 since
2565    // test.record_replay joined the Nextest producers. 135 since
2566    // test.pmu_integration_cases took the 25 external_signal_interrupt
2567    // ptrace cases that need the PMU timer.
2568    // One required local M1 target adds one Nextest result producer.
2569    // 137 with the additive required M2 stack consumer; all 136 prior producers remain.
2570    if expected.len() != 137 {
2571        return Err(format!(
2572            "structured result producer registry has {} entries, expected 137",
2573            expected.len()
2574        ));
2575    }
2576
2577    let mut expected_counts = NEXTEST_EXPECTED_COUNTS
2578        .iter()
2579        .copied()
2580        .collect::<BTreeMap<_, _>>();
2581    // 43 since privileged-test.pmu_detcore_time_cases joined it
2582    // (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3663). 41 since
2583    // test.liteinst_strict and test.liteinst_strict_on_host were retired with
2584    // the LiteInst host hybrid (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3520);
2585    // 42 since test.record_replay joined it; 43 since
2586    // test.pmu_integration_cases joined it.
2587    // The M1 seven-identity and M2 eleven-identity targets each add one
2588    // registry entry without altering existing CLI counts.
2589    if expected_counts.len() != 45 {
2590        return Err(format!(
2591            "Nextest expected-count registry has {} entries, expected 45",
2592            expected_counts.len()
2593        ));
2594    }
2595
2596    let mut seen_by_kind = BTreeMap::<StructuredResultProducerKind, usize>::new();
2597    for step in &cfg.steps {
2598        let tag = step.tag();
2599        let command_kinds = StructuredResultProducerKind::ALL
2600            .into_iter()
2601            .filter_map(|kind| {
2602                let occurrences = step.cmd.matches(kind.command_marker()).count();
2603                (occurrences != 0).then_some((kind, occurrences))
2604            })
2605            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
2606        let command_kind = match command_kinds.as_slice() {
2607            [] => None,
2608            [(kind, 1)] => Some(*kind),
2609            [(kind, occurrences)] => {
2610                return Err(format!(
2611                    "{tag} invokes the {kind:?} structured result producer {occurrences} times; expected exactly once"
2612                ));
2613            }
2614            _ => {
2615                return Err(format!(
2616                    "{tag} invokes more than one structured result producer: {command_kinds:?}"
2617                ));
2618            }
2619        };
2620        let command = crate::nextest_build_selections::execution_command(step)?;
2621        if command.contains("NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED") {
2622            return Err(format!(
2623                "{tag} declares NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED in command text instead of typed step environment"
2624            ));
2625        }
2626        if command_kind == Some(StructuredResultProducerKind::Nextest)
2627            || step.cmd.contains("nextest-binaries.rs executable ")
2628        {
2629            crate::nextest_build_selections::assert_command_selection(step)?;
2630        }
2631        let declared = step
2632            .structured_test_results_manifest()
2633            .map_err(|error| format!("{tag}: {error}"))?;
2634        match (expected.remove(tag.as_str()), command_kind, declared) {
2635            (Some(expected_kind), Some(actual_kind), Some(manifest)) => {
2636                if actual_kind != expected_kind {
2637                    return Err(format!(
2638                        "{tag} is registered as {expected_kind:?} but invokes {actual_kind:?}"
2639                    ));
2640                }
2641                if manifest.owner != tag {
2642                    return Err(format!(
2643                        "{tag} declares structured result owner {:?}",
2644                        manifest.owner
2645                    ));
2646                }
2647                *seen_by_kind.entry(expected_kind).or_default() += 1;
2648            }
2649            (Some(expected_kind), None, _) => {
2650                return Err(format!(
2651                    "{tag} is registered as {expected_kind:?} but no longer invokes that writer"
2652                ));
2653            }
2654            (Some(expected_kind), Some(actual_kind), None) => {
2655                return Err(format!(
2656                    "{tag} invokes {actual_kind:?} and is registered as {expected_kind:?}, but omits its structured result declaration"
2657                ));
2658            }
2659            (None, Some(actual_kind), _) => {
2660                return Err(format!(
2661                    "{tag} invokes unregistered structured result producer {actual_kind:?}"
2662                ));
2663            }
2664            (None, None, Some(_)) => {
2665                return Err(format!(
2666                    "{tag} declares structured results but invokes no registered writer"
2667                ));
2668            }
2669            (None, None, None) => {}
2670        }
2671
2672        match (
2673            expected_counts.remove(tag.as_str()),
2674            step.env.get("NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED"),
2675        ) {
2676            (Some(expected_count), Some(actual)) if actual == &expected_count.to_string() => {}
2677            (Some(expected_count), Some(actual)) => {
2678                return Err(format!(
2679                    "{tag} expects {expected_count} Nextest tests but declares {actual:?}"
2680                ));
2681            }
2682            (Some(expected_count), None) => {
2683                return Err(format!(
2684                    "{tag} omits NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED={expected_count}"
2685                ));
2686            }
2687            (None, Some(actual)) => {
2688                return Err(format!(
2689                    "{tag} declares unexpected NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED={actual:?}"
2690                ));
2691            }
2692            (None, None) => {}
2693        }
2694    }
2695    if !expected.is_empty() {
2696        return Err(format!(
2697            "registered structured result producers are absent from the DAG: {}",
2698            expected.keys().copied().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")
2699        ));
2700    }
2701    if !expected_counts.is_empty() {
2702        return Err(format!(
2703            "Nextest expected-count steps are absent from the DAG: {}",
2704            expected_counts
2705                .keys()
2706                .copied()
2707                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
2708                .join(", ")
2709        ));
2710    }
2711    let actual_group_counts = StructuredResultProducerKind::ALL
2712        .into_iter()
2713        .map(|kind| seen_by_kind.get(&kind).copied().unwrap_or_default())
2714        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
2715    // TestHarness 34 -> 36 with the compat bucket and its hosted twin, and
2716    // 37 with the focused lane's portablecompat.manifest_compat, and 38 with
2717    // the SaBRe lane's sabrecompat.manifest_compat, and 39 with the strict
2718    // lane's strictcompat.manifest_compat, and 40 with the rr lane's
2719    // rrcompat.manifest_compat. Envelope 2 -> 7 with the five super stress
2720    // probe nodes. Nextest 69 -> 71 when test.detcore_time and its hosted twin
2721    // were enrolled, and 72 with privileged-test.pmu_detcore_time_cases
2722    // (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3663). TestHarness 56 with
2723    // the 16 Buck import twins. Nextest 68 since test.liteinst_strict, its
2724    // hosted twin, liteinst.strict and super.liteinst_python3_verify_diagnostics
2725    // were retired with the LiteInst host hybrid
2726    // (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3520), and 69 with
2727    // test.record_replay, and 70 with test.pmu_integration_cases.
2728    // The local required allocator and stack consumers each add one Nextest writer.
2729    if actual_group_counts != [72, 56, 7, 2] {
2730        return Err(format!(
2731            "structured result producer group counts changed: {actual_group_counts:?}"
2732        ));
2733    }
2734    Ok(())
2735}
2736
2737fn result_identity(result: &DagManifest) -> String {
2738    format!(
2739        "{}/{}/{}/{}/{}",
2740        result.lane,
2741        result.category,
2742        result.test.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
2743        result.mode.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
2744        result.backend.as_deref().unwrap_or("")
2745    )
2746}
2747
2748/// The manifest-cell result selectors a step owns, borrowed.
2749///
2750/// The same population as `Step::effective_result_manifests`, which clones
2751/// every selector on every call: the explicit `result_manifests` cells when
2752/// the step declares them (an explicit empty list owns nothing), else its
2753/// `manifest` selector.
2754fn result_selectors(step: &Step) -> impl Iterator<Item = &DagManifest> + '_ {
2755    let explicit = step.result_manifests.as_deref();
2756    let fallback = if explicit.is_none() {
2757        step.manifest.as_ref()
2758    } else {
2759        None
2760    };
2761    explicit
2762        .into_iter()
2763        .flatten()
2764        .filter_map(|manifest| match manifest {
2765            ResultManifest::ManifestCell(cell) => Some(cell),
2766            ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(_) => None,
2767        })
2768        .chain(fallback)
2769}
2770
2771/// Selector fields in the order `DagManifest::matches_exact_result` compares
2772/// them: lane, category, then test, mode and backend, where `None` matches
2773/// any value.
2774type SelectorKey<'a> = (
2775    &'a str,
2776    &'a str,
2777    Option<&'a str>,
2778    Option<&'a str>,
2779    Option<&'a str>,
2780);
2781
2782/// `result_manifest_owner` for many results against one step list.
2783///
2784/// `result_manifest_owner` scans every step and clones every step's
2785/// selectors for each result, so checking a profile's results costs results
2786/// times selectors, almost all of it allocation: 1,233 by 1,233 for the full
2787/// profile, about seven million selector clones across the profiles and the
2788/// Buck E2E selection, which made `generate` 80% of the scorecard self-test's
2789/// CPU time. This indexes the borrowed selectors once by their
2790/// exact fields. A selector matches an exact result exactly when its lane
2791/// and category are equal and each of test, mode and backend is either unset
2792/// or equal, so the owners of an exact result are precisely the steps under
2793/// the eight keys that set each of those three fields to the result's value
2794/// or leave it unset.
2795///
2796/// The index alone decides only the one-owner case. An inexact result, no
2797/// owner, or several owners is handed to `result_manifest_owner` itself, so
2798/// every refusal and its text are the library's own.
2799struct ResultOwnerIndex<'a> {
2800    steps: &'a [Step],
2801    owners: BTreeMap<SelectorKey<'a>, Vec<usize>>,
2802}
2803
2804impl<'a> ResultOwnerIndex<'a> {
2805    fn new(steps: &'a [Step]) -> Self {
2806        let mut owners = BTreeMap::<SelectorKey<'a>, Vec<usize>>::new();
2807        for (index, step) in steps.iter().enumerate() {
2808            for selector in result_selectors(step) {
2809                let key = (
2810                    selector.lane.as_str(),
2811                    selector.category.as_str(),
2812                    selector.test.as_deref(),
2813                    selector.mode.as_deref(),
2814                    selector.backend.as_deref(),
2815                );
2816                let steps = owners.entry(key).or_default();
2817                if steps.last() != Some(&index) {
2818                    steps.push(index);
2819                }
2820            }
2821        }
2822        Self { steps, owners }
2823    }
2824
2825    fn owner(&self, result: &DagManifest) -> Result<&'a Step, String> {
2826        let lane = result.lane.as_str();
2827        let category = result.category.as_str();
2828        let (Some(test), Some(mode), Some(backend)) = (
2829            result.test.as_deref(),
2830            result.mode.as_deref(),
2831            result.backend.as_deref(),
2832        ) else {
2833            return result_manifest_owner(self.steps, result);
2834        };
2835        if [lane, category, test, mode, backend]
2836            .iter()
2837            .any(|field| field.is_empty())
2838        {
2839            return result_manifest_owner(self.steps, result);
2840        }
2841        let mut found = Vec::new();
2842        for test in [Some(test), None] {
2843            for mode in [Some(mode), None] {
2844                for backend in [Some(backend), None] {
2845                    if let Some(steps) = self.owners.get(&(lane, category, test, mode, backend)) {
2846                        found.extend_from_slice(steps);
2847                    }
2848                }
2849            }
2850        }
2851        found.sort_unstable();
2852        found.dedup();
2853        match found.as_slice() {
2854            [owner] => Ok(&self.steps[*owner]),
2855            _ => result_manifest_owner(self.steps, result),
2856        }
2857    }
2858}
2859
2860/// Manifest categories the quick profile's pinned-root verify smoke
2861/// (quick.e2e_verify) omits: the compatibility corpus runs programs installed
2862/// on the validation host and reads fixtures compatprep.fixtures prepares,
2863/// neither of which the quick profile has. quick.e2e_verify passes
2864/// `--exclude-category` for each, and neither its expected cells nor its
2865/// owned results include them.
2866pub const QUICK_EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES: &[&str] = &["compat"];
2867
2868/// The exact harness command quick.e2e_verify runs, with one
2869/// `--exclude-category` per [`QUICK_EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES`] entry. The generator
2870/// requires the committed node to end with it and validate.rs requires it of
2871/// the raw publisher, so the omitted cells and the omitted results agree.
2872pub fn quick_verify_command() -> String {
2873    let mut command =
2874        "target/debug/test-harness run --lane portable --mode verify --backend ptrace --ci-only"
2875            .to_string();
2876    for category in QUICK_EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES {
2877        command.push_str(" --exclude-category ");
2878        command.push_str(category);
2879    }
2880    command
2881}
2882
2883fn quick_verify_cell(cell: &DagManifest) -> bool {
2884    cell.lane == "portable"
2885        && cell.mode.as_deref() == Some("verify")
2886        && cell.backend.as_deref() == Some("ptrace")
2887        && !QUICK_EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES.contains(&cell.category.as_str())
2888}
2889
2890fn expected_for_label<'a>(label: &str, cells: &'a [DagManifest]) -> Vec<&'a DagManifest> {
2891    cells
2892        .iter()
2893        .filter(|cell| match label {
2894            "full" => true,
2895            "portable" => cell.lane == "portable",
2896            HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL => cell.lane == "portable" && !hosted_portable_excludes(cell),
2897            HOSTED_PRIVILEGED_LABEL => cell.lane == "privileged",
2898            "privileged" => cell.lane == "privileged",
2899            "quick" => quick_verify_cell(cell),
2900            // The corpus-only run type runs exactly the strict compatibility
2901            // bucket, without the backends its release build cannot run.
2902            "portable-strict-compat-only" => {
2903                cell.lane == "portable"
2904                    && cell.category == "compat"
2905                    && !portable_focused_excludes(cell)
2906            }
2907            "super" => false,
2908            // The import twins own every cell, as the cargo buckets do in full.
2909            FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL => true,
2910            _ => false,
2911        })
2912        .collect()
2913}
2914
2915/// The Buck full selection replaces exactly the cargo E2E path and reports
2916/// the same cells, each owned by exactly one node.
2917fn assert_buck_e2e_selection(cfg: &DagConfig, cells: &[DagManifest]) -> Result<(), String> {
2918    let replaced = buck_replaced_tags(cfg)?;
2919    let mut expected_replaced = BUCK_REPLACED_PRODUCERS
2920        .iter()
2921        .map(|tag| (*tag).to_string())
2922        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2923    expected_replaced.insert(FULL_SCORECARD_TAG.into());
2924    for category in [
2925        "applications",
2926        "bin_c",
2927        "c_programs",
2928        "chaos_c",
2929        "compat",
2930        "data_handling",
2931        "debugger_c",
2932        "determinism_stress",
2933        "determinism_stress_c",
2934        "language_runtimes",
2935        "shared_futex_c",
2936        "system_utils",
2937        "util_c",
2938    ] {
2939        expected_replaced.insert(format!("e2e.manifest_{category}"));
2940    }
2941    for category in ["applications", "c_programs", "system_utils"] {
2942        expected_replaced.insert(format!("privileged-e2e.manifest_{category}"));
2943    }
2944    if replaced != expected_replaced {
2945        return Err(format!(
2946            "the Buck E2E selection replaces {:?}, expected {:?}",
2947            replaced, expected_replaced
2948        ));
2949    }
2950    let selected = buck_e2e_selection(cfg)?;
2951    let full = select_steps_by_labels(cfg, &["full".to_string()])?;
2952    let full_tags = full.steps.iter().map(Step::tag).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2953    let selected_tags = selected
2954        .steps
2955        .iter()
2956        .map(Step::tag)
2957        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2958    // The selection is the full profile minus the replaced nodes plus exactly
2959    // the full-buck-e2e nodes (on the committed DAG: 90 - 22 + 19 = 87, pinned
2960    // by committed_buck_e2e_selection_replaces_22_nodes_with_18, named before
2961    // e2e.buck_stage made the added nodes 19).
2962    let added = selected_tags
2963        .difference(&full_tags)
2964        .cloned()
2965        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2966    let buck_nodes = cfg
2967        .steps
2968        .iter()
2969        .filter(|step| step.labels == [FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL])
2970        .map(Step::tag)
2971        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2972    if added != buck_nodes {
2973        return Err(format!(
2974            "the Buck E2E selection adds {added:?}, expected the full-buck-e2e nodes {buck_nodes:?}"
2975        ));
2976    }
2977    let dropped = full_tags
2978        .difference(&selected_tags)
2979        .cloned()
2980        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2981    if dropped != replaced {
2982        return Err(format!(
2983            "the Buck E2E selection drops {dropped:?}, but replaces {replaced:?}"
2984        ));
2985    }
2986    // The stage needs only the pinned Reverie, so it overlaps the producers
2987    // the cells wait for; the cells refuse inputs staged for another commit,
2988    // so they must wait for it.
2989    let runner_node = |tag: &str, flag: &str| {
2990        selected
2991            .steps
2992            .iter()
2993            .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
2994            .filter(|step| {
2995                step.cmd
2996                    .ends_with(&format!("./ci/buck-e2e/validate-node {flag}"))
2997            })
2998            .ok_or_else(|| {
2999                format!("the Buck E2E selection has no {tag} running validate-node {flag}")
3000            })
3001    };
3002    let stage = runner_node(BUCK_STAGE_TAG, "--stage-only")?;
3003    if stage.deps != ["pre.reverie_pin"] {
3004        return Err(format!(
3005            "{BUCK_STAGE_TAG} depends on {:?}, expected only pre.reverie_pin",
3006            stage.deps
3007        ));
3008    }
3009    let cells_node = runner_node(BUCK_CELLS_TAG, "--cells-only")?;
3010    if !cells_node.deps.iter().any(|dep| dep == BUCK_STAGE_TAG) {
3011        return Err(format!(
3012            "{BUCK_CELLS_TAG} does not wait for {BUCK_STAGE_TAG}"
3013        ));
3014    }
3015    // Keeping the records after the cells ran must end inside the bound dagrun
3016    // enforces, or completed work is reaped and reported as a timeout.
3017    let wall = cells_node.timeout.to_string();
3018    if cells_node.timeout == 0 || cells_node.env.get(BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV) != Some(&wall) {
3019        return Err(format!(
3020            "{BUCK_CELLS_TAG} sets {BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV} to {:?}, expected its wall bound {wall:?}",
3021            cells_node.env.get(BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV)
3022        ));
3023    }
3024    for step in &selected.steps {
3025        if step.labels == [FULL_BUCK_E2E_LABEL]
3026            && !is_buck_runner_node(step)
3027            && !step
3028                .deps
3029                .iter()
3030                .any(|dep| dep == BUCK_CELLS_TAG || dep.ends_with(BUCK_TWIN_SUFFIX))
3031        {
3032            return Err(format!("{} does not wait for the Buck rows", step.tag()));
3033        }
3034        // A twin reads rows it is handed on the host; inside the pinned root
3035        // $VALIDATE_RUN_STATE would not name the rows e2e.buck_cells wrote.
3036        if is_buck_import_twin(step)
3037            && (step.cmd.starts_with("./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh")
3038                || step.cmd.matches("E2E_IMPORT_RESULTS=").count() != 1
3039                || step.cmd.contains("run-with-hermit-e2e-artifact"))
3040        {
3041            return Err(format!(
3042                "{} is not a host-side import of the Buck rows",
3043                step.tag()
3044            ));
3045        }
3046    }
3047    let expected = expected_for_label("full", cells);
3048    let owners = ResultOwnerIndex::new(&selected.steps);
3049    for result in &expected {
3050        owners
3051            .owner(result)
3052            .map_err(|error| format!("Buck E2E result ownership failed: {error}"))?;
3053    }
3054    let expected_ids = expected
3055        .into_iter()
3056        .map(result_identity)
3057        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
3058    let actual_ids = selected
3059        .steps
3060        .iter()
3061        .flat_map(result_selectors)
3062        .map(result_identity)
3063        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
3064    if actual_ids != expected_ids {
3065        return Err(format!(
3066            "the Buck E2E selection reports {} cells, the full profile {}",
3067            actual_ids.len(),
3068            expected_ids.len()
3069        ));
3070    }
3071    Ok(())
3072}
3073
3074/// The pinned-root workspace producer's two nodes. The Cargo half has no path
3075/// to the rust-script producer, the preparation half depends directly on both,
3076/// nothing but the preparation half consumes the Cargo half (so no consumer
3077/// can read target/validate before its executables are listed and hashed),
3078/// and the two payloads rejoin to exactly the unsplit producer's payload.
3079fn assert_pinned_workspace_split(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
3080    let by_tag = cfg
3081        .steps
3082        .iter()
3083        .map(|step| (step.tag(), step))
3084        .collect::<BTreeMap<_, _>>();
3085    let find = |tag: &str| {
3086        by_tag
3087            .get(tag)
3088            .copied()
3089            .ok_or_else(|| format!("validation DAG lost {tag}"))
3090    };
3091    let compile = find(PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG)?;
3092    let prepare = find(PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG)?;
3093    let mut pending = compile.deps.clone();
3094    let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
3095    while let Some(dependency) = pending.pop() {
3096        if dependency == PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG {
3097            return Err(format!(
3098                "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} waits for {PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG}; only the preparation half needs the rust-script tools"
3099            ));
3100        }
3101        if seen.insert(dependency.clone()) {
3102            pending.extend(find(&dependency)?.deps.iter().cloned());
3103        }
3104    }
3105    for required in [PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG, PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG] {
3106        if !prepare.deps.iter().any(|dependency| dependency == required) {
3107            return Err(format!(
3108                "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG} must depend directly on {required}"
3109            ));
3110        }
3111    }
3112    if let Some(consumer) = cfg.steps.iter().find(|step| {
3113        step.tag() != PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG
3114            && step
3115                .deps
3116                .iter()
3117                .any(|dependency| dependency == PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG)
3118    }) {
3119        return Err(format!(
3120            "{} consumes {PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} directly; only {PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG} may, so no consumer runs before preparation",
3121            consumer.tag()
3122        ));
3123    }
3124    if compile.labels != prepare.labels {
3125        return Err(format!(
3126            "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} labels {:?} differ from {PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG} labels {:?}",
3127            compile.labels, prepare.labels
3128        ));
3129    }
3130    let payload = pinned_workspace_producer_payload(cfg)?;
3131    let (compile_payload, prepare_payload) = split_workspace_payload(&payload)?;
3132    if compile_payload != crate::nextest_build_selections::execution_command(compile)?
3133        || prepare_payload != crate::nextest_build_selections::execution_command(prepare)?
3134    {
3135        return Err(format!(
3136            "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} and {PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG} are not the exact halves of one workspace payload"
3137        ));
3138    }
3139    // The Cargo half carries the unsplit producer's heavy budget, which the
3140    // hosted producer still runs whole. The walls are compared with their
3141    // authored values: the Cargo half keeps build.workspace's, and only the
3142    // hosted twin carries the longer hosted wall.
3143    if compile.timeout != crate::validation_dag_static::WORKSPACE_WALL_SECONDS {
3144        return Err(format!(
3145            "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} lost the workspace build's budget"
3146        ));
3147    }
3148    if let Some(hosted) = by_tag.get("build.workspace_on_host") {
3149        if hosted.timeout != crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_WORKSPACE_WALL_SECONDS {
3150            return Err(format!(
3151                "build.workspace_on_host has a {}s wall, not the hosted {}s",
3152                hosted.timeout,
3153                crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_WORKSPACE_WALL_SECONDS
3154            ));
3155        }
3156        if compile.cpu_timeout != hosted.cpu_timeout
3157            || compile.hint.rss_baseline_bytes != hosted.hint.rss_baseline_bytes
3158            || compile.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes != hosted.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes
3159            || compile.hint.classification != hosted.hint.classification
3160        {
3161            return Err(format!(
3162                "{PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG} lost the workspace build's budget"
3163            ));
3164        }
3165    }
3166    Ok(())
3167}
3168
3169/// check.script_unit_tests_on_host is check.script_unit_tests with only its
3170/// hosted identity and longer wall changed, and the local node keeps the
3171/// authored wall and leaves the hosted lane to its twin.
3172fn assert_hosted_script_unit_tests(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
3173    let only = |tag: &str| -> Result<&Step, String> {
3174        let mut found = cfg.steps.iter().filter(|step| step.tag() == tag);
3175        match (found.next(), found.next()) {
3176            (Some(step), None) => Ok(step),
3177            (None, _) => Err(format!("{tag} is absent")),
3178            (Some(_), Some(_)) => Err(format!("{tag} appears more than once")),
3179        }
3180    };
3181    let local = only(SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG)?;
3182    let hosted = only(HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG)?;
3183    if local.timeout != crate::validation_dag_static::SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_SECONDS {
3184        return Err(format!(
3185            "{SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} has a {}s wall, not its authored {}s",
3186            local.timeout,
3187            crate::validation_dag_static::SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_SECONDS
3188        ));
3189    }
3190    if hosted.timeout != crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_SECONDS {
3191        return Err(format!(
3192            "{HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} has a {}s wall, not the hosted {}s",
3193            hosted.timeout,
3194            crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_SECONDS
3195        ));
3196    }
3197    if local
3198        .labels
3199        .iter()
3200        .any(|label| label == HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL)
3201    {
3202        return Err(format!(
3203            "{SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} keeps the {HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL} label its hosted twin carries"
3204        ));
3205    }
3206    if hosted.labels != [HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL] {
3207        return Err(format!(
3208            "{HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} has labels {:?}, not only {HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL}",
3209            hosted.labels
3210        ));
3211    }
3212    if hosted.fail_fast_family.as_deref() != Some(HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG) {
3213        return Err(format!(
3214            "{HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} is not its own fail-fast family"
3215        ));
3216    }
3217    // Everything else is the local node's: undo the four permitted changes and
3218    // the description suffix, then compare the two nodes' serialized form.
3219    let mut normalized = hosted.clone();
3220    normalized.job = local.job.clone();
3221    normalized.labels = local.labels.clone();
3222    normalized.timeout = local.timeout;
3223    normalized.fail_fast_family = local.fail_fast_family.clone();
3224    normalized.description = hosted
3225        .description
3226        .strip_suffix(crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_WALL_DESCRIPTION)
3227        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} lost its hosted wall description"))?
3228        .to_string();
3229    let one = |step: &Step| {
3230        dag_to_json(&DagConfig {
3231            steps: vec![step.clone()],
3232            ..Default::default()
3233        })
3234    };
3235    if one(&normalized) != one(local) {
3236        return Err(format!(
3237            "{HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} differs from {SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG} in more than its hosted identity and wall"
3238        ));
3239    }
3240    Ok(())
3241}
3242
3243fn assert_rust_script_producer_contract(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
3244    type ProducerContract<'a> = (&'a str, &'a [&'a str], &'a [&'a str], i64, f64);
3245    let expected: &[ProducerContract<'_>] = &[
3246        (
3247            "build.rust_scripts",
3248            &["full", "hosted-portable", "portable"],
3249            &["pre.reverie_pin"],
3250            7200,
3251            190.0,
3252        ),
3253        (
3254            "build.rust_scripts_on_host",
3255            &["hosted-privileged"],
3256            &["pre.reverie_pin_on_host"],
3257            7200,
3258            190.0,
3259        ),
3260        (
3261            "build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root",
3262            &["full", "portable"],
3263            &["pre.reverie_pin", "setup.pinned_root_fetch"],
3264            7200,
3265            190.0,
3266        ),
3267        (
3268            "quick-super-build.rust_scripts",
3269            &["quick", "super"],
3270            &["pre.reverie_pin"],
3271            crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_QUICK_SUPER_CPU_SECONDS,
3272            0.0,
3273        ),
3274        (
3275            "quick-super-build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root",
3276            &["quick", "super"],
3277            &["pre.reverie_pin", "setup.pinned_root_fetch"],
3278            crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_QUICK_SUPER_CPU_SECONDS,
3279            0.0,
3280        ),
3281    ];
3282    let expected_tags = expected
3283        .iter()
3284        .map(|(tag, ..)| (*tag).to_string())
3285        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
3286    let actual_producers = cfg
3287        .steps
3288        .iter()
3289        .filter(|step| {
3290            matches!(
3291                step.job.as_str(),
3292                "rust_scripts" | "rust_scripts_on_host" | "rust_scripts_in_pinned_root"
3293            )
3294        })
3295        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
3296    let actual_tags = actual_producers
3297        .iter()
3298        .map(|step| step.tag())
3299        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
3300    if actual_producers.len() != expected.len() || actual_tags != expected_tags {
3301        return Err(format!(
3302            "rust-script producer identity population changed: expected={} {expected_tags:?}, actual={} {actual_tags:?}",
3303            expected.len(),
3304            actual_producers.len(),
3305        ));
3306    }
3307
3308    for (tag, labels, deps, cpu_timeout, est_duration_s) in expected {
3309        let step = cfg
3310            .steps
3311            .iter()
3312            .find(|step| step.tag() == *tag)
3313            .ok_or_else(|| format!("committed DAG lost {tag}"))?;
3314        let execution_command = crate::nextest_build_selections::execution_command(step)?;
3315        let expected_command = if matches!(
3316            *tag,
3317            "build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root" | "quick-super-build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root"
3318        ) {
3319            crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_COMMAND.replacen(
3320                DAGRUN_PREPARE_COMMAND,
3321                "",
3322                1,
3323            )
3324        } else {
3325            crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_COMMAND.to_string()
3326        };
3327        let expected_labels = labels
3328            .iter()
3329            .map(|label| (*label).to_string())
3330            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
3331        let expected_deps = deps
3332            .iter()
3333            .map(|dependency| (*dependency).to_string())
3334            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
3335        let has_no_result_ownership =
3336            step.manifest.is_none() && matches!(step.result_manifests.as_deref(), Some([]));
3337        if execution_command != expected_command
3338            || step.labels != expected_labels
3339            || step.deps != expected_deps
3340            || !has_no_result_ownership
3341            || step.timeout != crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_WALL_SECONDS
3342            || step.cpu_timeout != *cpu_timeout
3343            || step.hint.est_duration_s != *est_duration_s
3344            || step.hint.rss_baseline_bytes
3345                != Some(crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_RSS_BASELINE_BYTES)
3346            || step.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes
3347                != Some(crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_HARD_MEM_MAX_BYTES)
3348            || step.hint.classification != dagrun::model::StepClass::CpuBound
3349            || step.hint.preferred_inner_jobs
3350                != Some(crate::validation_dag_static::RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_INNER_JOBS)
3351            || step.jobs_flag.as_deref() != Some("")
3352            || step.jobs_env.as_deref() != Some("CARGO_BUILD_JOBS")
3353            || step.fail_fast_family.as_deref() != Some(*tag)
3354        {
3355            return Err(format!(
3356                "{tag} changed its exact rust-script producer identity or resource contract: {step:?}"
3357            ));
3358        }
3359    }
3360    Ok(())
3361}
3362
3363fn assert_dagrun_preparation_placement(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
3364    let step = |tag: &str| {
3365        cfg.steps
3366            .iter()
3367            .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
3368            .ok_or_else(|| format!("committed DAG lost {tag}"))
3369    };
3370    for tag in [
3371        "build.rust_scripts",
3372        "quick-super-build.rust_scripts",
3373        "build.rust_scripts_on_host",
3374    ] {
3375        let command = &step(tag)?.cmd;
3376        let prepare = command.find(DAGRUN_PREPARE_COMMAND);
3377        let build = command.find("./ci/prepare-rust-scripts.sh");
3378        if command.matches(DAGRUN_PREPARE_COMMAND).count() != 1
3379            || command.matches("./ci/prepare-rust-scripts.sh").count() != 1
3380            || !matches!((prepare, build), (Some(prepare), Some(build)) if prepare < build)
3381        {
3382            return Err(format!(
3383                "{tag} must prepare dagrun exactly once before opening the rust-script Cargo build: {command}"
3384            ));
3385        }
3386    }
3387    for tag in [
3388        "build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root",
3389        "quick-super-build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root",
3390        "setup.manifest_plan",
3391        "quick-super-setup.manifest_plan",
3392        "setup.manifest_plan_on_host",
3393        "setup.manifest_plan_in_pinned_root",
3394        "quick-super-setup.manifest_plan_in_pinned_root",
3395    ] {
3396        let command = &step(tag)?.cmd;
3397        if command.contains(DAGRUN_PREPARE_COMMAND) {
3398            return Err(format!(
3399                "{tag} must not replant host dagrun preparation after the rust-script Cargo build begins: {command}"
3400            ));
3401        }
3402    }
3403    Ok(())
3404}
3405
3406/// Manifest buckets whose `test-harness run` selector omits `--allow-empty`.
3407///
3408/// A node for one of these buckets that selects no cells fails with "filters
3409/// selected no cells" instead of passing having executed nothing. c-programs
3410/// joined when the backend-parity-c bucket was folded into it
3411/// (<https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3301>): the fold moved 279
3412/// selected cells (276 portable, 3 privileged) onto its nodes, and a bucket
3413/// that large must not be able to report green on an empty selection. The
3414/// generator, the manifest DAG audit in `test-harness validate`, and the
3415/// validation driver's raw-publisher check all read this one list.
3416pub const FAIL_CLOSED_MANIFEST_BUCKETS: &[&str] = &["c-programs", "compat"];
3417
3418/// The selector flags a manifest node for `category` passes after
3419/// `--lane <lane> --category <category>`.
3420/// Manifest buckets that contain diagnostic cells. Their nodes run the
3421/// harness with `--diagnostic-results` and declare dagrun structured-result
3422/// schema 4, so a diagnostic cell's failure is reported without failing the
3423/// node; every other bucket writes and declares schema 2, and the harness
3424/// refuses to run a diagnostic cell without the flag. Empty until a bucket
3425/// with diagnostic cells exists.
3426pub const DIAGNOSTIC_MANIFEST_BUCKETS: &[&str] = &["compat"];
3427
3428/// The harness selection a manifest bucket node `tag` passes after
3429/// `test-harness run`: its lane, its category, the `--label` of the run type
3430/// it selects ([`manifest_run_type`]) and the category's
3431/// [`manifest_selector_flags`]. The generator and validate.rs's raw-census
3432/// check both read it.
3433pub fn manifest_bucket_selection(tag: &str, manifest: &DagManifest) -> String {
3434    let label = manifest_run_type(tag)
3435        .map(|label| format!(" --label {label}"))
3436        .unwrap_or_default();
3437    format!(
3438        "--lane {} --category {}{label} {}",
3439        manifest.lane,
3440        manifest.category,
3441        manifest_selector_flags(&manifest.category)
3442    )
3443}
3444
3445pub fn manifest_selector_flags(category: &str) -> &'static str {
3446    match (
3447        FAIL_CLOSED_MANIFEST_BUCKETS.contains(&category),
3448        DIAGNOSTIC_MANIFEST_BUCKETS.contains(&category),
3449    ) {
3450        (true, true) => "--ci-only --prebuilt --diagnostic-results",
3451        (true, false) => "--ci-only --prebuilt",
3452        (false, true) => "--ci-only --allow-empty --prebuilt --diagnostic-results",
3453        (false, false) => "--ci-only --allow-empty --prebuilt",
3454    }
3455}
3456
3457/// Every node of a [`FAIL_CLOSED_MANIFEST_BUCKETS`] bucket runs its exact
3458/// selector without `--allow-empty`, and every such bucket still has a node.
3459fn assert_fail_closed_manifest_selectors(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
3460    let mut covered = BTreeSet::new();
3461    for step in &cfg.steps {
3462        let Some(manifest) = step.manifest.as_ref() else {
3463            continue;
3464        };
3465        if !FAIL_CLOSED_MANIFEST_BUCKETS.contains(&manifest.category.as_str()) {
3466            continue;
3467        }
3468        let selector = format!(
3469            "target/debug/test-harness run {}",
3470            manifest_bucket_selection(&step.tag(), manifest)
3471        );
3472        if step.cmd.contains("--allow-empty") || step.cmd.matches(selector.as_str()).count() != 1 {
3473            return Err(format!(
3474                "{} must fail closed on an empty selection: it must run `{selector}` exactly once and never pass --allow-empty",
3475                step.tag()
3476            ));
3477        }
3478        covered.insert(manifest.category.as_str());
3479    }
3480    if let Some(bucket) = FAIL_CLOSED_MANIFEST_BUCKETS
3481        .iter()
3482        .find(|bucket| !covered.contains(**bucket))
3483    {
3484        return Err(format!("fail-closed manifest bucket {bucket} has no node"));
3485    }
3486    Ok(())
3487}
3488
3489fn assert_manifest_gate_width_contract(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
3490    let ordinary = cfg
3491        .steps
3492        .iter()
3493        .find(|step| step.tag() == "gate.manifest")
3494        .ok_or("committed DAG lost gate.manifest")?;
3495    let local_cpu_seconds = crate::validation_dag_static::MANIFEST_GATE_CPU_SECONDS;
3496    for (tag, wall_seconds, cpu_seconds) in [
3497        ("gate.manifest", 900, local_cpu_seconds),
3498        ("quick-super-gate.manifest", 900, local_cpu_seconds),
3499        ("gate.manifest_on_host", 180, 600),
3500    ] {
3501        let step = cfg
3502            .steps
3503            .iter()
3504            .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
3505            .ok_or_else(|| format!("committed DAG lost {tag}"))?;
3506        let expected_audit_jobs = (tag != "gate.manifest_on_host").then_some("1");
3507        if step.hint.preferred_inner_jobs
3508            != Some(crate::validation_dag_static::MANIFEST_GATE_INNER_JOBS)
3509            || step.jobs_env.as_deref() != Some("CARGO_BUILD_JOBS")
3510            || step.jobs_flag.as_deref() != Some("")
3511            || step
3512                .env
3513                .get("HERMIT_VALIDATE_AUDIT_JOBS")
3514                .map(String::as_str)
3515                != expected_audit_jobs
3516            || step.cmd != ordinary.cmd
3517            || step.timeout != wall_seconds
3518            || step.cpu_timeout != cpu_seconds
3519        {
3520            return Err(format!(
3521                "{tag} must retain the exact audit command and {wall_seconds}s wall/{cpu_seconds}s CPU caps while reserving the two-worker width and carrying every smaller admission through CARGO_BUILD_JOBS: {step:?}"
3522            ));
3523        }
3524    }
3525    Ok(())
3526}
3527
3528/// Every tool self-test runs as one leaf node in each profile that runs the
3529/// ordinary or quick/super manifest gate, after that gate, with its exact
3530/// command. Nothing may depend on these nodes: they test repository tooling,
3531/// and a product node waiting on them would put them back on its critical path.
3532fn assert_tool_self_test_nodes(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
3533    let mut expected = BTreeSet::new();
3534    for tool in TOOL_SELF_TESTS {
3535        let command = format!(
3536            "export PATH=\"$PWD/ci/rust-script-bin:$PATH\"; export HERMIT_RUST_SCRIPT_ARTIFACT_ROOT=\"$PWD/target/ci/rust-scripts\"; export HERMIT_PREBUILT_RUST_SCRIPTS_REQUIRED=1; target/debug/test-harness selftest {}",
3537            tool.name
3538        );
3539        for (tag, labels, gate) in [
3540            (
3541                format!("{TOOL_SELF_TEST_GROUP_PREFIX}{}", tool.name),
3542                &["full", HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL, "portable"][..],
3543                "gate.manifest",
3544            ),
3545            (
3546                quick_super_variant(&format!("{TOOL_SELF_TEST_GROUP_PREFIX}{}", tool.name)),
3547                &["quick", "super"][..],
3548                "quick-super-gate.manifest",
3549            ),
3550        ] {
3551            let step = cfg
3552                .steps
3553                .iter()
3554                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
3555                .ok_or_else(|| format!("committed DAG lost tool self-test node {tag}"))?;
3556            if step.cmd != command
3557                || step.labels != labels
3558                || step.deps != {
3559                    let mut deps = [gate, "pre.reverie_pin"];
3560                    deps.sort_unstable();
3561                    deps
3562                }
3563                || step.timeout <= 0
3564                || step.cpu_timeout <= 0
3565            {
3566                return Err(format!(
3567                    "{tag} must run exactly `test-harness selftest {}` after {gate} in {labels:?} with positive caps: {step:?}",
3568                    tool.name
3569                ));
3570            }
3571            expected.insert(tag);
3572        }
3573    }
3574    let actual = cfg
3575        .steps
3576        .iter()
3577        .filter(|step| {
3578            step.group == TOOL_SELF_TEST_GROUP
3579                || step.group == quick_super_variant(TOOL_SELF_TEST_GROUP)
3580        })
3581        .map(Step::tag)
3582        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
3583    if actual != expected {
3584        return Err(format!(
3585            "tool self-test nodes {actual:?} differ from TOOL_SELF_TESTS {expected:?}"
3586        ));
3587    }
3588    if let Some((step, dependency)) = cfg.steps.iter().find_map(|step| {
3589        step.deps
3590            .iter()
3591            .find(|dependency| expected.contains(*dependency))
3592            .map(|dependency| (step.tag(), dependency))
3593    }) {
3594        return Err(format!(
3595            "{step} depends on tool self-test {dependency}; self-tests must stay off every product node's critical path"
3596        ));
3597    }
3598    Ok(())
3599}
3600
3601fn critical_path_wall_seconds(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<i64, String> {
3602    let by_tag = cfg
3603        .steps
3604        .iter()
3605        .map(|step| (step.tag(), step))
3606        .collect::<BTreeMap<_, _>>();
3607    let mut longest = BTreeMap::<String, i64>::new();
3608    while longest.len() < by_tag.len() {
3609        let mut advanced = false;
3610        for (tag, step) in &by_tag {
3611            if longest.contains_key(tag) {
3612                continue;
3613            }
3614            if step
3615                .deps
3616                .iter()
3617                .any(|dependency| !by_tag.contains_key(dependency))
3618            {
3619                return Err(format!(
3620                    "{tag} names a dependency outside its selected graph"
3621                ));
3622            }
3623            if step
3624                .deps
3625                .iter()
3626                .any(|dependency| !longest.contains_key(dependency))
3627            {
3628                continue;
3629            }
3630            let predecessor = step
3631                .deps
3632                .iter()
3633                .filter_map(|dependency| longest.get(dependency))
3634                .copied()
3635                .max()
3636                .unwrap_or(0);
3637            longest.insert(tag.clone(), predecessor + step.timeout);
3638            advanced = true;
3639        }
3640        if !advanced {
3641            return Err("selected graph contains a dependency cycle".into());
3642        }
3643    }
3644    longest
3645        .values()
3646        .copied()
3647        .max()
3648        .ok_or_else(|| "selected graph is empty".to_string())
3649}
3650
3651/// Every committed node declares a wall timeout, a CPU budget (its own or the
3652/// config default) and a memory cap. A node without them would run unboxed
3653/// while scripts/validate.rs printed "cgroup boxing ACTIVE": a green that
3654/// verified less than it claimed. Checked here, where the committed graph is
3655/// made, rather than again at the start of every validation run.
3656fn assert_declared_caps(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
3657    let undeclared: Vec<String> = cfg
3658        .steps
3659        .iter()
3660        .filter(|step| {
3661            let memory =
3662                step.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes.is_some() || step.hint.rss_baseline_bytes.is_some();
3663            let cpu = step.cpu_timeout > 0 || cfg.default_step_cpu_timeout > 0;
3664            let wall = step.timeout > 0;
3665            !(memory && cpu && wall)
3666        })
3667        .map(Step::tag)
3668        .collect();
3669    if undeclared.is_empty() {
3670        Ok(())
3671    } else {
3672        Err(format!(
3673            "{} node(s) lack declared resource caps (timeout, cpu_timeout and a memory hint) \
3674             and would run unboxed: {}",
3675            undeclared.len(),
3676            undeclared.join(", ")
3677        ))
3678    }
3679}
3680
3681fn assert_m1_allocator_gate(cfg: &DagConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
3682    let find = |tag: &str| {
3683        cfg.steps
3684            .iter()
3685            .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
3686            .ok_or_else(|| format!("required M1 node missing: {tag}"))
3687    };
3688    let producer = find("build.liteinst_allocator_fixtures_in_pinned_root")?;
3689    let consumer = find("test.liteinst_allocator")?;
3690    for step in [producer, consumer] {
3691        if step.labels != ["full", "portable"]
3692            || !step.cmd.starts_with("./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh ")
3693        {
3694            return Err(format!(
3695                "{} lost required local pinned placement",
3696                step.tag()
3697            ));
3698        }
3699    }
3700    if !producer
3701        .cmd
3702        .contains("./ci/build-liteinst-allocator-fixtures.rs")
3703        || producer
3704            .deps
3705            .iter()
3706            .any(|tag| tag == "build.workspace_in_pinned_root")
3707    {
3708        return Err("M1 diagnostic producer lost its independent explicit leaf closure".into());
3709    }
3710    for required in [
3711        "build.e2e_artifact_in_pinned_root",
3712        "build.liteinst_allocator_fixtures_in_pinned_root",
3713        "pre.reverie_pin",
3714    ] {
3715        if !consumer.deps.iter().any(|tag| tag == required) {
3716            return Err(format!("M1 consumer lost prerequisite {required}"));
3717        }
3718    }
3719    if consumer
3720        .env
3721        .get("NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED")
3722        .map(String::as_str)
3723        != Some("7")
3724        || consumer.integration_test_binaries.as_deref()
3725            != Some(&["liteinst_allocator".to_owned()][..])
3726    {
3727        return Err("M1 consumer lost its seven-test target population".into());
3728    }
3729    crate::nextest_build_selections::assert_command_selection(consumer)?;
3730    let command = crate::nextest_build_selections::execution_command(consumer)?;
3731    for required in [
3732        "HERMIT_M1_ALLOCATOR_FIXTURE_BUNDLE",
3733        "--print-standalone",
