5472
5473    /// The compatibility website export refuses any text containing "/home/",
5474    /// and every finalized-proof carrier embeds the run's whole plan, so a
5475    /// node description that merely mentions that prefix breaks every site
5476    /// rebuild from a full plan. Name the offending field in the source and
5477    /// the committed DAG, then check the whole committed file, since the
5478    /// carrier holds every field. "/users/" is checked case-insensitively
5479    /// because the site's per-string owner-path pattern ignores case.
5480    #[test]
5481    fn no_dag_text_names_a_user_home_path() {
5482        fn refuse(origin: &str, field: &str, text: &str) {
5483            for needle in ["/home/", "/users/"] {
5484                let line = text
5485                    .lines()
5486                    .find(|line| line.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(needle));
5487                assert!(
5488                    line.is_none(),
5489                    "{origin}: {field} names {needle:?}: {}",
5490                    line.unwrap_or_default()
5491                );
5492            }
5493        }
5494        let text = include_str!("../../dag/validate.json");
5495        let committed = dag_from_json(text).unwrap();
5496        for (origin, dag) in [
5497            (
5498                "validation_dag_static",
5499                crate::validation_dag_static::config(),
5500            ),
5501            (OUTPUT, committed),
5502        ] {
5503            refuse(origin, "the DAG description", &dag.description);
5504            for step in &dag.steps {
5505                let tag = step.tag();
5506                refuse(origin, &format!("{tag} desc"), &step.desc);
5507                refuse(origin, &format!("{tag} description"), &step.description);
5508            }
5509        }
5510        refuse(OUTPUT, "the file", text);
5511    }
5512
5513    #[test]
5514    fn steps_sharing_a_result_file_never_run_in_one_run_type() {
5515        // e2e.manifest_compat, portablecompat.manifest_compat,
5516        // sabrecompat.manifest_compat, strictcompat.manifest_compat,
5517        // rrcompat.manifest_compat, e2e.manifest_compat_on_host and the
5518        // full-buck-e2e import twin e2e.manifest_compat_buck all write
5519        // $E2E_RESULT_ROOT/portable/manifest_compat/results.jsonl. That is safe
5520        // only while no run type selects two of them: a run selects one label,
5521        // so their label sets must be non-empty and pairwise disjoint.
5522        let dag = generated_dag();
5523        let mut writers: BTreeMap<String, Vec<&Step>> = BTreeMap::new();
5524        for step in &dag.steps {
5525            for flag in ["--results", "--junit"] {
5526                if let Some((_, rest)) = step.cmd.split_once(&format!("{flag} \"")) {
5527                    let path = rest.split('"').next().unwrap().to_string();
5528                    writers.entry(path).or_default().push(step);
5529                }
5530            }
5531        }
5532        let shared = writers
5533            .get("$E2E_RESULT_ROOT/portable/manifest_compat/results.jsonl")
5534            .map_or(0, Vec::len);
5535        assert_eq!(
5536            shared, 7,
5537            "the six manifest_compat buckets and the Buck import twin share one result file"
5538        );
5539        for (path, steps) in &writers {
5540            for (index, first) in steps.iter().enumerate() {
5541                for second in &steps[index + 1..] {
5542                    assert!(
5543                        !first.labels.is_empty()
5544                            && !second.labels.is_empty()
5545                            && !first
5546                                .labels
5547                                .iter()
5548                                .any(|label| second.labels.contains(label)),
5549                        "{} and {} both write {path} and can run in one run type ({:?} vs {:?})",
5550                        first.tag(),
5551                        second.tag(),
5552                        first.labels,
5553                        second.labels
5554                    );
5555                }
5556            }
5557        }
5558    }
5559
5560    #[test]
5561    fn new_plans_never_request_a_ptrace_parity_reference() {
5562        let root = crate::git_environment::checkout_root();
5563        let dag = generated_dag();
5564        assert_eq!(
5565            dag.steps
5566                .iter()
5567                .filter(|step| step.cmd.contains("--parity-reference"))
5568                .map(|step| step.tag())
5569                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
5570            Vec::<String>::new(),
5571            "https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3301 removed the ptrace reference run from every generated step"
5572        );
5573        // The former parity selectors were folded into the c-programs pair
5574        // (slice S6 of https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3301). That
5575        // pair keeps its width and resources, carries no reference flag, and
5576        // fails closed on an empty selection.
5577        for retired in [
5578            "e2e.manifest_backend_parity_c",
5579            "e2e.manifest_backend_parity_c_on_host",
5580        ] {
5581            assert!(
5582                dag.steps.iter().all(|step| step.tag() != retired),
5583                "{retired} must stay folded into c-programs"
5584            );
5585        }
5586        // The hosted selector also omits KVM, liteinst and in-guest-trap
5587        // cells: GitHub-hosted runners have no PMU and no CPUID faulting
5588        // (HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS).
5589        let selectors = [
5590            (
5591                "e2e.manifest_c_programs",
5592                "--category c-programs --ci-only --prebuilt --results",
5593            ),
5594            (
5595                "e2e.manifest_c_programs_on_host",
5596                "--category c-programs --ci-only --prebuilt --exclude-backend kvm --exclude-backend liteinst --exclude-backend in-guest-trap --results",
5597            ),
5598        ];
5599        for (tag, selector_argv) in selectors {
5600            let step = dag.steps.iter().find(|step| step.tag() == tag).unwrap();
5601            assert!(step.cmd.contains(selector_argv), "{tag}");
5602            assert!(!step.cmd.contains("--allow-empty"));
5603            assert_eq!(step.jobs_flag.as_deref(), Some("--jobs"));
5604            assert_eq!(step.hint.preferred_inner_jobs, Some(8));
5605            let selector = step.manifest.as_ref().unwrap();
5606            assert_eq!(selector.lane, "portable");
5607            assert_eq!(selector.category, "c-programs");
5608            assert_eq!(selector.test, None);
5609            assert_eq!(selector.mode, None);
5610            assert_eq!(selector.backend, None);
5611            assert_eq!(step.hint.resources.get("manifest_guest"), Some(&8));
5612            assert!(!step.cmd.contains("--probe-disabled"));
5613        }
5614        // A planted reference flag on either selector is refused by the
5615        // generator's own invariants, with the reason named.
5616        let cells = expected_cells(&root).unwrap();
5617        assert_invariants(&dag, &cells).unwrap();
5618        for (tag, _) in selectors {
5619            let mut planted = dag.clone();
5620            let step = planted
5621                .steps
5622                .iter_mut()
5623                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
5624                .unwrap();
5625            assert_eq!(step.cmd.matches(" --results ").count(), 1);
5626            step.cmd = step
5627                .cmd
5628                .replace(" --results ", " --parity-reference ptrace --results ");
5629            assert_eq!(
5630                assert_invariants(&planted, &cells).unwrap_err(),
5631                format!(
5632                    "{tag} passes --parity-reference; backend parity no longer decides a validation outcome (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3301)"
5633                )
5634            );
5635        }
5636    }
5637
5638    /// GitHub-hosted runners have no PMU and no CPUID faulting, so the
5639    /// hosted-portable profile omits KVM, liteinst and in-guest-trap cells from
5640    /// every command, owned result, and expected population, while the local
5641    /// profiles keep requiring them.
5642    #[test]
5643    fn hosted_portable_omits_the_excluded_backends_everywhere_and_locally_keeps_them() {
5644        assert_eq!(
5645            HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS,
5646            ["kvm", "liteinst", "in-guest-trap"]
5647        );
5648        let flags = hosted_portable_exclusion_flags();
5649        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
5650        let cells = expected_cells(&crate::git_environment::checkout_root()).unwrap();
5651        let excluded = |cell: &DagManifest| hosted_portable_excludes(cell);
5652        let portable = expected_for_label("portable", &cells);
5653        let hosted = expected_for_label(HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL, &cells);
5654        let omitted = portable.iter().filter(|cell| excluded(cell)).count();
5655        assert!(omitted > 0, "the corpus has no portable cell to omit");
5656        // Each excluded backend has portable cells, so no part of the
5657        // exclusion is vacuous.
5658        for backend in HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS {
5659            assert!(
5660                portable
5661                    .iter()
5662                    .any(|cell| cell.backend.as_deref() == Some(*backend)),
5663                "the corpus has no portable {backend} cell to omit"
5664            );
5665        }
5666        assert_eq!(hosted.len() + omitted, portable.len());
5667        assert!(hosted.iter().all(|cell| !excluded(cell)));
5668        assert!(
5669            expected_for_label("full", &cells)
5670                .iter()
5671                .any(|cell| excluded(cell)),
5672            "the local full profile must still require the excluded backends' cells"
5673        );
5674
5675        let owned = |step: &Step| {
5676            step.effective_result_manifests()
5677                .iter()
5678                .cloned()
5679                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
5680        };
5681        let mut hosted_owned = 0;
5682        let mut local_omitted = 0;
5683        for step in committed
5684            .steps
5685            .iter()
5686            .filter(|step| step.manifest.is_some() && step.labels == [HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL])
5687        {
5688            let category = &step.manifest.as_ref().unwrap().category;
5689            assert!(
5690                step.cmd
5691                    .contains(&format!("{}{flags} ", manifest_selector_flags(category))),
5692                "{}: {}",
5693                step.tag(),
5694                step.cmd
5695            );
5696            assert_eq!(
5697                step.cmd.matches("--exclude-backend").count(),
5698                HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS.len(),
5699                "{}: {}",
5700                step.tag(),
5701                step.cmd
5702            );
5703            let local_tag = step
5704                .tag()
5705                .strip_suffix(HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX)
5706                .unwrap()
5707                .to_string();
5708            let local = committed
5709                .steps
5710                .iter()
5711                .find(|candidate| candidate.tag() == local_tag)
5712                .unwrap();
5713            assert!(!local.cmd.contains("--exclude-backend"), "{local_tag}");
5714            let hosted_cells = owned(step);
5715            let local_cells = owned(local);
5716            assert!(
5717                hosted_cells.iter().all(|cell| !excluded(cell)),
5718                "{}",
5719                step.tag()
5720            );
5721            assert_eq!(
5722                hosted_cells,
5723                local_cells
5724                    .iter()
5725                    .filter(|cell| !excluded(cell))
5726                    .cloned()
5727                    .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
5728                "{}",
5729                step.tag()
5730            );
5731            hosted_owned += hosted_cells.len();
5732            local_omitted += local_cells.len() - hosted_cells.len();
5733        }
5734        assert_eq!(hosted_owned, hosted.len());
5735        assert_eq!(local_omitted, omitted);
5736        let scorecard = |tag: &str| {
5737            committed
5738                .steps
5739                .iter()
5740                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
5741                .unwrap()
5742                .cmd
5743                .clone()
5744        };
5745        assert!(
5746            scorecard("scorecard.compatibility_on_host").ends_with(&format!(
5747                "verify-results --results \"$E2E_RESULT_ROOT\" --lanes portable{flags}"
5748            ))
5749        );
5750        assert!(scorecard("scorecard.compatibility").ends_with("--lanes portable"));
5751
5752        let mut planted = committed.clone();
5753        let step = planted
5754            .steps
5755            .iter_mut()
5756            .find(|step| step.tag() == "e2e.manifest_c_programs_on_host")
5757            .unwrap();
5758        step.cmd = step.cmd.replace(&flags, "");
5759        assert_eq!(
5760            assert_invariants(&planted, &cells).unwrap_err(),
5761            "hosted-portable step(s) do not carry the backend exclusion `--exclude-backend kvm --exclude-backend liteinst --exclude-backend in-guest-trap` exactly once: e2e.manifest_c_programs_on_host"
5762        );
5763        let mut planted = committed.clone();
5764        let step = planted
5765            .steps
5766            .iter_mut()
5767            .find(|step| step.tag() == "scorecard.compatibility_on_host")
5768            .unwrap();
5769        step.cmd = step.cmd.replace(&flags, "");
5770        assert_eq!(
5771            assert_invariants(&planted, &cells).unwrap_err(),
5772            "hosted-portable step(s) do not carry the backend exclusion `--exclude-backend kvm --exclude-backend liteinst --exclude-backend in-guest-trap` exactly once: scorecard.compatibility_on_host"
5773        );
5774
5775        // A repeated exclusion still contains the anchored substring, but
5776        // `test-harness` refuses it ("--exclude-backend kvm was given twice"),
5777        // so the step would fail when run. The invariant counts the flag.
5778        for (tag, anchor) in [
5779            (
5780                "e2e.manifest_bin_c_on_host",
5781                "--prebuilt --exclude-backend kvm --exclude-backend liteinst --exclude-backend in-guest-trap",
5782            ),
5783            (
5784                "scorecard.compatibility_on_host",
5785                "--lanes portable --exclude-backend kvm --exclude-backend liteinst --exclude-backend in-guest-trap",
5786            ),
5787        ] {
5788            let mut planted = committed.clone();
5789            let step = planted
5790                .steps
5791                .iter_mut()
5792                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
5793                .unwrap();
5794            assert_eq!(step.cmd.matches(anchor).count(), 1, "{tag}");
5795            step.cmd = step
5796                .cmd
5797                .replace(anchor, &format!("{anchor} --exclude-backend kvm"));
5798            assert_eq!(
5799                step.cmd.matches("--exclude-backend kvm").count(),
5800                2,
5801                "{tag}"
5802            );
5803            assert_eq!(
5804                assert_invariants(&planted, &cells).unwrap_err(),
5805                format!(
5806                    "hosted-portable step(s) do not carry the backend exclusion `--exclude-backend kvm --exclude-backend liteinst --exclude-backend in-guest-trap` exactly once: {tag}"
5807                )
5808            );
5809        }
5810
5811        // The shell consumers of ci/expected-e2e-plan.json apply the same list.
5812        let filter = format!(
5813            "select(.lane == \"portable\"{})",
5814            HOSTED_PORTABLE_EXCLUDED_BACKENDS
5815                .iter()
5816                .map(|backend| format!(" and .backend != \"{backend}\""))
5817                .collect::<String>()
5818        );
5819        for (path, text) in [
5820            (
5821                ".github/workflows/ci-portable.yml",
5822                include_str!("../../../.github/workflows/ci-portable.yml"),
5823            ),
5824            (
5825                "ci/check-shard-coverage.sh",
5826                include_str!("../../check-shard-coverage.sh"),
5827            ),
5828            (
5829                "ci/hermetic/run-split-validate.sh",
5830                include_str!("../../hermetic/run-split-validate.sh"),
5831            ),
5832        ] {
5833            assert_eq!(
5834                text.matches(&filter).count(),
5835                1,
5836                "{path} must apply `{filter}` once"
5837            );
5838            assert!(
5839                !text.contains("[.cells[] | select(.lane == \"portable\")]")
5840                    && !text.contains("[.cells[] | select(.lane == \"portable\")\n"),
5841                "{path} still counts the unfiltered portable population"
5842            );
5843        }
5844    }

6105    #[test]
6106    fn hosted_nextest_selections_keep_local_cases_and_failure_limits() {
6107        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6108        let mut checked = BTreeSet::new();
6109        for hosted in committed.steps.iter().filter(|step| {
6110            step.group == "test"
6111                && step.job.ends_with(HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX)
6112                && step.env.contains_key("NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED")
6113        }) {
6114            let local_tag = hosted
6115                .tag()
6116                .strip_suffix(HOSTED_VARIANT_SUFFIX)
6117                .unwrap()
6118                .to_owned();
6119            checked.insert(local_tag.clone());
6120            let local = committed
6121                .steps
6122                .iter()
6123                .find(|step| step.tag() == local_tag)
6124                .unwrap();
6125            let local_payload = if local
6126                .cmd
6127                .starts_with("./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh ")
6128            {
6129                let argv = shell_words::split(&local.cmd).unwrap();
6130                let boundary = argv.iter().position(|arg| arg == "--").unwrap();
6131                assert_eq!(argv[boundary + 3], PINNED_ROOT_COMMAND_GUARD);
6132                argv[boundary + 5].clone()
6133            } else {
6134                local.cmd.clone()
6135            };
6136            let expected_host_payload = if local_tag == "test.regular_crates" {
6137                const PINNED: &str = "--calibration-launch-proof /run/hermit-nextest-launch.json";
6138                assert_eq!(local_payload.matches(PINNED).count(), 1);
6139                assert_eq!(hosted.cmd.matches("--calibration-host").count(), 1);
6140                assert!(local.deps.iter().any(|dep| dep == "build.rust_scripts"));
6141                local_payload.replace(PINNED, "--calibration-host")
6142            } else if local_tag == "test.cli" {
6143                // The hosted twin excludes exactly the CPUID-faulting cases,
6144                // by exact name, which the local selection must keep running:
6145                // no local filter names one, and no local substring --skip
6146                // matches one.
6147                let local_argv = shell_words::split(&local_payload).unwrap();
6148                assert!(!local_argv.iter().any(|arg| arg == "-E"));
6149                for name in HOSTED_PORTABLE_CPUID_FAULTING_CLI_TESTS {
6150                    assert!(
6151                        !local_payload.contains(name),
6152                        "test.cli must keep running {name}"
6153                    );
6154                    for pattern in local_argv
6155                        .windows(2)
6156                        .filter(|pair| pair[0] == "--skip")
6157                        .map(|pair| &pair[1])
6158                    {
6159                        assert!(
6160                            !name.contains(pattern.as_str()),
6161                            "test.cli's --skip {pattern} skips {name}"
6162                        );
6163                    }
6164                }
6165                let exclusions = HOSTED_PORTABLE_CPUID_FAULTING_CLI_TESTS
6166                    .iter()
6167                    .map(|name| format!("test(={name})"))
6168                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
6169                    .join(" | ");
6170                assert_eq!(local_payload.matches(" -- ").count(), 1);
6171                local_payload.replacen(" -- ", &format!(" -E 'not ({exclusions})' -- "), 1)
6172            } else {
6173                local_payload
6174            };
6175            assert_eq!(
6176                hosted.cmd,
6177                expected_host_payload,
6178                "{} changed its test selection",
6179                hosted.tag()
6180            );
6181            assert_eq!(
6182                hosted.timeout,
6183                local.timeout,
6184                "{} changed its timeout",
6185                hosted.tag()
6186            );
6187            assert_eq!(
6188                hosted.cpu_timeout,
6189                local.cpu_timeout,
6190                "{} changed its CPU timeout",
6191                hosted.tag()
6192            );
6193            assert_eq!(
6194                hosted.env.get(crate::nextest_binaries::SELECTION_ENV),
6195                local.env.get(crate::nextest_binaries::SELECTION_ENV),
6196                "{} changed {}",
6197                hosted.tag(),
6198                crate::nextest_binaries::SELECTION_ENV
6199            );
6200            // Both counts are measured by listing. Each exact test(=NAME) term
6201            // excludes at most one test, so the hosted twin executes exactly
6202            // that many fewer only if every listed name exists and nothing
6203            // else is excluded.
6204            let hosted_only_skips = if local_tag == "test.cli" {
6205                HOSTED_PORTABLE_CPUID_FAULTING_CLI_TESTS.len()
6206            } else {
6207                0
6208            };
6209            let count =
6210                |step: &Step| -> usize { step.env["NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED"].parse().unwrap() };
6211            assert_eq!(
6212                count(hosted) + hosted_only_skips,
6213                count(local),
6214                "{} changed NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED",
6215                hosted.tag()
6216            );
6217        }
6218        assert_eq!(
6219            checked,
6220            BTreeSet::from(
6221                [
6222                    "test.app_strict_verify",
6223                    "test.arbitrary_binaries",
6224                    "test.cli",
6225                    "test.command_strict_verify",
6226                    "test.detcore_misc",
6227                    "test.detcore_parallel",
6228                    "test.detcore_time",
6229                    "test.detcore_unit",
6230                    "test.hermit_integration",
6231                    "test.hermit_modes",
6232                    "test.hermit_unit",
6233                    "test.ignored_syscall_regressions",
6234                    "test.regular_crates",
6235                    "test.rr_suite_contract",
6236                    "test.sabre_examples",
6237                ]
6238                .map(str::to_owned)
6239            ),
6240            "every hosted Nextest selection must be compared to its local selection"
6241        );
6242        let local = committed
6243            .steps
6244            .iter()
6245            .find(|step| step.tag() == "e2e.manifest_c_programs")
6246            .unwrap();
6247        let hosted = committed
6248            .steps
6249            .iter()
6250            .find(|step| step.tag() == "e2e.manifest_c_programs_on_host")
6251            .unwrap();
6252        // 900 s covers the 104 folded backend-parity-c tests (slice S6 of
6253        // https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3301); the two buckets
6254        // measured 128.62 s and 75.76 s of wall time in one run.
6255        assert_eq!(local.timeout, 900);
6256        assert_eq!(hosted.timeout, local.timeout);
6257    }

6259    #[test]
6260    fn committed_buck_e2e_selection_replaces_22_nodes_with_18() {
6261        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6262        let cells = expected_cells(&crate::git_environment::checkout_root()).unwrap();
6263        assert_buck_e2e_selection(&committed, &cells).unwrap();
6264        // The name predates e2e.buck_stage: 19 full-buck-e2e nodes since
6265        // the stage left e2e.buck_cells, 18 before.
6266        // 90 full nodes (89 before build.workspace_compile_in_pinned_root
6267        // split the Cargo half out of build.workspace_in_pinned_root, 88
6268        // before test.record_replay joined full, 89 before
6269        // test.liteinst_strict was retired with the LiteInst host hybrid,
6270        // https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3520; 87 before
6271        // test.detcore_time joined full, 88 before
6272        // privileged-test.pmu_detcore_time_cases did) - 22 replaced + 19
6273        // full-buck-e2e nodes (18, and 86 in all, before e2e.buck_stage).
6274        // 88 since test.pmu_integration_cases joined full (91 full
6275        // nodes). 90 since build.liteinst_allocator_fixtures and
6276        // test.liteinst_allocator joined full (93 full nodes).
6277        // 91 with the additive required M2 stack node (94 full nodes).
6278        assert_eq!(buck_e2e_selection(&committed).unwrap().steps.len(), 91);
6279
6280        fn twin(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> &mut Step {
6281            cfg.steps
6282                .iter_mut()
6283                .find(|step| step.tag() == "e2e.manifest_c_programs_buck")
6284                .unwrap()
6285        }
6286        let mut wrapped = committed.clone();
6287        let cmd = twin(&mut wrapped).cmd.clone();
6288        twin(&mut wrapped).cmd = format!("./ci/hermetic/run-in-pinned-root.sh -- {cmd}");
6289        let error = assert_buck_e2e_selection(&wrapped, &cells).unwrap_err();
6290        assert!(error.contains("not a host-side import"), "{error}");
6291
6292        let mut unlabelled = committed.clone();
6293        twin(&mut unlabelled).labels.clear();
6294        let error = assert_buck_e2e_selection(&unlabelled, &cells).unwrap_err();
6295        assert!(error.contains("Buck E2E"), "{error}");
6296    }
6297
6298    /// ci-hub's whole-run budget for a validation
6299    /// (`hermit_run_timeout_seconds` in dev-hermit
6300    /// ci-hub/validate/start_unit.py, for its 4800 s lock child deadline).
6301    /// It does not depend on the selection: a quick, envelope-only or
6302    /// qemu-l2-only run gets the same 4200 s as a full one.
6303    const CI_HUB_RUN_BUDGET_S: i64 = 4200;
6304    /// Preparation every committed selection must survive: scripts/validate.rs
6305    /// anchors the run epoch before locks, freshness checks, plan construction
6306    /// and the cgroup re-exec, and hands dagrun only what is left, while a
6307    /// committed selection's node budgets are never clamped to it. Measured
6308    /// preparation on one development host: 65 s and 134 s typically, 734 s at
6309    /// worst (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3896).
6310    const PREPARATION_RESERVE_S: i64 = 1200;
6311
6312    #[test]
6313    fn every_committed_node_declares_its_caps_and_an_uncapped_one_is_refused() {
6314        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6315        assert_declared_caps(&committed).unwrap();
6316        for (what, edit) in [
6317            (
6318                "wall",
6319                &(|step: &mut Step| step.timeout = 0) as &dyn Fn(&mut Step),
6320            ),
6321            ("memory", &|step: &mut Step| {
6322                step.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes = None;
6323                step.hint.rss_baseline_bytes = None;
6324            }),
6325        ] {
6326            let mut broken = committed.clone();
6327            edit(&mut broken.steps[0]);
6328            let error = assert_declared_caps(&broken).unwrap_err();
6329            assert!(
6330                error.contains(&broken.steps[0].tag()) && error.contains("would run unboxed"),
6331                "{what}: {error}"
6332            );
6333        }
6334        let mut no_cpu = committed.clone();
6335        no_cpu.default_step_cpu_timeout = 0;
6336        no_cpu.steps[0].cpu_timeout = 0;
6337        assert!(assert_declared_caps(&no_cpu).is_err());
6338    }
6339
6340    #[test]
6341    fn committed_selections_survive_a_slow_preparation() {
6342        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6343        let labels: BTreeSet<&str> = committed
6344            .steps
6345            .iter()
6346            .flat_map(|step| step.labels.iter().map(String::as_str))
6347            .collect();
6348        let mut selections = vec![(
6349            "full with the Buck runner".to_string(),
6350            buck_e2e_selection(&committed).unwrap(),
6351        )];
6352        for label in &labels {
6353            selections.push((
6354                format!("label {label}"),
6355                select_steps_by_labels(&committed, &[label.to_string()]).unwrap(),
6356            ));
6357        }
6358        // The three focused profiles that carried a 3600 s build node
6359        // (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3906) must stay covered.
6360        for label in ["full", "quick", "envelope-only", "qemu-l2-only"] {
6361            assert!(
6362                labels.contains(label),
6363                "the committed DAG lost label {label}"
6364            );
6365        }
6366        for (name, selection) in &selections {
6367            // The refusal of https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3896:
6368            // 734 s of preparation left 3466 s, and dagrun refused every node
6369            // because e2e.buck_cells declared 3600 s.
6370            let after_734 = dagrun::scheduler::steps_violating_run_timeout(
6371                selection,
6372                CI_HUB_RUN_BUDGET_S - 734,
6373            );
6374            assert!(
6375                after_734.is_empty(),
6376                "{name}: after 734 s of preparation dagrun refuses {after_734:?}"
6377            );
6378            let after_reserve = dagrun::scheduler::steps_violating_run_timeout(
6379                selection,
6380                CI_HUB_RUN_BUDGET_S - PREPARATION_RESERVE_S,
6381            );
6382            assert!(
6383                after_reserve.is_empty(),
6384                "{name}: these committed nodes leave less than {PREPARATION_RESERVE_S} s of the \
6385                 {CI_HUB_RUN_BUDGET_S} s run budget for preparation, so a slow preparation \
6386                 makes dagrun refuse the whole run: {after_reserve:?}"
6387            );
6388        }
6389    }

6391    #[test]
6392    fn buck_cells_hand_validate_node_their_wall_bound() {
6393        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6394        let cells = expected_cells(&crate::git_environment::checkout_root()).unwrap();
6395        fn cells_node(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> &mut Step {
6396            cfg.steps
6397                .iter_mut()
6398                .find(|step| step.tag() == BUCK_CELLS_TAG)
6399                .unwrap()
6400        }
6401        let mut committed_cells = committed.clone();
6402        let node = cells_node(&mut committed_cells);
6403        assert_eq!(node.timeout, 2400);
6404        assert_eq!(
6405            node.env.get(BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV).map(String::as_str),
6406            Some("2400")
6407        );
6408
6409        let mut missing = committed.clone();
6410        cells_node(&mut missing).env.remove(BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV);
6411        let mut longer = committed.clone();
6412        cells_node(&mut longer)
6413            .env
6414            .insert(BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV.into(), "2401".into());
6415        let mut shortened = committed.clone();
6416        cells_node(&mut shortened).timeout = 900;
6417        let mut unbounded = committed.clone();
6418        cells_node(&mut unbounded).timeout = 0;
6419        cells_node(&mut unbounded)
6420            .env
6421            .insert(BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV.into(), "0".into());
6422        for planted in [missing, longer, shortened, unbounded] {
6423            let error = assert_buck_e2e_selection(&planted, &cells).unwrap_err();
6424            assert!(error.contains(BUCK_STEP_WALL_ENV), "{error}");
6425        }
6426    }

6428    #[test]
6429    fn c_programs_nodes_refuse_an_empty_selection() {
6430        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6431        let cells = expected_cells(&crate::git_environment::checkout_root()).unwrap();
6432        assert_invariants(&committed, &cells).unwrap();
6433        let c_programs = committed
6434            .steps
6435            .iter()
6436            .filter(|step| {
6437                step.manifest
6438                    .as_ref()
6439                    .is_some_and(|manifest| manifest.category == "c-programs")
6440            })
6441            .map(|step| step.tag())
6442            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
6443        assert_eq!(
6444            c_programs,
6445            [
6446                "e2e.manifest_c_programs",
6447                "privileged-e2e.manifest_c_programs",
6448                "privileged-only-e2e.manifest_c_programs",
6449                "e2e.manifest_c_programs_on_host",
6450                "privileged-only-e2e.manifest_c_programs_on_host",
6451                // The full-buck-e2e import twins of the two full buckets.
6452                "e2e.manifest_c_programs_buck",
6453                "privileged-e2e.manifest_c_programs_buck",
6454            ]
6455            .map(str::to_owned)
6456        );
6457        assert_eq!(
6458            manifest_selector_flags("c-programs"),
6459            "--ci-only --prebuilt"
6460        );
6461        assert_eq!(
6462            manifest_selector_flags("bin-c"),
6463            "--ci-only --allow-empty --prebuilt"
6464        );
6465        for tag in &c_programs {
6466            let mut planted = committed.clone();
6467            let step = planted
6468                .steps
6469                .iter_mut()
6470                .find(|step| &step.tag() == tag)
6471                .unwrap();
6472            assert!(!step.cmd.contains("--allow-empty"), "{tag}");
6473            assert_eq!(step.cmd.matches("--ci-only --prebuilt").count(), 1);
6474            step.cmd = step
6475                .cmd
6476                .replace("--ci-only --prebuilt", "--ci-only --allow-empty --prebuilt");
6477            let error = assert_fail_closed_manifest_selectors(&planted).unwrap_err();
6478            assert!(
6479                error.starts_with(&format!(
6480                    "{tag} must fail closed on an empty selection: it must run `target/debug/test-harness run --lane "
6481                )) && error.ends_with("--category c-programs --ci-only --prebuilt` exactly once and never pass --allow-empty"),
6482                "{error}"
6483            );
6484        }
6485        let mut dropped = committed.clone();
6486        dropped.steps.retain(|step| {
6487            step.manifest
6488                .as_ref()
6489                .is_none_or(|manifest| manifest.category != "c-programs")
6490        });
6491        assert_eq!(
6492            assert_fail_closed_manifest_selectors(&dropped).unwrap_err(),
6493            "fail-closed manifest bucket c-programs has no node"
6494        );
6495    }

6523    #[test]
6524    fn rust_script_producers_retain_exact_identity_and_measured_resources() {
6525        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6526        assert_rust_script_producer_contract(&committed).unwrap();
6527        let tags = [
6528            "build.rust_scripts",
6529            "build.rust_scripts_on_host",
6530            "build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root",
6531            "quick-super-build.rust_scripts",
6532            "quick-super-build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root",
6533        ];
6534        type ResourceMutation = fn(&mut Step, &str);
6535        let old_resource_mutations: [(&str, ResourceMutation); 3] = [
6536            ("wall", |step, _| step.timeout = 300),
6537            ("baseline", |step, tag| {
6538                step.hint.rss_baseline_bytes = Some(if tag.starts_with("quick-super-") {
6539                    2 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
6540                } else {
6541                    1024 * 1024 * 1024
6542                })
6543            }),
6544            ("hard cap", |step, _| {
6545                step.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes = Some(2 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024)
6546            }),
6547        ];
6548        for tag in tags {
6549            for (name, mutate) in old_resource_mutations {
6550                let mut changed = committed.clone();
6551                let step = changed
6552                    .steps
6553                    .iter_mut()
6554                    .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
6555                    .unwrap();
6556                mutate(step, tag);
6557                let error = assert_rust_script_producer_contract(&changed).unwrap_err();
6558                assert!(error.contains(tag), "{name} mutation: {error}");
6559            }
6560        }
6561
6562        let mut changed_command = committed.clone();
6563        changed_command
6564            .steps
6565            .iter_mut()
6566            .find(|step| step.tag() == "build.rust_scripts")
6567            .unwrap()
6568            .cmd
6569            .push_str(" --planted");
6570        assert!(
6571            assert_rust_script_producer_contract(&changed_command)
6572                .unwrap_err()
6573                .contains("build.rust_scripts")
6574        );
6575
6576        let mut changed_deps = committed.clone();
6577        changed_deps
6578            .steps
6579            .iter_mut()
6580            .find(|step| step.tag() == "build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root")
6581            .unwrap()
6582            .deps
6583            .clear();
6584        assert!(
6585            assert_rust_script_producer_contract(&changed_deps)
6586                .unwrap_err()
6587                .contains("build.rust_scripts_in_pinned_root")
6588        );
6589
6590        let mut changed_ownership = committed.clone();
6591        changed_ownership
6592            .steps
6593            .iter_mut()
6594            .find(|step| step.tag() == "build.rust_scripts_on_host")
6595            .unwrap()
6596            .result_manifests = None;
6597        assert!(
6598            assert_rust_script_producer_contract(&changed_ownership)
6599                .unwrap_err()
6600                .contains("build.rust_scripts_on_host")
6601        );
6602
6603        let mut changed_population = committed;
6604        changed_population
6605            .steps
6606            .retain(|step| step.tag() != "quick-super-build.rust_scripts");
6607        assert!(
6608            assert_rust_script_producer_contract(&changed_population)
6609                .unwrap_err()
6610                .contains("identity population")
6611        );
6612    }

6669    #[test]
6670    fn tool_self_test_guard_refuses_each_planted_drift() {
6671        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6672        assert_tool_self_test_nodes(&committed).unwrap();
6673        let step_mut = |cfg: &mut DagConfig, tag: &str| -> usize {
6674            cfg.steps.iter().position(|step| step.tag() == tag).unwrap()
6675        };
6676        let refused = |mutate: &dyn Fn(&mut DagConfig), expected: &[&str]| {
6677            let mut planted = committed.clone();
6678            mutate(&mut planted);
6679            let error = assert_tool_self_test_nodes(&planted).unwrap_err();
6680            for fragment in expected {
6681                assert!(
6682                    error.contains(fragment),
6683                    "{fragment:?} missing from {error}"
6684                );
6685            }
6686        };
6687        // A product node that waits on a self-test puts it back on the
6688        // critical path, which is the defect this layout removed.
6689        refused(
6690            &|cfg| {
6691                let index = step_mut(cfg, "build.workspace_in_pinned_root");
6692                cfg.steps[index].deps.push("selftest.scorecard".into());
6693            },
6694            &[
6695                "build.workspace_in_pinned_root depends on tool self-test selftest.scorecard",
6696                "critical path",
6697            ],
6698        );
6699        refused(
6700            &|cfg| {
6701                let index = step_mut(cfg, "selftest.pressure_test");
6702                cfg.steps.remove(index);
6703            },
6704            &["lost tool self-test node selftest.pressure_test"],
6705        );
6706        refused(
6707            &|cfg| {
6708                let index = step_mut(cfg, "selftest.validate_rs");
6709                cfg.steps[index].labels.retain(|label| label != "portable");
6710            },
6711            &["selftest.validate_rs must run exactly"],
6712        );
6713        refused(
6714            &|cfg| {
6715                let index = step_mut(cfg, "quick-super-selftest.dbt_budget");
6716                cfg.steps[index].cmd = "true".into();
6717            },
6718            &["quick-super-selftest.dbt_budget must run exactly"],
6719        );
6720        refused(
6721            &|cfg| {
6722                let index = step_mut(cfg, "selftest.scorecard");
6723                cfg.steps[index].cpu_timeout = 0;
6724            },
6725            &["selftest.scorecard must run exactly", "positive caps"],
6726        );
6727        refused(
6728            &|cfg| {
6729                let index = step_mut(cfg, "selftest.manifest_cli");
6730                let mut extra = cfg.steps[index].clone();
6731                extra.job = "bogus".into();
6732                cfg.steps.push(extra);
6733            },
6734            &["selftest.bogus", "differ from TOOL_SELF_TESTS"],
6735        );
6736    }

6738    #[test]
6739    fn manifest_gate_carries_a_one_core_admission_over_inherited_build_width() {
6740        use dagrun::model::command_with_inner_jobs;
6741        use dagrun::model::env_with_inner_jobs;
6742
6743        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6744        assert_manifest_gate_width_contract(&committed).unwrap();
6745        for tag in [
6746            "gate.manifest",
6747            "quick-super-gate.manifest",
6748            "gate.manifest_on_host",
6749        ] {
6750            let step = committed
6751                .steps
6752                .iter()
6753                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
6754                .unwrap();
6755            assert_eq!(
6756                env_with_inner_jobs(step, "", Some(2)),
6757                Some(("CARGO_BUILD_JOBS".into(), "2".into())),
6758                "{tag} must expose both admitted workers on an unconstrained host"
6759            );
6760            assert_eq!(
6761                env_with_inner_jobs(step, "", Some(1)),
6762                Some(("CARGO_BUILD_JOBS".into(), "1".into())),
6763                "{tag} must replace an inherited CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=4 when admitted to one core"
6764            );
6765            assert_eq!(
6766                command_with_inner_jobs(step, "-j", Some(2)),
6767                step.cmd,
6768                "{tag} must suppress dagrun's default -j argument and preserve the exact test-harness command"
6769            );
6770            assert_eq!(
6771                command_with_inner_jobs(step, "-j", Some(1)),
6772                step.cmd,
6773                "{tag} must preserve the exact test-harness command at a reduced admission"
6774            );
6775
6776            let mut unbound = step.clone();
6777            unbound.jobs_env = None;
6778            let inherited = ("CARGO_BUILD_JOBS".to_string(), "4".to_string());
6779            let opponent = env_with_inner_jobs(&unbound, "", Some(1)).unwrap_or(inherited);
6780            assert_eq!(
6781                opponent,
6782                ("CARGO_BUILD_JOBS".into(), "4".into()),
6783                "the opponent must preserve the RUN1902 oversubscription mechanism"
6784            );
6785            let mut broken = committed.clone();
6786            broken
6787                .steps
6788                .iter_mut()
6789                .find(|candidate| candidate.tag() == tag)
6790                .unwrap()
6791                .jobs_env = None;
6792            assert!(
6793                assert_manifest_gate_width_contract(&broken)
6794                    .unwrap_err()
6795                    .contains(tag)
6796            );
6797
6798            // The local gates carry MANIFEST_GATE_CPU_SECONDS; the
6799            // hosted-privileged variant keeps 600. Swapping either value fails.
6800            let mut recapped = committed.clone();
6801            recapped
6802                .steps
6803                .iter_mut()
6804                .find(|candidate| candidate.tag() == tag)
6805                .unwrap()
6806                .cpu_timeout = if tag == "gate.manifest_on_host" {
6807                crate::validation_dag_static::MANIFEST_GATE_CPU_SECONDS
6808            } else {
6809                600
6810            };
6811            assert!(
6812                assert_manifest_gate_width_contract(&recapped)
6813                    .unwrap_err()
6814                    .contains(tag)
6815            );
6816        }
6817    }

6819    #[test]
6820    fn result_classification_and_failure_families_retain_their_pre_cutover_policy() {
6821        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6822        let cells = expected_cells(&crate::git_environment::checkout_root()).unwrap();
6823        assert_invariants(&committed, &cells).unwrap();
6824
6825        let mut changed_family = committed.clone();
6826        changed_family
6827            .steps
6828            .iter_mut()
6829            .find(|step| {
6830                step.tag() == crate::validation_dag_static::PMU_MEMORY_FAILURE_FAMILY_MEMBERS[0]
6831            })
6832            .unwrap()
6833            .fail_fast_family = Some("independent family".into());
6834        let error = assert_invariants(&changed_family, &cells).unwrap_err();
6835        assert!(
6836            error.contains("shared pre-cutover PMU failure family"),
6837            "{error}"
6838        );
6839
6840        let mut bypassed = committed;
6841        bypassed
6842            .steps
6843            .iter_mut()
6844            .find(|step| step.tag() == "check.check_outcome_consumers")
6845            .unwrap()
6846            .cmd = OUTCOME_CONSUMERS_COMMAND.replace(
6847            "./ci/check-outcome-consumers-node.sh",
6848            "./scripts/test-check-status-outcome.sh && ./scripts/check-merge-gate-policy.sh",
6849        );
6850        let error = assert_invariants(&bypassed, &cells).unwrap_err();
6851        assert!(
6852            error.contains("no-result classification wrapper"),
6853            "{error}"
6854        );

6856        fn accept_step(cfg: &mut DagConfig) -> &mut Step {
6857            cfg.steps
6858                .iter_mut()
6859                .find(|step| step.tag() == CANONICAL_ADAPTER_ACCEPT_TAG)
6860                .unwrap()
6861        }
6862        let base = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6863        let mut hosted = base.clone();
6864        accept_step(&mut hosted)
6865            .labels
6866            .push(HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL.into());
6867        let error = assert_invariants(&hosted, &cells).unwrap_err();
6868        assert!(error.contains("labelled exactly"), "{error}");
6869
6870        let mut unscheduled = base.clone();
6871        accept_step(&mut unscheduled).labels.clear();
6872        let error = assert_invariants(&unscheduled, &cells).unwrap_err();
6873        assert!(error.contains("labelled exactly"), "{error}");
6874
6875        let mut through_make = base.clone();
6876        accept_step(&mut through_make).cmd = CANONICAL_ADAPTER_ACCEPT_COMMAND.replace(
6877            "python3 ./scripts/test_validate_stop_paths.py --canonical-adapter-accept-arm-only",
6878            "make lint-parent-checks # --canonical-adapter-accept-arm-only",
6879        );
6880        let error = assert_invariants(&through_make, &cells).unwrap_err();
6881        assert!(error.contains("not through make"), "{error}");
6882
6883        let mut doubled = base;
6884        doubled
6885            .steps
6886            .iter_mut()
6887            .find(|step| step.tag() == "check.lint_checks")
6888            .unwrap()
6889            .cmd
6890            .push_str(" && python3 ./scripts/test_validate_stop_paths.py --canonical-adapter-accept-arm-only");
6891        let error = assert_invariants(&doubled, &cells).unwrap_err();
6892        assert!(
6893            error.contains("exactly check.canonical_adapter_accept"),
6894            "{error}"
6895        );

6899    fn structured_result_registry_is_exact_and_bijective() {
6900        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
6901        assert_structured_result_producers(&committed).unwrap();
6902
6903        for kind in StructuredResultProducerKind::ALL {
6904            let tag = kind.tags()[0];
6905            let mut missing = committed.clone();
6906            missing
6907                .steps
6908                .iter_mut()
6909                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
6910                .unwrap()
6911                .result_manifests
6912                .as_mut()
6913                .unwrap()
6914                .retain(|manifest| !matches!(manifest, ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(_)));
6915            let error = assert_structured_result_producers(&missing).unwrap_err();
6916            assert!(error.contains(tag) && error.contains("omits"), "{error}");
6917
6918            let mut changed_writer = committed.clone();
6919            let step = changed_writer
6920                .steps
6921                .iter_mut()
6922                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
6923                .unwrap();
6924            step.cmd = step
6925                .cmd
6926                .replace(kind.command_marker(), "removed-structured-result-writer");
6927            let error = assert_structured_result_producers(&changed_writer).unwrap_err();
6928            assert!(
6929                error.contains(tag) && error.contains("no longer invokes"),
6930                "{error}"
6931            );
6932        }
6933
6934        let template = committed
6935            .steps
6936            .iter()
6937            .find(|step| step.tag() == "test.regular_crates")
6938            .unwrap()
6939            .result_manifests
6940            .as_ref()
6941            .unwrap()
6942            .iter()
6943            .find(|manifest| matches!(manifest, ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(_)))
6944            .unwrap()
6945            .clone();
6946
6947        let mut extra = committed.clone();
6948        let extra_step = extra
6949            .steps
6950            .iter_mut()
6951            .find(|step| step.tag() == "quick.run_smoke")
6952            .unwrap();
6953        let mut extra_manifest = template.clone();
6954        let ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(declaration) = &mut extra_manifest else {
6955            unreachable!()
6956        };
6957        declaration.owner = extra_step.tag();
6958        extra_step
6959            .result_manifests
6960            .as_mut()
6961            .unwrap()
6962            .push(extra_manifest);
6963        let error = assert_structured_result_producers(&extra).unwrap_err();
6964        assert!(
6965            error.contains("quick.run_smoke") && error.contains("declares"),
6966            "{error}"
6967        );
6968
6969        let mut duplicate = committed.clone();
6970        duplicate
6971            .steps
6972            .iter_mut()
6973            .find(|step| step.tag() == "test.regular_crates")
6974            .unwrap()
6975            .result_manifests
6976            .as_mut()
6977            .unwrap()
6978            .push(template.clone());
6979        let error = assert_structured_result_producers(&duplicate).unwrap_err();
6980        assert!(error.contains("more than once"), "{error}");
6981
6982        let mut wrong_owner = committed.clone();
6983        let ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(declaration) = wrong_owner
6984            .steps
6985            .iter_mut()
6986            .find(|step| step.tag() == "test.regular_crates")
6987            .unwrap()
6988            .result_manifests
6989            .as_mut()
6990            .unwrap()
6991            .iter_mut()
6992            .find(|manifest| matches!(manifest, ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(_)))
6993            .unwrap()
6994        else {
6995            unreachable!()
6996        };
6997        declaration.owner = "other.step".into();
6998        let error = assert_structured_result_producers(&wrong_owner).unwrap_err();
6999        assert!(error.contains("owner 'other.step'"), "{error}");
7000    }

7014                .as_array_mut()
7015                .unwrap()
7016                .iter_mut()
7017                .find(|manifest| manifest["kind"] == "structured-test-results")

7030    #[test]
7031    fn structured_result_counts_and_ownership_survive_generation_transforms() {
7032        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
7033        for (tag, mutation) in [
7034            ("test.regular_crates", None),
7035            ("test.cli", Some("999")),
7036            ("quick.run_smoke", Some("1")),
7037        ] {
7038            let mut changed = committed.clone();
7039            let step = changed
7040                .steps
7041                .iter_mut()
7042                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
7043                .unwrap();
7044            match mutation {
7045                Some(value) => {
7046                    step.env
7047                        .insert("NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED".into(), value.into());
7048                }
7049                None => {
7050                    step.env.remove("NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED");
7051                }
7052            }
7053            let error = assert_structured_result_producers(&changed).unwrap_err();
7054            assert!(error.contains(tag), "{error}");
7055        }
7056
7057        let mut inline_count = committed.clone();
7058        inline_count
7059            .steps
7060            .iter_mut()
7061            .find(|step| step.tag() == "test.cli")
7062            .unwrap()
7063            .cmd
7064            .insert_str(0, "NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED=71 ");
7065        let error = assert_structured_result_producers(&inline_count).unwrap_err();
7066        assert!(
7067            error.contains("test.cli") && error.contains("command text"),
7068            "{error}"
7069        );
7070
7071        let mut duplicate_writer = committed.clone();
7072        duplicate_writer
7073            .steps
7074            .iter_mut()
7075            .find(|step| step.tag() == "test.regular_crates")
7076            .unwrap()
7077            .cmd
7078            .push_str("; ./ci/run-nextest-counted.sh -p duplicate");
7079        let error = assert_structured_result_producers(&duplicate_writer).unwrap_err();
7080        assert!(
7081            error.contains("test.regular_crates") && error.contains("2 times"),
7082            "{error}"
7083        );
7084        let mut normalized = committed
7085            .steps
7086            .iter()
7087            .find(|step| step.tag() == "test.regular_crates")
7088            .unwrap()
7089            .clone();
7090        let before_normalize = normalized
7091            .result_manifests
7092            .as_deref()
7093            .unwrap_or_default()
7094            .iter()
7095            .filter(|manifest| matches!(manifest, ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(_)))
7096            .cloned()
7097            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
7098        normalize_step(&mut normalized, Path::new("/repo"), Path::new("/run-state")).unwrap();
7099        let after_normalize = normalized
7100            .result_manifests
7101            .as_deref()
7102            .unwrap_or_default()
7103            .to_vec();
7104        assert_eq!(before_normalize, after_normalize);
7105        assert!(normalized.effective_result_manifests().is_empty());
7106
7107        let cells = expected_cells(&crate::git_environment::checkout_root()).unwrap();
7108        let mut reattached = committed.clone();
7109        let before = reattached
7110            .steps
7111            .iter()
7112            .map(|step| (step.tag(), step.result_manifests.clone()))
7113            .collect::<BTreeMap<_, _>>();
7114        attach_result_ownership(&mut reattached, &cells);
7115        for step in &reattached.steps {
7116            let before_structured = before[&step.tag()]
7117                .as_deref()
7118                .unwrap_or_default()
7119                .iter()
7120                .filter(|manifest| matches!(manifest, ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(_)))
7121                .collect::<Vec<_>>();
7122            let after_structured = step
7123                .result_manifests
7124                .as_deref()
7125                .unwrap_or_default()
7126                .iter()
7127                .filter(|manifest| matches!(manifest, ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(_)))
7128                .collect::<Vec<_>>();
7129            assert_eq!(before_structured, after_structured, "{}", step.tag());
7130        }
7131        assert_structured_result_producers(&reattached).unwrap();
7132    }

7134    #[test]
7135    fn pinned_workspace_compile_does_not_wait_for_the_rust_script_tools() {
7136        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
7137        let find = |cfg: &DagConfig, tag: &str| {
7138            cfg.steps
7139                .iter()
7140                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
7141                .unwrap()
7142                .clone()
7143        };
7144        let full = select_steps_by_labels(&committed, &["full".to_string()]).unwrap();
7145        let compile = find(&full, PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG);
7146        let prepare = find(&full, PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG);
7147        assert_eq!(compile.job, PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_JOB);
7148        // The chain-B edge is gone from the Cargo half, directly and
7149        // transitively, and the preparation half carries it and the compile
7150        // edge.
7151        assert!(
7152            !compile
7153                .deps
7154                .iter()
7155                .any(|dep| dep == PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG)
7156        );
7157        assert_eq!(compile.deps, ["pre.reverie_pin", "setup.pinned_root_fetch"]);
7158        for required in [PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG, PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG] {
7159            assert!(prepare.deps.iter().any(|dep| dep == required), "{required}");
7160        }
7161        assert_pinned_workspace_split(&committed).unwrap();
7162
7163        // The two payloads concatenate to exactly the unsplit producer's
7164        // payload, under one wrapper argv (env names, --out, --cargo-home and
7165        // the pinned-root guard).
7166        let canonical = crate::validation_dag_static::config();
7167        let producer = find(&canonical, "build.workspace");
7168        let joined = pinned_workspace_producer_payload(&committed).unwrap();
7169        assert_eq!(joined, producer.cmd);
7170        let compile_payload = crate::nextest_build_selections::execution_command(&compile).unwrap();
7171        let prepare_payload = crate::nextest_build_selections::execution_command(&prepare).unwrap();
7172        assert!(prepare_payload.ends_with("./ci/nextest-binaries.rs prepare full"));
7173        assert!(!compile_payload.contains("nextest-binaries.rs"));
7174        assert_eq!(
7175            split_workspace_payload(&producer.cmd).unwrap(),
7176            (compile_payload.clone(), prepare_payload.clone())
7177        );
7178        assert_eq!(
7179            join_workspace_payloads(&compile_payload, &prepare_payload).unwrap(),
7180            producer.cmd
7181        );
7182        let wrapper = |step: &Step| {
7183            let mut argv = shell_words::split(&step.cmd).unwrap();
7184            argv.pop();
7185            argv
7186        };
7187        assert_eq!(wrapper(&compile), wrapper(&prepare));
7188        for needle in [
7189            "--out",
7190            "ignored/hermetic/split",
7191            "--cargo-home",
7192            "ignored/hermetic/split/cargo",
7193            PINNED_ROOT_COMMAND_GUARD,
7194        ] {
7195            assert!(
7196                wrapper(&compile).iter().any(|arg| arg == needle),
7197                "{needle}"
7198            );
7199        }
7200
7201        // The cut refuses a payload without the boundary and one with two.
7202        let missing = producer.cmd.replacen(
7203            WORKSPACE_PREPARATION_BOUNDARY,
7204            " ; ./ci/nextest-binaries.rs prepare ",
7205            1,
7206        );
7207        assert!(
7208            split_workspace_payload(&missing)
7209                .unwrap_err()
7210                .contains("exact Nextest preparation boundary")
7211        );
7212        let duplicated = format!("{} && ./ci/nextest-binaries.rs prepare full", producer.cmd);
7213        assert!(
7214            split_workspace_payload(&duplicated)
7215                .unwrap_err()
7216                .contains("exact Nextest preparation boundary")
7217        );
7218        // The twin splitter, which receives the unwrapped payload, refuses too.
7219        let mut twin = find(&committed, PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG);
7220        twin.cmd = duplicated;
7221        assert!(split_pinned_workspace_twin(twin.clone()).is_err());
7222        twin.cmd = missing;
7223        assert!(split_pinned_workspace_twin(twin).is_err());
7224
7225        // The graph assertion refuses each way of undoing the split's contract.
7226        let mutated = |tag: &str, edit: &dyn Fn(&mut Step)| {
7227            let mut changed = committed.clone();
7228            edit(
7229                changed
7230                    .steps
7231                    .iter_mut()
7232                    .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
7233                    .unwrap(),
7234            );
7235            assert_pinned_workspace_split(&changed).unwrap_err()
7236        };
7237        let waits = mutated(PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG, &|step| {
7238            step.deps.push(PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG.into())
7239        });
7240        assert!(waits.contains("waits for"), "{waits}");
7241        // Through an intermediate pinned-root producer that itself waits for
7242        // the rust-script tools.
7243        let waits_transitively = mutated(PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG, &|step| {
7244            step.deps.push("setup.manifest_plan_in_pinned_root".into())
7245        });
7246        assert!(
7247            waits_transitively.contains("waits for"),
7248            "{waits_transitively}"
7249        );
7250        let unordered = mutated(PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG, &|step| {
7251            step.deps.retain(|dep| dep != PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG)
7252        });
7253        assert!(unordered.contains("must depend directly"), "{unordered}");
7254        let unscripted = mutated(PINNED_WORKSPACE_PREPARE_TAG, &|step| {
7255            step.deps.retain(|dep| dep != PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG)
7256        });
7257        assert!(unscripted.contains("must depend directly"), "{unscripted}");
7258        let bypass = mutated("build.e2e_artifact_in_pinned_root", &|step| {
7259            step.deps.push(PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG.into())
7260        });
7261        assert!(bypass.contains("no consumer runs before"), "{bypass}");
7262        let rerooted = mutated(PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG, &|step| {
7263            step.cmd = step.cmd.replacen(
7264                "ignored/hermetic/split/cargo",
7265                "ignored/hermetic/other/cargo",
7266                1,
7267            )
7268        });
7269        assert!(
7270            rerooted.contains("different pinned-root wrappers"),
7271            "{rerooted}"
7272        );
7273        let relabeled = mutated(PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG, &|step| {
7274            step.labels.pop();
7275        });
7276        assert!(relabeled.contains("labels"), "{relabeled}");
7277        // The Cargo half keeps build.workspace's authored wall and the hosted
7278        // twin keeps the hosted wall; neither may take the other's, and the
7279        // hosted twin's CPU bound stays the Cargo half's.
7280        let compile_wall = mutated(PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG, &|step| {
7281            step.timeout = crate::validation_dag_static::HOSTED_WORKSPACE_WALL_SECONDS
7282        });
7283        assert!(
7284            compile_wall.contains("lost the workspace build's budget"),
7285            "{compile_wall}"
7286        );
7287        let hosted_wall = mutated("build.workspace_on_host", &|step| {
7288            step.timeout = crate::validation_dag_static::WORKSPACE_WALL_SECONDS
7289        });
7290        assert!(
7291            hosted_wall.contains("not the hosted 2100s"),
7292            "{hosted_wall}"
7293        );
7294        let hosted_cpu = mutated("build.workspace_on_host", &|step| step.cpu_timeout += 1);
7295        assert!(
7296            hosted_cpu.contains("lost the workspace build's budget"),
7297            "{hosted_cpu}"
7298        );
7299        // The assertion is wired into the generator's invariant set.
7300        let mut changed = committed.clone();
7301        changed
7302            .steps
7303            .iter_mut()
7304            .find(|step| step.tag() == PINNED_WORKSPACE_COMPILE_TAG)
7305            .unwrap()
7306            .deps
7307            .push(PINNED_RUST_SCRIPTS_TAG.into());
7308        let cells = expected_cells(&crate::git_environment::checkout_root()).unwrap();
7309        let error = assert_invariants(&changed, &cells).unwrap_err();
7310        assert!(error.contains("waits for"), "{error}");
7311    }

7313    #[test]
7314    fn hosted_script_unit_tests_twin_changes_only_its_identity_and_wall() {
7315        let committed = dag_from_json(include_str!("../../dag/validate.json")).unwrap();
7316        assert_hosted_script_unit_tests(&committed).unwrap();
7317        let find = |cfg: &DagConfig, tag: &str| {
7318            cfg.steps
7319                .iter()
7320                .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
7321                .unwrap()
7322                .clone()
7323        };
7324        let local = find(&committed, SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG);
7325        let hosted = find(&committed, HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG);
7326        assert_eq!(local.timeout, 900);
7327        assert_eq!(hosted.timeout, 1500);
7328        assert_eq!(hosted.cpu_timeout, local.cpu_timeout);
7329        assert_eq!(hosted.cmd, local.cmd);
7330        assert_eq!(hosted.deps, local.deps);
7331
7332        // Each lane selects exactly one of the two nodes.
7333        let tags = |labels: &[&str]| {
7334            select_steps_by_labels(
7335                &committed,
7336                &labels
7337                    .iter()
7338                    .map(|label| label.to_string())
7339                    .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
7340            )
7341            .unwrap()
7342            .steps
7343            .iter()
7344            .map(Step::tag)
7345            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
7346        };
7347        for (labels, selected, absent) in [
7348            (
7349                &[HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL][..],
7350                HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG,
7351                SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG,
7352            ),
7353            (
7354                &["full"][..],
7355                SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG,
7356                HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG,
7357            ),
7358            (
7359                &["portable"][..],
7360                SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG,
7361                HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG,
7362            ),
7363        ] {
7364            let chosen = tags(labels);
7365            assert!(chosen.contains(selected), "{labels:?}");
7366            assert!(!chosen.contains(absent), "{labels:?}");
7367        }
7368
7369        // The pass rebuilds the committed twin from the authored node.
7370        let mut authored = committed.clone();
7371        authored
7372            .steps
7373            .retain(|step| step.tag() != HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG);
7374        let original = authored
7375            .steps
7376            .iter_mut()
7377            .find(|step| step.tag() == SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG)
7378            .unwrap();
7379        original.labels.push(HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL.into());
7380        original.labels.sort();
7381        let mut rebuilt = authored.clone();
7382        materialize_hosted_script_unit_tests(&mut rebuilt).unwrap();
7383        let one = |step: &Step| {
7384            dag_to_json(&DagConfig {
7385                steps: vec![step.clone()],
7386                ..Default::default()
7387            })
7388        };
7389        assert_eq!(
7390            one(&find(&rebuilt, HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG)),
7391            one(&hosted)
7392        );
7393        assert_eq!(one(&find(&rebuilt, SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG)), one(&local));
7394        // It refuses a node that no longer runs in the hosted lane or no
7395        // longer carries the authored wall.
7396        let error = materialize_hosted_script_unit_tests(&mut committed.clone()).unwrap_err();
7397        assert!(error.contains("lost its hosted-portable label"), "{error}");
7398        let mut rewalled = authored.clone();
7399        rewalled
7400            .steps
7401            .iter_mut()
7402            .find(|step| step.tag() == SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG)
7403            .unwrap()
7404            .timeout = 1500;
7405        let error = materialize_hosted_script_unit_tests(&mut rewalled).unwrap_err();
7406        assert!(error.contains("not its authored 900s"), "{error}");
7407
7408        // The assertion refuses each way of undoing the twin's contract.
7409        let mutated = |tag: &str, edit: &dyn Fn(&mut Step)| {
7410            let mut changed = committed.clone();
7411            edit(
7412                changed
7413                    .steps
7414                    .iter_mut()
7415                    .find(|step| step.tag() == tag)
7416                    .unwrap(),
7417            );
7418            assert_hosted_script_unit_tests(&changed).unwrap_err()
7419        };
7420        let hosted_wall = mutated(HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG, &|step| step.timeout = 900);
7421        assert!(
7422            hosted_wall.contains("not the hosted 1500s"),
7423            "{hosted_wall}"
7424        );
7425        let local_wall = mutated(SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG, &|step| step.timeout = 1500);
7426        assert!(local_wall.contains("not its authored 900s"), "{local_wall}");
7427        for (what, edit) in [
7428            (
7429                "command",
7430                &(|step: &mut Step| step.cmd.push_str(" --skip-fixture")) as &dyn Fn(&mut Step),
7431            ),
7432            ("CPU bound", &|step: &mut Step| step.cpu_timeout += 1),
7433            ("dependencies", &|step: &mut Step| {
7434                step.deps.pop();
7435            }),
7436            ("memory hint", &|step: &mut Step| {
7437                step.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes = step.hint.hard_mem_max_bytes.map(|bytes| bytes + 1)
7438            }),
7439            ("environment", &|step: &mut Step| {
7440                step.env
7441                    .insert("HERMIT_SCRIPT_TEST_JOBS".into(), "1".into());
7442            }),
7443            ("authored description", &|step: &mut Step| {
7444                step.description.insert(0, 'x')
7445            }),
7446        ] {
7447            let error = mutated(HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG, edit);
7448            assert!(
7449                error.contains("in more than its hosted identity"),
7450                "{what}: {error}"
7451            );
7452        }
7453        let relabeled_local = mutated(SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG, &|step| {
7454            step.labels.push(HOSTED_PORTABLE_LABEL.into())
7455        });
7456        assert!(
7457            relabeled_local.contains("keeps the hosted-portable label"),
7458            "{relabeled_local}"
7459        );
7460        let relabeled_hosted = mutated(HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG, &|step| {
7461            step.labels.push("full".into())
7462        });
7463        assert!(
7464            relabeled_hosted.contains("has labels"),
7465            "{relabeled_hosted}"
7466        );
7467        let family = mutated(HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG, &|step| {
7468            step.fail_fast_family = Some(SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG.into())
7469        });
7470        assert!(family.contains("fail-fast family"), "{family}");
7471        let undescribed = mutated(HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG, &|step| {
7472            step.description = step
7473                .description
7474                .replace(" HOSTED WALL BOUND 2026-10-06:", " HOSTED WALL 2026-10-06:")
7475        });
7476        assert!(
7477            undescribed.contains("lost its hosted wall description"),
7478            "{undescribed}"
7479        );
7480        let mut duplicated = committed.clone();
7481        duplicated.steps.push(hosted.clone());
7482        let error = assert_hosted_script_unit_tests(&duplicated).unwrap_err();
7483        assert!(error.contains("appears more than once"), "{error}");
7484        let mut missing = committed.clone();
7485        missing
7486            .steps
7487            .retain(|step| step.tag() != HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG);
7488        let error = assert_hosted_script_unit_tests(&missing).unwrap_err();
7489        assert!(error.contains("is absent"), "{error}");
7490
7491        // The assertion is wired into the generator's invariant set.
7492        let mut changed = committed.clone();
7493        changed
7494            .steps
7495            .iter_mut()
7496            .find(|step| step.tag() == HOSTED_SCRIPT_UNIT_TESTS_TAG)
7497            .unwrap()
7498            .timeout = 900;
7499        let cells = expected_cells(&crate::git_environment::checkout_root()).unwrap();
7500        let error = assert_invariants(&changed, &cells).unwrap_err();
7501        assert!(error.contains("not the hosted 1500s"), "{error}");
7502    }
7503}
